{"parentUuid":"19425339-69c0-43ab-ae0a-b7e0f3f07bca","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"7f0c215b-3d46-41aa-829c-b3f1d75c1682","agentId":"a11938c512cfadfa8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DbJPkQanJxDx8sb2Jhsqwu","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  Public API for temporary logging captures on off-by-default long-polling\n4\t  endpoints (enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls, AC4/AC5/AC6/AC12).\n5\t\n6\t  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls.md\n7\t  Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md §5\n8\t\n9\t  A pure-functional, inline-SQL Admin-domain module (ADR-018/020) — the\n10\t  `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager` precedent: no `Plug.Conn`/controller knowledge,\n11\t  directly callable from `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s event handlers.\n12\t\n13\t  ## Cluster-safe timer (ANSWERED design question 1)\n14\t\n15\t  The capture \"timer\" is a ROW in `admin_temporary_captures`\n16\t  (migration 000020): `endpoint` (PRIMARY KEY — at most one active capture\n17\t  per endpoint), `enabled_at`, `expires_at = enabled_at +\n18\t  capture_duration_s()`, `started_by`. **State is a row, expiry is a\n19\t  comparison against `now()`** — no `Process.send_after/3` owns correctness,\n20\t  so a node restart/crash/multi-node deployment can never leak a capture or\n21\t  lose its expiry. An expired row still sitting in the table (cleanup\n22\t  pending) is ALREADY inactive: `active?/1`/`active_captures/0` compare\n23\t  `expires_at > now()`, never mere row presence.\n24\t\n25\t  ## Duration (ANSWERED design question 3)\n26\t\n27\t  `capture_duration_s/0` reads\n28\t  `Application.get_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s, 300)` — the\n29\t  war_room_manager env-override pattern, so tests can use a 1-second capture\n30\t  instead of waiting 5 minutes.\n31\t\n32\t  ## Audit (AC12 Option B — ANSWERED design question 2)\n33\t\n34\t  Enabling collection on a previously-off endpoint is an admin-initiated\n35\t  change to logging policy (the retention-settings precedent): `enable/2`\n36\t  and `disable/2` write `enable_temporary_capture` /\n37\t  `disable_temporary_capture` rows via `Nebu.Compliance.AuditWriter`\n38\t  (`target_type: \"endpoint\"`, `target_id: <endpoint>`). Audit writes are\n39\t  best-effort by AuditWriter's own never-raise contract — a down audit path\n40\t  degrades to an error log, never a broken capture toggle (logging must not\n41\t  break on audit failure; AuditWriter itself records the failure).\n42\t\n43\t  ## Cache invalidation\n44\t\n45\t  Every write (`enable/2`, `disable/2`, `cleanup_expired/0`) nudges\n46\t  `Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache.refresh_now/0` afterward — mirroring\n47\t  `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager` -> `Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.load_active/0` —\n48\t  so the hot-path persistence gate (`Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?/2`)\n49\t  reflects the change on the very next log entry.\n50\t  \"\"\"\n51\t\n52\t  alias Nebu.Compliance.AuditWriter\n53\t\n54\t  @doc \"\"\"\n55\t  The capture duration in seconds — `:temporary_capture_duration_s` env\n56\t  override, defaulting to 300 (5 minutes, AC5).\n57\t  \"\"\"\n58\t  @spec capture_duration_s() :: pos_integer()\n59\t  def capture_duration_s do\n60\t    Application.get_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s, 300)\n61\t  end\n62\t\n63\t  @doc \"\"\"\n64\t  Pure expiry comparison: a capture is expired at (and after) its\n65\t  `:expires_at` instant. No process timer, no DB — callable from any\n66\t  render/tick path with any `now`.\n67\t  \"\"\"\n68\t  @spec expired?(%{expires_at: DateTime.t()}, DateTime.t()) :: boolean()\n69\t  def expired?(%{expires_at: %DateTime{} = expires_at}, %DateTime{} = now) do\n70\t    DateTime.compare(now, expires_at) != :lt\n71\t  end\n72\t\n73\t  @doc \"\"\"\n74\t  Pure countdown: whole seconds remaining until `:expires_at`, clamped at 0\n75\t  (never negative — the mm:ss display's contract, AC6).\n76\t  \"\"\"\n77\t  @spec remaining_seconds(%{expires_at: DateTime.t()}, DateTime.t()) :: non_neg_integer()\n78\t  def remaining_seconds(%{expires_at: %DateTime{} = expires_at}, %DateTime{} = now) do\n79\t    expires_at |> DateTime.diff(now, :second) |> max(0)\n80\t  end\n81\t\n82\t  @doc \"\"\"\n83\t  Whether `endpoint` currently has a NON-EXPIRED capture row. DB-guarded\n84\t  (the `initial_war_room_status/0` contract): with no `Nebu.Repo` running\n85\t  (plain unit-test env) there is no captures table and therefore no active\n86\t  capture — answers `false`, never raises.\n87\t  \"\"\"\n88\t  @spec active?(String.t()) :: boolean()\n89\t  def active?(endpoint) when is_binary(endpoint) do\n90\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n91\t      result =\n92\t        Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n93\t          Nebu.Repo,\n94\t          \"SELECT 1 FROM admin_temporary_captures WHERE endpoint = $1 AND expires_at > now()\",\n95\t          [endpoint]\n96\t        )\n97\t\n98\t      result.rows != []\n99\t    else\n100\t      false\n101\t    end\n102\t  end\n103\t\n104\t  def active?(_endpoint), do: false\n105\t\n106\t  @doc \"\"\"\n107\t  All non-expired captures, as maps with `:endpoint`/`:enabled_at`/\n108\t  `:expires_at`/`:started_by`. Same DB guard as `active?/1` (returns `[]`\n109\t  without a running Repo).\n110\t  \"\"\"\n111\t  @spec active_captures() :: [map()]\n112\t  def active_captures do\n113\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n114\t      result =\n115\t        Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n116\t          Nebu.Repo,\n117\t          \"SELECT endpoint, enabled_at, expires_at, started_by \" <>\n118\t            \"FROM admin_temporary_captures WHERE expires_at > now() ORDER BY endpoint\",\n119\t          []\n120\t        )\n121\t\n122\t      Enum.map(result.rows, &row_to_capture/1)\n123\t    else\n124\t      []\n125\t    end\n126\t  end\n127\t\n128\t  @doc \"\"\"\n129\t  Enables temporary capture on `endpoint` for `capture_duration_s()`\n130\t  seconds, started by admin `started_by`. Upserts the endpoint's single row\n131\t  (re-enabling an already-capturing endpoint refreshes its window — the\n132\t  PK-upsert makes \"at most one active capture per endpoint\" structural).\n133\t\n134\t  Returns `{:ok, capture}` with the row as a map. Writes the AC12\n135\t  `enable_temporary_capture` audit entry and refreshes the hot-path\n136\t  capture cache.\n137\t  \"\"\"\n138\t  @spec enable(String.t(), String.t()) :: {:ok, map()}\n139\t  def enable(endpoint, started_by) when is_binary(endpoint) and is_binary(started_by) do\n140\t    duration_s = capture_duration_s()\n141\t\n142\t    result =\n143\t      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n144\t        Nebu.Repo,\n145\t        \"\"\"\n146\t        INSERT INTO admin_temporary_captures (endpoint, enabled_at, expires_at, started_by)\n147\t        VALUES ($1, now(), now() + ($2::int * interval '1 second'), $3)\n148\t        ON CONFLICT (endpoint) DO UPDATE\n149\t          SET enabled_at = EXCLUDED.enabled_at,\n150\t              expires_at = EXCLUDED.expires_at,\n151\t              started_by = EXCLUDED.started_by\n152\t        RETURNING endpoint, enabled_at, expires_at, started_by\n153\t        \"\"\",\n154\t        [endpoint, duration_s, started_by]\n155\t      )\n156\t\n157\t    [row] = result.rows\n158\t    capture = row_to_capture(row)\n159\t\n160\t    AuditWriter.log(\n161\t      started_by,\n162\t      \"enable_temporary_capture\",\n163\t      \"endpoint\",\n164\t      endpoint,\n165\t      %{\n166\t        \"duration_s\" => duration_s,\n167\t        \"expires_at\" => DateTime.to_iso8601(capture.expires_at)\n168\t      },\n169\t      \"success\"\n170\t    )\n171\t\n172\t    notify_capture_cache()\n173\t    {:ok, capture}\n174\t  end\n175\t\n176\t  @doc \"\"\"\n177\t  Ends an ACTIVE capture on `endpoint` early (AC5's \"Disable now\").\n178\t\n179\t  `actor` is the admin performing the disable; when omitted (e.g. callers\n180\t  that only know the endpoint), the audit entry is attributed to the\n181\t  capture's own `started_by`.\n182\t\n183\t  Returns `:ok`, or `{:error, :not_found}` when no non-expired capture\n184\t  exists — a safe no-op error, never a crash (the war_room `stop_session/1`\n185\t  shape). An expired-but-unswept row counts as not found: it is already off.\n186\t  \"\"\"\n187\t  @spec disable(String.t(), String.t() | nil) :: :ok | {:error, :not_found}\n188\t  def disable(endpoint, actor \\\\ nil) when is_binary(endpoint) do\n189\t    result =\n190\t      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n191\t        Nebu.Repo,\n192\t        \"DELETE FROM admin_temporary_captures WHERE endpoint = $1 AND expires_at > now() \" <>\n193\t          \"RETURNING started_by\",\n194\t        [endpoint]\n195\t      )\n196\t\n197\t    case result.rows do\n198\t      [[started_by]] ->\n199\t        AuditWriter.log(\n200\t          actor || started_by,\n201\t          \"disable_temporary_capture\",\n202\t          \"endpoint\",\n203\t          endpoint,\n204\t          %{},\n205\t          \"success\"\n206\t        )\n207\t\n208\t        notify_capture_cache()\n209\t        :ok\n210\t\n211\t      [] ->\n212\t        {:error, :not_found}\n213\t    end\n214\t  end\n215\t\n216\t  @doc \"\"\"\n217\t  Deletes expired capture rows (hygiene — expiry itself is the comparison,\n218\t  story Dev Notes item 3). Called periodically by\n219\t  `Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache`. Leaves active rows untouched. No audit\n220\t  entries: expiry is the timer doing its job, not an admin action (the\n221\t  enable entry already records the window).\n222\t  \"\"\"\n223\t  @spec cleanup_expired() :: :ok\n224\t  def cleanup_expired do\n225\t    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n226\t      Nebu.Repo,\n227\t      \"DELETE FROM admin_temporary_captures WHERE expires_at <= now()\",\n228\t      []\n229\t    )\n230\t\n231\t    notify_capture_cache()\n232\t    :ok\n233\t  end\n234\t\n235\t  defp row_to_capture([endpoint, enabled_at, expires_at, started_by]) do\n236\t    %{\n237\t      endpoint: endpoint,\n238\t      enabled_at: enabled_at,\n239\t      expires_at: expires_at,\n240\t      started_by: started_by\n241\t    }\n242\t  end\n243\t\n244\t  # Best-effort, mirrors WarRoomManager.notify_war_room_reload/0: forces the\n245\t  # hot-path capture cache to reload so should_persist?/2 flips on the very\n246\t  # next log entry. Safe no-op when the cache process is not running.\n247\t  defp notify_capture_cache do\n248\t    Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache.refresh_now()\n249\t  end\n250\tend\n251\t"}]},"uuid":"641a66cc-a985-416d-aba8-b04f0e7937d6","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:54:11.319Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/temporary_capture_manager.ex","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  Public API for temporary logging captures on off-by-default long-polling\n  endpoints (enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls, AC4/AC5/AC6/AC12).\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls.md\n  Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md §5\n\n  A pure-functional, inline-SQL Admin-domain module (ADR-018/020) — the\n  `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager` precedent: no `Plug.Conn`/controller knowledge,\n  directly callable from `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s event handlers.\n\n  ## Cluster-safe timer (ANSWERED design question 1)\n\n  The capture \"timer\" is a ROW in `admin_temporary_captures`\n  (migration 000020): `endpoint` (PRIMARY KEY — at most one active capture\n  per endpoint), `enabled_at`, `expires_at = enabled_at +\n  capture_duration_s()`, `started_by`. **State is a row, expiry is a\n  comparison against `now()`** — no `Process.send_after/3` owns correctness,\n  so a node restart/crash/multi-node deployment can never leak a capture or\n  lose its expiry. An expired row still sitting in the table (cleanup\n  pending) is ALREADY inactive: `active?/1`/`active_captures/0` compare\n  `expires_at > now()`, never mere row presence.\n\n  ## Duration (ANSWERED design question 3)\n\n  `capture_duration_s/0` reads\n  `Application.get_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s, 300)` — the\n  war_room_manager env-override pattern, so tests can use a 1-second capture\n  instead of waiting 5 minutes.\n\n  ## Audit (AC12 Option B — ANSWERED design question 2)\n\n  Enabling collection on a previously-off endpoint is an admin-initiated\n  change to logging policy (the retention-settings precedent): `enable/2`\n  and `disable/2` write `enable_temporary_capture` /\n  `disable_temporary_capture` rows via `Nebu.Compliance.AuditWriter`\n  (`target_type: \"endpoint\"`, `target_id: <endpoint>`). Audit writes are\n  best-effort by AuditWriter's own never-raise contract — a down audit path\n  degrades to an error log, never a broken capture toggle (logging must not\n  break on audit failure; AuditWriter itself records the failure).\n\n  ## Cache invalidation\n\n  Every write (`enable/2`, `disable/2`, `cleanup_expired/0`) nudges\n  `Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache.refresh_now/0` afterward — mirroring\n  `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager` -> `Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.load_active/0` —\n  so the hot-path persistence gate (`Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?/2`)\n  reflects the change on the very next log entry.\n  \"\"\"\n\n  alias Nebu.Compliance.AuditWriter\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  The capture duration in seconds — `:temporary_capture_duration_s` env\n  override, defaulting to 300 (5 minutes, AC5).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec capture_duration_s() :: pos_integer()\n  def capture_duration_s do\n    Application.get_env(:nebu, :temporary_capture_duration_s, 300)\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Pure expiry comparison: a capture is expired at (and after) its\n  `:expires_at` instant. No process timer, no DB — callable from any\n  render/tick path with any `now`.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec expired?(%{expires_at: DateTime.t()}, DateTime.t()) :: boolean()\n  def expired?(%{expires_at: %DateTime{} = expires_at}, %DateTime{} = now) do\n    DateTime.compare(now, expires_at) != :lt\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Pure countdown: whole seconds remaining until `:expires_at`, clamped at 0\n  (never negative — the mm:ss display's contract, AC6).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec remaining_seconds(%{expires_at: DateTime.t()}, DateTime.t()) :: non_neg_integer()\n  def remaining_seconds(%{expires_at: %DateTime{} = expires_at}, %DateTime{} = now) do\n    expires_at |> DateTime.diff(now, :second) |> max(0)\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Whether `endpoint` currently has a NON-EXPIRED capture row. DB-guarded\n  (the `initial_war_room_status/0` contract): with no `Nebu.Repo` running\n  (plain unit-test env) there is no captures table and therefore no active\n  capture — answers `false`, never raises.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec active?(String.t()) :: boolean()\n  def active?(endpoint) when is_binary(endpoint) do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      result =\n        Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n          Nebu.Repo,\n          \"SELECT 1 FROM admin_temporary_captures WHERE endpoint = $1 AND expires_at > now()\",\n          [endpoint]\n        )\n\n      result.rows != []\n    else\n      false\n    end\n  end\n\n  def active?(_endpoint), do: false\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  All non-expired captures, as maps with `:endpoint`/`:enabled_at`/\n  `:expires_at`/`:started_by`. Same DB guard as `active?/1` (returns `[]`\n  without a running Repo).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec active_captures() :: [map()]\n  def active_captures do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      result =\n        Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n          Nebu.Repo,\n          \"SELECT endpoint, enabled_at, expires_at, started_by \" <>\n            \"FROM admin_temporary_captures WHERE expires_at > now() ORDER BY endpoint\",\n          []\n        )\n\n      Enum.map(result.rows, &row_to_capture/1)\n    else\n      []\n    end\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Enables temporary capture on `endpoint` for `capture_duration_s()`\n  seconds, started by admin `started_by`. Upserts the endpoint's single row\n  (re-enabling an already-capturing endpoint refreshes its window — the\n  PK-upsert makes \"at most one active capture per endpoint\" structural).\n\n  Returns `{:ok, capture}` with the row as a map. Writes the AC12\n  `enable_temporary_capture` audit entry and refreshes the hot-path\n  capture cache.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec enable(String.t(), String.t()) :: {:ok, map()}\n  def enable(endpoint, started_by) when is_binary(endpoint) and is_binary(started_by) do\n    duration_s = capture_duration_s()\n\n    result =\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"\"\"\n        INSERT INTO admin_temporary_captures (endpoint, enabled_at, expires_at, started_by)\n        VALUES ($1, now(), now() + ($2::int * interval '1 second'), $3)\n        ON CONFLICT (endpoint) DO UPDATE\n          SET enabled_at = EXCLUDED.enabled_at,\n              expires_at = EXCLUDED.expires_at,\n              started_by = EXCLUDED.started_by\n        RETURNING endpoint, enabled_at, expires_at, started_by\n        \"\"\",\n        [endpoint, duration_s, started_by]\n      )\n\n    [row] = result.rows\n    capture = row_to_capture(row)\n\n    AuditWriter.log(\n      started_by,\n      \"enable_temporary_capture\",\n      \"endpoint\",\n      endpoint,\n      %{\n        \"duration_s\" => duration_s,\n        \"expires_at\" => DateTime.to_iso8601(capture.expires_at)\n      },\n      \"success\"\n    )\n\n    notify_capture_cache()\n    {:ok, capture}\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Ends an ACTIVE capture on `endpoint` early (AC5's \"Disable now\").\n\n  `actor` is the admin performing the disable; when omitted (e.g. callers\n  that only know the endpoint), the audit entry is attributed to the\n  capture's own `started_by`.\n\n  Returns `:ok`, or `{:error, :not_found}` when no non-expired capture\n  exists — a safe no-op error, never a crash (the war_room `stop_session/1`\n  shape). An expired-but-unswept row counts as not found: it is already off.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec disable(String.t(), String.t() | nil) :: :ok | {:error, :not_found}\n  def disable(endpoint, actor \\\\ nil) when is_binary(endpoint) do\n    result =\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"DELETE FROM admin_temporary_captures WHERE endpoint = $1 AND expires_at > now() \" <>\n          \"RETURNING started_by\",\n        [endpoint]\n      )\n\n    case result.rows do\n      [[started_by]] ->\n        AuditWriter.log(\n          actor || started_by,\n          \"disable_temporary_capture\",\n          \"endpoint\",\n          endpoint,\n          %{},\n          \"success\"\n        )\n\n        notify_capture_cache()\n        :ok\n\n      [] ->\n        {:error, :not_found}\n    end\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Deletes expired capture rows (hygiene — expiry itself is the comparison,\n  story Dev Notes item 3). Called periodically by\n  `Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache`. Leaves active rows untouched. No audit\n  entries: expiry is the timer doing its job, not an admin action (the\n  enable entry already records the window).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec cleanup_expired() :: :ok\n  def cleanup_expired do\n    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n      Nebu.Repo,\n      \"DELETE FROM admin_temporary_captures WHERE expires_at <= now()\",\n      []\n    )\n\n    notify_capture_cache()\n    :ok\n  end\n\n  defp row_to_capture([endpoint, enabled_at, expires_at, started_by]) do\n    %{\n      endpoint: endpoint,\n      enabled_at: enabled_at,\n      expires_at: expires_at,\n      started_by: started_by\n    }\n  end\n\n  # Best-effort, mirrors WarRoomManager.notify_war_room_reload/0: forces the\n  # hot-path capture cache to reload so should_persist?/2 flips on the very\n  # next log entry. Safe no-op when the cache process is not running.\n  defp notify_capture_cache do\n    Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache.refresh_now()\n  end\nend\n","numLines":251,"startLine":1,"totalLines":251}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"19425339-69c0-43ab-ae0a-b7e0f3f07bca","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

NEVER touches the DB itself.\n40\t  \"\"\"\n41\t  @spec active?(String.t()) :: boolean()\n42\t  def active?(endpoint) when is_binary(endpoint) do\n43\t    MapSet.member?(:persistent_term.get(@cache_key, MapSet.new()), endpoint)\n44\t  end\n45\t\n46\t  def active?(_endpoint), do: false\n47\t\n48\t  @doc \"\"\"\n49\t  Forces an immediate, synchronous refresh from the DB. Why this exists\n\n  `Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?/2` is called from\n  `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Handler.log/2`, which runs SYNCHRONOUSLY in the\n  arbitrary calling process for EVERY `Logger.*` call app-wide — including\n  from within `Nebu.Repo.QueryTelemetry`'s own logging, which fires from\n  inside an Ecto query's telemetry callback (see `PolicyCache`'s moduledoc\n  for the exact connection-pool deadlock this already caused once). A live\n  `Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!/3` against `admin_temporary_captures` on that\n  hot path would risk the same class of bug. This cache keeps the\n  persistence-decision path a pure, in-memory, non-blocking read.\n\n  `active?/1` is safe to call even when this process is not running (e.g.\n  plain unit test runs with no DB): `:persistent_term.get/2`'s default\n  clause returns an empty set (no active captures).\n  \"\"\"\n\n  use GenServer\n\n  require Logger\n\n  @cache_key :nebu_log_store_capture_cache\n  @refresh_interval_ms 2_000\n\n  def start_link(_opts \\\\ []), do: GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, :ok, name: __MODULE__)\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Whether `endpoint` currently has an active temporary capture, per the\n  cached snapshot (refreshed every #{@refresh_interval_ms}ms, and forced\n  synchronously on every `Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager` write via\n  `refresh_now/0`). NEVER touches the DB itself.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec active?(String.t()) :: boolean()\n  def active?(endpoint) when is_binary(endpoint) do\n    MapSet.member?(:persistent_term.get(@cache_key, MapSet.new()), endpoint)\n  end\n\n  def active?(_endpoint), do: false\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Forces an immediate, synchronous refresh from the DB. Called by\n  `Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager`'s `enable/2`/`disable/2`/\n  `cleanup_expired/0` so `should_persist?/2` reflects the change on the\n  VERY NEXT `Logger.*` call — no waiting for the periodic tick. Safe no-op\n  when this process is not currently running.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec refresh_now() :: :ok\n  def refresh_now do\n    if Process.whereis(__MODULE__) do\n      GenServer.call(__MODULE__, :refresh_now)\n    else\n      :ok\n    end\n  end\n\n  @impl true\n  def init(:ok) do\n    refresh()\n    schedule_refresh()\n    {:ok, %{}}\n  end\n\n  @impl true\n  def handle_call(:refresh_now, _from, state) do\n    refresh()\n    {:reply, :ok, state}\n  end\n\n  @impl true\n  def handle_info(:refresh, state) do\n    refresh()\n    schedule_refresh()\n    {:noreply, state}\n  end\n\n  def handle_info(_other, state), do: {:noreply, state}\n\n  defp schedule_refresh, do: Process.send_after(self(), :refresh, @refresh_interval_ms)\n\n  defp refresh do\n    endpoints =\n      Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager.active_captures()\n      |> MapSet.new(& &1.endpoint)\n\n    :persistent_term.put(@cache_key, endpoints)\n    :ok\n  rescue\n    e ->\n      Logger.warning(\"Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache failed to refresh: #{inspect(e)}\")\n      :ok\n  end\nend\n","numLines":101,"startLine":1,"totalLines":101}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"001d08ff-b3d8-400f-86f1-42a9666c526c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"ae1f9d8e-32a2-4d14-9f10-7b88578affc8","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"7f0c215b-3d46-41aa-829c-b3f1d75c1682","agentId":"a11938c512cfadfa8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015rM7nShYtWRVkXiVviEiaD","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t-- enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls (AC5/AC6, ANSWERED design\n2\t-- question 1): DB-backed temporary-capture timer for long-polling\n3\t-- endpoints. \"State is a row, expiry is a comparison against now()\" — no\n4\t-- process timer owns correctness (cluster-safe, survives node restarts;\n5\t-- the war_room_sessions precedent from 000019).\n6\t--\n7\t-- endpoint is the PRIMARY KEY: at most one active capture per endpoint\n8\t-- (story Dev Notes schema). Re-enabling upserts the same row with a fresh\n9\t-- expires_at. Expired rows are semantically OFF the moment now() passes\n10\t-- expires_at — the periodic cleanup (Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache /\n11\t-- Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager.cleanup_expired/0) is hygiene only,\n12\t-- never load-bearing for correctness.\n13\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS admin_temporary_captures (\n14\t    endpoint    TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n15\t    enabled_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n16\t    expires_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n17\t    started_by  TEXT NOT NULL\n18\t);\n19\t\n20\t-- AC1/AC2: the inspector's per-endpoint logging config writes DYNAMIC\n21\t-- server_config keys (log_min_level_<route> / log_retention_days_<route>,\n22\t-- one pair per observed endpoint) via upsert_server_config/1. The\n23\t-- config_update_mutable RLS allowlist (000002, last extended by 000018) is\n24\t-- key-enumerated — a brand-new key's FIRST upsert always succeeds as an\n25\t-- INSERT (config_insert_only), but any LATER upsert is an UPDATE that\n26\t-- silently affects 0 rows unless the key is allowed here (the exact\n27\t-- silent-data-loss class 000018 documents). Dynamic per-route keys cannot\n28\t-- be enumerated, so the two key FAMILIES are allowed via LIKE patterns\n29\t-- (backslash-escaped '_' so the SQL wildcard does not over-match).\n30\t--\n31\t-- Re-create the policy with the two patterns added. Idempotent (DROP IF\n32\t-- EXISTS). Key list = 000018's verbatim.\n33\tDROP POLICY IF EXISTS config_update_mutable ON server_config;\n34\tCREATE POLICY config_update_mutable ON server_config\n35\t    FOR UPDATE\n36\t    USING (\n37\t        key IN (\n38\t            'max_users', 'registration_enabled', 'default_room_visibility',\n39\t            'default_max_members', 'bootstrap_completed',\n40\t            'oidc_user_id_claim', 'oidc_displayname_claim', 'oidc_email_claim',\n41\t            'oidc_directory_enabled', 'oidc_directory_endpoint',\n42\t            'gdpr_retention_days', 'signing_key_id', 'compliance_mode_enabled',\n43\t            'scim_token_hash',\n44\t            'matrix_oidc_refresh_mode', 'admin_oidc_refresh_mode',\n45\t            'matrix_oidc_extra_scopes', 'admin_oidc_extra_scopes',\n46\t            'admin_group_claim_path',\n47\t            'log_collect_info', 'log_retention_info_days',\n48\t            'log_collect_debug', 'log_retention_debug_days',\n49\t            'log_retention_debug_max_count',\n50\t            'log_retention_info_max_count'\n51\t        )\n52\t        OR key LIKE 'log\\_min\\_level\\_%'\n53\t        OR key LIKE 'log\\_retention\\_days\\_%'\n54\t    );\n55\t"}]},"uuid":"e5814864-f697-4818-bf32-969bd9a02141","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:54:12.815Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/priv/migrations/000020_admin_temporary_captures.up.sql","content":"-- enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls (AC5/AC6, ANSWERED design\n-- question 1): DB-backed temporary-capture timer for long-polling\n-- endpoints. \"State is a row, expiry is a comparison against now()\" — no\n-- process timer owns correctness (cluster-safe, survives node restarts;\n-- the war_room_sessions precedent from 000019).\n--\n-- endpoint is the PRIMARY KEY: at most one active capture per endpoint\n-- (story Dev Notes schema). Re-enabling upserts the same row with a fresh\n-- expires_at. Expired rows are semantically OFF the moment now() passes\n-- expires_at — the periodic cleanup (Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache /\n-- Nebu.Admin.TemporaryCaptureManager.cleanup_expired/0) is hygiene only,\n-- never load-bearing for correctness.\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS admin_temporary_captures (\n    endpoint    TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    enabled_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n    expires_at  TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n    started_by  TEXT NOT NULL\n);\n\n-- AC1/AC2: the inspector's per-endpoint logging config writes DYNAMIC\n-- server_config keys (log_min_level_<route> / log_retention_days_<route>,\n-- one pair per observed endpoint) via upsert_server_config/1. The\n-- config_update_mutable RLS allowlist (000002, last extended by 000018) is\n-- key-enumerated — a brand-new key's FIRST upsert always succeeds as an\n-- INSERT (config_insert_only), but any LATER upsert is an UPDATE that\n-- silently affects 0 rows unless the key is allowed here (the exact\n-- silent-data-loss class 000018 documents). Dynamic per-route keys cannot\n-- be enumerated, so the two key FAMILIES are allowed via LIKE patterns\n-- (backslash-escaped '_' so the SQL wildcard does not over-match).\n--\n-- Re-create the policy with the two patterns added. Idempotent (DROP IF\n-- EXISTS). Key list = 000018's verbatim.\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS config_update_mutable ON server_config;\nCREATE POLICY config_update_mutable ON server_config\n    FOR UPDATE\n    USING (\n        key IN (\n            'max_users', 'registration_enabled', 'default_room_visibility',\n            'default_max_members', 'bootstrap_completed',\n            'oidc_user_id_claim', 'oidc_displayname_claim', 'oidc_email_claim',\n            'oidc_directory_enabled', 'oidc_directory_endpoint',\n            'gdpr_retention_days', 'signing_key_id', 'compliance_mode_enabled',\n            'scim_token_hash',\n            'matrix_oidc_refresh_mode', 'admin_oidc_refresh_mode',\n            'matrix_oidc_extra_scopes', 'admin_oidc_extra_scopes',\n            'admin_group_claim_path',\n            'log_collect_info', 'log_retention_info_days',\n            'log_collect_debug', 'log_retention_debug_days',\n            'log_retention_debug_max_count',\n            'log_retention_info_max_count'\n        )\n        OR key LIKE 'log\\_min\\_level\\_%'\n        OR key LIKE 'log\\_retention\\_days\\_%'\n    );\n","numLines":55,"startLine":1,"totalLines":55}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ae1f9d8e-32a2-4d14-9f10-7b88578affc8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

WITHOUT the dynamic per-endpoint LIKE families).\nDROP POLICY IF EXISTS config_update_mutable ON server_config;\nCREATE POLICY config_update_mutable ON server_config\n    FOR UPDATE\n    USING (key IN (\n        'max_users', 'registration_enabled', 'default_room_visibility',\n        'default_max_members', 'bootstrap_completed',\n        'oidc_user_id_claim', 'oidc_displayname_claim', 'oidc_email_claim',\n        'oidc_directory_enabled', 'oidc_directory_endpoint',\n        'gdpr_retention_days', 'signing_key_id', 'compliance_mode_enabled',\n        'scim_token_hash',\n        'matrix_oidc_refresh_mode', 'admin_oidc_refresh_mode',\n        'matrix_oidc_extra_scopes', 'admin_oidc_extra_scopes',\n        'admin_group_claim_path',\n        'log_collect_info', 'log_retention_info_days',\n        'log_collect_debug', 'log_retention_debug_days',\n        'log_retention_debug_max_count',\n        'log_retention_info_max_count'\n    ));\n","numLines":25,"startLine":1,"totalLines":25}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"bb79c24f-dfde-4293-8ae2-487028d30ac4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
