{"parentUuid":"27416336-c9b8-4d2e-ae85-d27cb75edd8f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a11938c512cfadfa8","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CcpJs8HK4Bkgs1kFYkUUX","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01BVtsTLt3j5jLt9of5jsZr8","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/web/log_store.ex"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8397,"cache_read_input_tokens":30658,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":8397,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CcpJs7GJ8dTiyYKMKjxqs","attributionAgent":"nebu-code-reviewer","type":"assistant","uuid":"a688e5ef-2387-4f1a-a8ac-c4919da24602","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:54:28.142Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
{"parentUuid":"a688e5ef-2387-4f1a-a8ac-c4919da24602","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"7f0c215b-3d46-41aa-829c-b3f1d75c1682","agentId":"a11938c512cfadfa8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BVtsTLt3j5jLt9of5jsZr8","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Web.LogStore do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  Public-facing module for the operational log store\n4\t  (enhanced-logging/operational-log-store).\n5\t\n6\t  Follows the `Nebu.Web.TokenDenylist`-style naming convention: the domain\n7\t  (`Nebu.Web`, shared infrastructure, exempt from ADR-020's Public-API rule —\n8\t  same as `Nebu.Web.Plugs.RequestLogger` itself) exposes exactly one module\n9\t  other code should call into.\n10\t\n11\t  ## Design (see the story's Background section for full rationale)\n12\t\n13\t  The operational log store is EXPLICITLY SEPARATE from\n14\t  `Nebu.Compliance.AuditLog` — mutable/pruneable diagnostics, not the\n15\t  tamper-evident/immutable compliance audit trail.\n16\t\n17\t  A single Erlang `:logger` handler (`Nebu.Web.LogStore.Handler`, attached via\n18\t  `attach_handler/0`) intercepts EVERY `Logger.*` call app-wide:\n19\t\n20\t    1. ALWAYS broadcasts the parsed entry via `Phoenix.PubSub` to topic\n21\t       `\"operational_logs\"` (independent of persistence — feeds a later\n22\t       Admin UI live view).\n23\t    2. CONDITIONALLY queues the entry for batched INSERT via\n24\t       `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Writer.write_async/1`, gated on `should_persist?/1`\n25\t       (WARN/ERROR always; INFO/DEBUG per the `server_config` policy exposed\n26\t       by `get_policy/0`).\n27\t  \"\"\"\n28\t\n29\t  alias Nebu.Web.LogStore.Handler\n30\t\n31\t  @handler_id :nebu_operational_log_handler\n32\t\n33\t  @policy_keys ~w(\n34\t    log_collect_info log_retention_info_days log_retention_info_max_count\n35\t    log_collect_debug log_retention_debug_days log_retention_debug_max_count\n36\t  )a\n37\t\n38\t  @doc \"\"\"\n39\t  Attaches the central `:logger` handler (AC2). Idempotent — tolerates being\n40\t  called when the handler is already attached (does NOT register a\n41\t  duplicate).\n42\t  \"\"\"\n43\t  @spec attach_handler() :: :ok\n44\t  def attach_handler do\n45\t    case :logger.add_handler(@handler_id, Handler, %{}) do\n46\t      :ok -> :ok\n47\t      {:error, {:already_exist, _id}} -> :ok\n48\t      {:error, reason} -> raise \"Nebu.Web.LogStore.attach_handler/0 failed: #{inspect(reason)}\"\n49\t    end\n50\t  end\n51\t\n52\t  @doc \"\"\"\n53\t  Returns `{:ok, policy}` — the current admin-configurable logging policy,\n54\t  read from `server_config` (AC3/AC9). Every one of the 5 policy keys is\n55\t  always present in the returned map (defaulted when unset in\n56\t  `server_config`, or when `Nebu.Repo` is not running at all — e.g. plain unit\n57\t  test runs with no live DB): INFO/DEBUG collection default to `false`\n58\t  (disabled); the 3 retention keys default to `nil` (no TTL/cap configured).\n59\t  \"\"\"\n60\t  @spec get_policy() :: {:ok, map()}\n61\t  def get_policy do\n62\t    {:ok, read_policy_from_db()}\n63\t  end\n64\t\n65\t  @doc false\n66\t  # Live DB read of the policy — the ONLY place that actually queries\n67\t  # server_config for these 5 keys. Called by `get_policy/0` (always a live\n68\t  # read, used by tests/AC9 and by `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Retention`, an hourly\n69\t  # background job where a live read is perfectly fine) AND by\n70\t  # `Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache` (its own periodic refresh tick — NEVER\n71\t  # called from `Handler.log/2`'s hot path, see `should_persist?/1`).\n72\t  @spec read_policy_from_db() :: map()\n73\t  def read_policy_from_db do\n74\t    raw = read_server_config()\n75\t\n76\t    %{\n77\t      log_collect_info: to_bool(Map.get(raw, \"log_collect_info\")),\n78\t      log_retention_info_days: to_int(Map.get(raw, \"log_retention_info_days\")),\n79\t      log_retention_info_max_count: to_int(Map.get(raw, \"log_retention_info_max_count\")),\n80\t      log_collect_debug: to_bool(Map.get(raw, \"log_collect_debug\")),\n81\t      log_retention_debug_days: to_int(Map.get(raw, \"log_retention_debug_days\")),\n82\t      log_retention_debug_max_count: to_int(Map.get(raw, \"log_retention_debug_max_count\"))\n83\t    }\n84\t  end\n85\t\n86\t  @doc \"\"\"\n87\t  Forces `Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache` to refresh immediately from the DB.\n88\t  Called by `Nebu.Admin.DB.upsert_server_config/1` whenever a change touches\n89\t  one of the 5 log-policy keys (mirrors the existing\n90\t  `Nebu.Web.OidcConfig.refresh/0` / `Nebu.Matrix.invalidate_oidc_claim_cache/0`\n91\t  cache-invalidation-on-write pattern already used there). Safe no-op when\n92\t  the cache process is not running.\n93\t  \"\"\"\n94\t  @spec refresh_policy_cache() :: :ok\n95\t  def refresh_policy_cache, do: Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache.refresh_now()\n96\t\n97\t  @doc \"\"\"\n98\t  Returns the 5 policy keys this module manages (mirrors the\n99\t  `config_update_mutable` RLS allowlist entries added by this story's\n100\t  migration).\n101\t  \"\"\"\n102\t  @spec policy_keys() :: [atom()]\n103\t  def policy_keys, do: @policy_keys\n104\t\n105\t  @doc \"\"\"\n106\t  Decides whether a log entry at `level` (a level STRING, e.g. `\"info\"`) must\n107\t  be persisted to `operational_logs` (AC7): WARN and ERROR (and any other\n108\t  level not explicitly gated — fail-safe toward persisting unknown/critical\n109\t  levels) always persist; INFO/DEBUG are gated by the `server_config` policy.\n110\t\n111\t  Reads the policy from `Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache` (a lock-free,\n112\t  `persistent_term`-backed cache, refreshed periodically) rather than\n113\t  `get_policy/0` directly — NEVER performs a live DB read on this path.\n114\t\n115\t  This matters beyond the general \"handler must not block\" design goal: this\n116\t  function is called from `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Handler.log/2`, which runs\n117\t  SYNCHRONOUSLY in whatever process is currently `Logger.*`-ing, including\n118\t  `Nebu.Repo.QueryTelemetry`'s own `Logger.debug/1` call — fired from\n119\t  WITHIN the telemetry callback of an Ecto query, in the SAME process that\n120\t  is using (or just used) a `Nebu.Repo` connection checkout. A live DB read\n121\t  here would re-enter `Nebu.Repo`'s connection pool from inside another\n122\t  query's completion callback — this was observed as a genuine\n123\t  connection-pool timeout in the integration suite before the cache was\n124\t  introduced.\n125\t  \"\"\"\n126\t  @spec should_persist?(String.t() | nil) :: boolean()\n127\t  def should_persist?(\"info\") do\n128\t    policy = Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache.get()\n129\t    policy.log_collect_info == true\n130\t  end\n131\t\n132\t  def should_persist?(\"debug\") do\n133\t    policy = Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache.get()\n134\t    policy.log_collect_debug == true\n135\t  end\n136\t\n137\t  def should_persist?(level) when is_binary(level), do: true\n138\t  def should_persist?(_level), do: false\n139\t\n140\t  @doc \"\"\"\n141\t  Route-aware persistence policy (enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls,\n142\t  AC4): the ingest-path check that additionally enforces the Trace Map's\n143\t  off-by-default long-polling endpoints\n144\t  (`Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.off_endpoint?/1` — the single canonical\n145\t  source of truth, AC13 reuse).\n146\t\n147\t  ERROR-level entries are ALWAYS persisted, even on an off-list route with\n148\t  no active capture (story amendment 2026-07-07, \"AC4 errors-always-persist\n149\t  exception\": \"off\" silences noise, never failures — a silently-red zone\n150\t  contradicts the leitstand mission). An off-list route with no active\n151\t  capture suppresses every other level (DEBUG/INFO/WARN). While a capture\n152\t  IS active (checked via the non-blocking `Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache`,\n153\t  never a live DB read on this path — same hot-path reasoning as\n154\t  `should_persist?/1`), the off-list route defers to the level-only policy\n155\t  above, exactly as if it were not off-list at all. Any other route (or a\n156\t  `nil` route — no `route_template` on the log entry) also defers to the\n157\t  level-only policy unchanged.\n158\t  \"\"\"\n159\t  @spec should_persist?(String.t() | nil, String.t() | nil) :: boolean()\n160\t  def should_persist?(level, route_template) do\n161\t    cond do\n162\t      level == \"error\" ->\n163\t        true\n164\t\n165\t      Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.off_endpoint?(route_template) ->\n166\t        Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache.active?(route_template) and should_persist?(level)\n167\t\n168\t      true ->\n169\t        should_persist?(level)\n170\t    end\n171\t  end\n172\t\n173\t  @doc \"\"\"\n174\t  Minimal read API (AC9) for tests and the (later) Admin UI. `filters` is an\n175\t  options map supporting `level`, `source`, `route_template`, `request_id` —\n176\t  any subset. An empty/no-filter map returns every row, most-recent first.\n177\t  \"\"\"\n178\t  @spec get_logs(map()) :: {:ok, [map()]}\n179\t  def get_logs(filters) when is_map(filters) do\n180\t    {where_sql, params} = build_where(filters)\n181\t\n182\t    sql = \"\"\"\n183\t    SELECT id, inserted_at, level, source, route_template, request_id, message, metadata, war_room_id\n184\t    FROM operational_logs\n185\t    #{where_sql}\n186\t    ORDER BY inserted_at DESC\n187\t    \"\"\"\n188\t\n189\t    result = Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(Nebu.Repo, sql, params)\n190\t    {:ok, Enum.map(result.rows, &row_to_log/1)}\n191\t  end\n192\t\n193\t  # ── policy helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n194\t\n195\t  defp read_server_config do\n196\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n197\t      {:ok, config} = Nebu.Admin.DB.get_server_config()\n198\t      config\n199\t    else\n200\t      %{}\n201\t    end\n202\t  end\n203\t\n204\t  defp to_bool(nil), do: false\n205\t  defp to_bool(true), do: true\n206\t  defp to_bool(false), do: false\n207\t  defp to_bool(\"true\"), do: true\n208\t  defp to_bool(\"false\"), do: false\n209\t  defp to_bool(_), do: false\n210\t\n211\t  defp to_int(nil), do: nil\n212\t  defp to_int(value) when is_integer(value), do: value\n213\t\n214\t  defp to_int(value) when is_binary(value) do\n215\t    case Integer.parse(value) do\n216\t      {int, _rest} -> int\n217\t      :error -> nil\n218\t    end\n219\t  end\n220\t\n221\t  defp to_int(_), do: nil\n222\t\n223\t  # ── get_logs/1 helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n224\t\n225\t  # enhanced-logging/war-room-mode (AC12): war_room_id is already SELECTed\n226\t  # and returned by row_to_log/1 below — it was just missing from this\n227\t  # allowlist, making the AC12 \"filter the stream by war_room_id\" use case\n228\t  # silently impossible (the filter key was accepted but ignored) even\n229\t  # though the column/SELECT already supported it.\n230\t  @allowed_filter_keys ~w(level source route_template request_id war_room_id)\n231\t\n232\t  defp build_where(filters) do\n233\t    filters\n234\t    |> Enum.map(fn {k, v} -> {to_string(k), v} end)\n235\t    |> Enum.filter(fn {k, _v} -> k in @allowed_filter_keys end)\n236\t    |> Enum.map(fn {k, v} -> {k, normalize_filter_value(k, v)} end)\n237\t    |> Enum.with_index(1)\n238\t    |> Enum.reduce({[], []}, fn {{k, v}, idx}, {conds, params} ->\n239\t      {conds ++ [\"#{k} = $#{idx}\"], params ++ [v]}\n240\t    end)\n241\t    |> then(fn {conds, params} ->\n242\t      where_sql = if conds == [], do: \"\", else: \"WHERE \" <> Enum.join(conds, \" AND \")\n243\t      {where_sql, params}\n244\t    end)\n245\t  end\n246\t\n247\t  # enhanced-logging/war-room-mode (AC12): `war_room_id` is a native\n248\t  # Postgres `uuid` column — Postgrex decodes/encodes `uuid` as a 16-byte\n249\t  # BINARY (see `Postgrex.Extensions.UUID`), unlike every other identifier\n250\t  # in this codebase (room_id/user_id/device_id/request_id), which are\n251\t  # plain TEXT and therefore always the human-readable string form.\n252\t  # `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.mount/3`'s `war_room_id` URL query param arrives\n253\t  # as a canonical hex-dash STRING (as any real bookmarked URL would carry\n254\t  # it); a value obtained directly from a prior `RETURNING id`/`SELECT id`\n255\t  # call elsewhere may already be the raw 16-byte binary form. Accept\n256\t  # EITHER here and normalize to whichever form Postgrex's own encoder\n257\t  # actually requires for a `uuid`-typed bound parameter (raw binary) —\n258\t  # `Ecto.UUID.cast/1` accepts both forms as input, `Ecto.UUID.dump!/1`\n259\t  # then converts the resulting canonical string to that raw binary.\n260\t  defp normalize_filter_value(\"war_room_id\", v) when is_binary(v) do\n261\t    case Ecto.UUID.cast(v) do\n262\t      {:ok, canonical_string} -> Ecto.UUID.dump!(canonical_string)\n263\t      :error -> v\n264\t    end\n265\t  end\n266\t\n267\t  defp normalize_filter_value(_key, v), do: v\n268\t\n269\t  defp row_to_log([id, inserted_at, level, source, route_template, request_id, message, metadata, war_room_id]) do\n270\t    %{\n271\t      id: id,\n272\t      inserted_at: inserted_at,\n273\t      level: level,\n274\t      source: source,\n275\t      route_template: route_template,\n276\t      request_id: request_id,\n277\t      message: message,\n278\t      metadata: metadata,\n279\t      war_room_id: war_room_id\n280\t    }\n281\t  end\n282\tend\n283\t"}]},"uuid":"1737a7b2-3ab1-42df-b084-5f099a55d46f","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:54:28.169Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/web/log_store.ex","content":"defmodule Nebu.Web.LogStore do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  Public-facing module for the operational log store\n  (enhanced-logging/operational-log-store).\n\n  Follows the `Nebu.Web.TokenDenylist`-style naming convention: the domain\n  (`Nebu.Web`, shared infrastructure, exempt from ADR-020's Public-API rule —\n  same as `Nebu.Web.Plugs.RequestLogger` itself) exposes exactly one module\n  other code should call into.\n\n  ## Design (see the story's Background section for full rationale)\n\n  The operational log store is EXPLICITLY SEPARATE from\n  `Nebu.Compliance.AuditLog` — mutable/pruneable diagnostics, not the\n  tamper-evident/immutable compliance audit trail.\n\n  A single Erlang `:logger` handler (`Nebu.Web.LogStore.Handler`, attached via\n  `attach_handler/0`) intercepts EVERY `Logger.*` call app-wide:\n\n    1. ALWAYS broadcasts the parsed entry via `Phoenix.PubSub` to topic\n       `\"operational_logs\"` (independent of persistence — feeds a later\n       Admin UI live view).\n    2. CONDITIONALLY queues the entry for batched INSERT via\n       `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Writer.write_async/1`, gated on `should_persist?/1`\n       (WARN/ERROR always; INFO/DEBUG per the `server_config` policy exposed\n       by `get_policy/0`).\n  \"\"\"\n\n  alias Nebu.Web.LogStore.Handler\n\n  @handler_id :nebu_operational_log_handler\n\n  @policy_keys ~w(\n    log_collect_info log_retention_info_days log_retention_info_max_count\n    log_collect_debug log_retention_debug_days log_retention_debug_max_count\n  )a\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Attaches the central `:logger` handler (AC2). Idempotent — tolerates being\n  called when the handler is already attached (does NOT register a\n  duplicate).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec attach_handler() :: :ok\n  def attach_handler do\n    case :logger.add_handler(@handler_id, Handler, %{}) do\n      :ok -> :ok\n      {:error, {:already_exist, _id}} -> :ok\n      {:error, reason} -> raise \"Nebu.Web.LogStore.attach_handler/0 failed: #{inspect(reason)}\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Returns `{:ok, policy}` — the current admin-configurable logging policy,\n  read from `server_config` (AC3/AC9). Every one of the 5 policy keys is\n  always present in the returned map (defaulted when unset in\n  `server_config`, or when `Nebu.Repo` is not running at all — e.g. plain unit\n  test runs with no live DB): INFO/DEBUG collection default to `false`\n  (disabled); the 3 retention keys default to `nil` (no TTL/cap configured).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec get_policy() :: {:ok, map()}\n  def get_policy do\n    {:ok, read_policy_from_db()}\n  end\n\n  @doc false\n  # Live DB read of the policy — the ONLY place that actually queries\n  # server_config for these 5 keys. Called by `get_policy/0` (always a live\n  # read, used by tests/AC9 and by `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Retention`, an hourly\n  # background job where a live read is perfectly fine) AND by\n  # `Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache` (its own periodic refresh tick — NEVER\n  # called from `Handler.log/2`'s hot path, see `should_persist?/1`).\n  @spec read_policy_from_db() :: map()\n  def read_policy_from_db do\n    raw = read_server_config()\n\n    %{\n      log_collect_info: to_bool(Map.get(raw, \"log_collect_info\")),\n      log_retention_info_days: to_int(Map.get(raw, \"log_retention_info_days\")),\n      log_retention_info_max_count: to_int(Map.get(raw, \"log_retention_info_max_count\")),\n      log_collect_debug: to_bool(Map.get(raw, \"log_collect_debug\")),\n      log_retention_debug_days: to_int(Map.get(raw, \"log_retention_debug_days\")),\n      log_retention_debug_max_count: to_int(Map.get(raw, \"log_retention_debug_max_count\"))\n    }\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Forces `Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache` to refresh immediately from the DB.\n  Called by `Nebu.Admin.DB.upsert_server_config/1` whenever a change touches\n  one of the 5 log-policy keys (mirrors the existing\n  `Nebu.Web.OidcConfig.refresh/0` / `Nebu.Matrix.invalidate_oidc_claim_cache/0`\n  cache-invalidation-on-write pattern already used there). Safe no-op when\n  the cache process is not running.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec refresh_policy_cache() :: :ok\n  def refresh_policy_cache, do: Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache.refresh_now()\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Returns the 5 policy keys this module manages (mirrors the\n  `config_update_mutable` RLS allowlist entries added by this story's\n  migration).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec policy_keys() :: [atom()]\n  def policy_keys, do: @policy_keys\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Decides whether a log entry at `level` (a level STRING, e.g. `\"info\"`) must\n  be persisted to `operational_logs` (AC7): WARN and ERROR (and any other\n  level not explicitly gated — fail-safe toward persisting unknown/critical\n  levels) always persist; INFO/DEBUG are gated by the `server_config` policy.\n\n  Reads the policy from `Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache` (a lock-free,\n  `persistent_term`-backed cache, refreshed periodically) rather than\n  `get_policy/0` directly — NEVER performs a live DB read on this path.\n\n  This matters beyond the general \"handler must not block\" design goal: this\n  function is called from `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Handler.log/2`, which runs\n  SYNCHRONOUSLY in whatever process is currently `Logger.*`-ing, including\n  `Nebu.Repo.QueryTelemetry`'s own `Logger.debug/1` call — fired from\n  WITHIN the telemetry callback of an Ecto query, in the SAME process that\n  is using (or just used) a `Nebu.Repo` connection checkout. A live DB read\n  here would re-enter `Nebu.Repo`'s connection pool from inside another\n  query's completion callback — this was observed as a genuine\n  connection-pool timeout in the integration suite before the cache was\n  introduced.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec should_persist?(String.t() | nil) :: boolean()\n  def should_persist?(\"info\") do\n    policy = Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache.get()\n    policy.log_collect_info == true\n  end\n\n  def should_persist?(\"debug\") do\n    policy = Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache.get()\n    policy.log_collect_debug == true\n  end\n\n  def should_persist?(level) when is_binary(level), do: true\n  def should_persist?(_level), do: false\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Route-aware persistence policy (enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls,\n  AC4): the ingest-path check that additionally enforces the Trace Map's\n  off-by-default long-polling endpoints\n  (`Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.off_endpoint?/1` — the single canonical\n  source of truth, AC13 reuse).\n\n  ERROR-level entries are ALWAYS persisted, even on an off-list route with\n  no active capture (story amendment 2026-07-07, \"AC4 errors-always-persist\n  exception\": \"off\" silences noise, never failures — a silently-red zone\n  contradicts the leitstand mission). An off-list route with no active\n  capture suppresses every other level (DEBUG/INFO/WARN). While a capture\n  IS active (checked via the non-blocking `Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache`,\n  never a live DB read on this path — same hot-path reasoning as\n  `should_persist?/1`), the off-list route defers to the level-only policy\n  above, exactly as if it were not off-list at all. Any other route (or a\n  `nil` route — no `route_template` on the log entry) also defers to the\n  level-only policy unchanged.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec should_persist?(String.t() | nil, String.t() | nil) :: boolean()\n  def should_persist?(level, route_template) do\n    cond do\n      level == \"error\" ->\n        true\n\n      Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.off_endpoint?(route_template) ->\n        Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache.active?(route_template) and should_persist?(level)\n\n      true ->\n        should_persist?(level)\n    end\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Minimal read API (AC9) for tests and the (later) Admin UI. `filters` is an\n  options map supporting `level`, `source`, `route_template`, `request_id` —\n  any subset. An empty/no-filter map returns every row, most-recent first.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec get_logs(map()) :: {:ok, [map()]}\n  def get_logs(filters) when is_map(filters) do\n    {where_sql, params} = build_where(filters)\n\n    sql = \"\"\"\n    SELECT id, inserted_at, level, source, route_template, request_id, message, metadata, war_room_id\n    FROM operational_logs\n    #{where_sql}\n    ORDER BY inserted_at DESC\n    \"\"\"\n\n    result = Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(Nebu.Repo, sql, params)\n    {:ok, Enum.map(result.rows, &row_to_log/1)}\n  end\n\n  # ── policy helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  defp read_server_config do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      {:ok, config} = Nebu.Admin.DB.get_server_config()\n      config\n    else\n      %{}\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp to_bool(nil), do: false\n  defp to_bool(true), do: true\n  defp to_bool(false), do: false\n  defp to_bool(\"true\"), do: true\n  defp to_bool(\"false\"), do: false\n  defp to_bool(_), do: false\n\n  defp to_int(nil), do: nil\n  defp to_int(value) when is_integer(value), do: value\n\n  defp to_int(value) when is_binary(value) do\n    case Integer.parse(value) do\n      {int, _rest} -> int\n      :error -> nil\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp to_int(_), do: nil\n\n  # ── get_logs/1 helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  # enhanced-logging/war-room-mode (AC12): war_room_id is already SELECTed\n  # and returned by row_to_log/1 below — it was just missing from this\n  # allowlist, making the AC12 \"filter the stream by war_room_id\" use case\n  # silently impossible (the filter key was accepted but ignored) even\n  # though the column/SELECT already supported it.\n  @allowed_filter_keys ~w(level source route_template request_id war_room_id)\n\n  defp build_where(filters) do\n    filters\n    |> Enum.map(fn {k, v} -> {to_string(k), v} end)\n    |> Enum.filter(fn {k, _v} -> k in @allowed_filter_keys end)\n    |> Enum.map(fn {k, v} -> {k, normalize_filter_value(k, v)} end)\n    |> Enum.with_index(1)\n    |> Enum.reduce({[], []}, fn {{k, v}, idx}, {conds, params} ->\n      {conds ++ [\"#{k} = $#{idx}\"], params ++ [v]}\n    end)\n    |> then(fn {conds, params} ->\n      where_sql = if conds == [], do: \"\", else: \"WHERE \" <> Enum.join(conds, \" AND \")\n      {where_sql, params}\n    end)\n  end\n\n  # enhanced-logging/war-room-mode (AC12): `war_room_id` is a native\n  # Postgres `uuid` column — Postgrex decodes/encodes `uuid` as a 16-byte\n  # BINARY (see `Postgrex.Extensions.UUID`), unlike every other identifier\n  # in this codebase (room_id/user_id/device_id/request_id), which are\n  # plain TEXT and therefore always the human-readable string form.\n  # `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.mount/3`'s `war_room_id` URL query param arrives\n  # as a canonical hex-dash STRING (as any real bookmarked URL would carry\n  # it); a value obtained directly from a prior `RETURNING id`/`SELECT id`\n  # call elsewhere may already be the raw 16-byte binary form. Accept\n  # EITHER here and normalize to whichever form Postgrex's own encoder\n  # actually requires for a `uuid`-typed bound parameter (raw binary) —\n  # `Ecto.UUID.cast/1` accepts both forms as input, `Ecto.UUID.dump!/1`\n  # then converts the resulting canonical string to that raw binary.\n  defp normalize_filter_value(\"war_room_id\", v) when is_binary(v) do\n    case Ecto.UUID.cast(v) do\n      {:ok, canonical_string} -> Ecto.UUID.dump!(canonical_string)\n      :error -> v\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp normalize_filter_value(_key, v), do: v\n\n  defp row_to_log([id, inserted_at, level, source, route_template, request_id, message, metadata, war_room_id]) do\n    %{\n      id: id,\n      inserted_at: inserted_at,\n      level: level,\n      source: source,\n      route_template: route_template,\n      request_id: request_id,\n      message: message,\n      metadata: metadata,\n      war_room_id: war_room_id\n    }\n  end\nend\n","numLines":283,"startLine":1,"totalLines":283}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a688e5ef-2387-4f1a-a8ac-c4919da24602","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
{"parentUuid":"1737a7b2-3ab1-42df-b084-5f099a55d46f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a11938c512cfadfa8","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CcpJs8HK4Bkgs1kFYkUUX","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CyaqPb5ZX8re8BvQPUXrBT","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/web/log_store/handler.ex"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8397,"cache_read_input_tokens":30658,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":8397,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CcpJs7GJ8dTiyYKMKjxqs","attributionAgent":"nebu-code-reviewer","type":"assistant","uuid":"eee2c6a9-a1bc-49bb-ac77-db81623ad05c","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:54:29.150Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
{"parentUuid":"eee2c6a9-a1bc-49bb-ac77-db81623ad05c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"7f0c215b-3d46-41aa-829c-b3f1d75c1682","agentId":"a11938c512cfadfa8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CyaqPb5ZX8re8BvQPUXrBT","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Web.LogStore.Handler do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  The Erlang `:logger` handler callback for the operational log store\n4\t  (enhanced-logging/operational-log-store, AC2/AC10).\n5\t\n6\t  Attached (idempotently) via `Nebu.Web.LogStore.attach_handler/0`, this\n7\t  module's `log/2` is invoked SYNCHRONOUSLY, in the calling process, for\n8\t  EVERY `Logger.*` call app-wide once attached — it therefore MUST be cheap\n9\t  and MUST NEVER raise (a crash here would crash whatever unrelated code\n10\t  happened to log something). It does no blocking DB write itself: it always\n11\t  broadcasts (cheap, in-memory `Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast/3`) and, only when\n12\t  the entry's level should be persisted per policy, hands the entry to\n13\t  `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Writer.write_async/1` — a non-blocking `GenServer.cast/2`\n14\t  to a dedicated batching writer process.\n15\t\n16\t  ## SECURITY LAW — never a second unredacted leak path\n17\t\n18\t  This is a GENERIC handler — it has no awareness of HTTP requests, Matrix\n19\t  events, or `PayloadRedactor`. It renders whatever the log event's `msg`\n20\t  already is and passes it through UNCHANGED. The \"No Sensitive Data in Logs\"\n21\t  guarantee for HTTP payload content is therefore entirely the upstream\n22\t  responsibility of `Nebu.Web.Plugs.RequestLogger` (which redacts BEFORE\n23\t  calling `Logger.debug/1`) — this handler does no redaction of its own and\n24\t  needs none, precisely BECAUSE it never re-derives fields from anything\n25\t  other than the already-rendered log event it is handed.\n26\t  \"\"\"\n27\t\n28\t  alias Nebu.Web.LogStore\n29\t\n30\t  @doc \"\"\"\n31\t  The `:logger` handler callback: `HandlerModule:log(LogEvent, Config)`.\n32\t  Always returns `:ok` — any unexpected shape in `log_event` is caught and\n33\t  swallowed rather than raised, since this runs inline in an arbitrary\n34\t  caller's process.\n35\t  \"\"\"\n36\t  @spec log(map(), map()) :: :ok\n37\t  def log(log_event, _config) do\n38\t    entry =\n39\t      log_event\n40\t      |> parse_log_event()\n41\t      |> maybe_tag_war_room()\n42\t\n43\t    safe_broadcast(entry)\n44\t\n45\t    # enhanced-logging/war-room-mode (AC4): an active, MATCHING war room\n46\t    # session persists REGARDLESS of the ambient per-level retention policy\n47\t    # — e.g. a DEBUG entry even when log_collect_debug is false. The\n48\t    # war-room match/tag decision (maybe_tag_war_room/1 above) already ran\n49\t    # BEFORE this gate, synchronously, precisely so an entry it tagged is\n50\t    # never discarded here.\n51\t    # enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls (AC4): route-aware so the\n52\t    # Trace Map's off-by-default long-polling endpoints are genuinely not\n53\t    # collected server-side (unless a temporary capture is active, or the\n54\t    # entry is ERROR-level — errors-always-persist).\n55\t    if LogStore.should_persist?(entry.level, entry.route_template) or not is_nil(entry[:war_room_id]) do\n56\t      safe_write(entry)\n57\t    end\n58\t\n59\t    :ok\n60\t  rescue\n61\t    _ -> :ok\n62\t  end\n63\t\n64\t  # ── parsing ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n65\t\n66\t  defp parse_log_event(log_event) do\n67\t    meta = Map.get(log_event, :meta, %{})\n68\t\n69\t    %{\n70\t      level: level_to_string(Map.get(log_event, :level)),\n71\t      source: source_from_meta(meta),\n72\t      route_template: metadata_string(Map.get(meta, :route_template)),\n73\t      request_id: metadata_string(Map.get(meta, :request_id)),\n74\t      message: render_msg(Map.get(log_event, :msg)),\n75\t      metadata: safe_metadata(meta)\n76\t    }\n77\t  end\n78\t\n79\t  defp level_to_string(level) when is_atom(level), do: Atom.to_string(level)\n80\t  defp level_to_string(level) when is_binary(level), do: level\n81\t  defp level_to_string(level), do: inspect(level)\n82\t\n83\t  # A best-effort classification, only used to populate the `source` column —\n84\t  # never relied upon for redaction/security decisions. Falls back to the\n85\t  # calling module's own name so unrecognised sources are still identifiable.\n86\t  #\n87\t  # bug-fix cycle 6 (admin-logs-monitoring-view, code-review finding M2\n88\t  # follow-up): this used to key off a bare `:module` metadata key, which\n89\t  # Elixir's `Logger.debug/info/warning/error` macros NEVER actually inject —\n90\t  # only `:mfa` (`{module, function, arity}`), captured automatically at the\n91\t  # macro's call site regardless of whether the call is\n92\t  # `Logger.info(\"string\", metadata)` or the \"report\" style\n93\t  # `Logger.info(map)`/`Logger.debug(fn -> ... end)` used by `RequestLogger`\n94\t  # and `Nebu.Repo.QueryTelemetry`. That meant `source_from_meta/1` ALWAYS\n95\t  # fell through to `\"unknown\"` for every real call in the app, breaking\n96\t  # `LogsLive.build_row/1`'s `source == \"http\"` lookup (AC6: status/method/\n97\t  # duration row display) and the AC1 \"Sources\" trace-map bubble counts for\n98\t  # ALL real traffic — see the regression tests below and in\n99\t  # `handler_test.exs`/`request_logger_test.exs`. `module_from_meta/1` derives\n100\t  # the calling module from `:mfa` first, falling back to a bare `:module` key\n101\t  # (cheap, harmless) for any caller that ever sets it explicitly.\n102\t  defp source_from_meta(meta) do\n103\t    case module_from_meta(meta) do\n104\t      Nebu.Web.Plugs.RequestLogger -> \"http\"\n105\t      Nebu.Repo.QueryTelemetry -> \"ecto\"\n106\t      module when is_atom(module) and not is_nil(module) -> Atom.to_string(module)\n107\t      _ -> \"unknown\"\n108\t    end\n109\t  end\n110\t\n111\t  defp module_from_meta(meta) do\n112\t    case Map.get(meta, :mfa) do\n113\t      {module, _fun, _arity} -> module\n114\t      _ -> Map.get(meta, :module)\n115\t    end\n116\t  end\n117\t\n118\t  defp metadata_string(nil), do: nil\n119\t  defp metadata_string(value) when is_binary(value), do: value\n120\t  defp metadata_string(value), do: to_string(value)\n121\t\n122\t  # Drops the fields already surfaced as their own columns/handled elsewhere\n123\t  # (time/pid/gl/domain/module/function/file/line/mfa are Logger-internal\n124\t  # bookkeeping; request_id/route_template are their own columns) and coerces\n125\t  # remaining metadata values into JSON/inspect-safe terms.\n126\t  @drop_meta_keys ~w(time pid gl domain module function file line mfa request_id route_template)a\n127\t\n128\t  defp safe_metadata(meta) when is_map(meta) do\n129\t    meta\n130\t    |> Map.drop(@drop_meta_keys)\n131\t    |> Enum.into(%{}, fn {k, v} -> {to_string(k), safe_meta_value(v)} end)\n132\t  end\n133\t\n134\t  defp safe_metadata(_meta), do: %{}\n135\t\n136\t  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_pid(v), do: inspect(v)\n137\t  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_reference(v), do: inspect(v)\n138\t  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_function(v), do: inspect(v)\n139\t  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_tuple(v), do: inspect(v)\n140\t  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_atom(v), do: v\n141\t  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_binary(v), do: v\n142\t  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_number(v), do: v\n143\t  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_map(v), do: safe_metadata(v)\n144\t  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_list(v), do: Enum.map(v, &safe_meta_value/1)\n145\t  defp safe_meta_value(v), do: inspect(v)\n146\t\n147\t  # Renders the `:logger` event's `msg` field, whatever shape it arrives in,\n148\t  # to a plain string — WITHOUT touching/re-deriving its content (see\n149\t  # moduledoc SECURITY LAW). `RequestLogger`'s `Logger.debug(fn -> ... end)`\n150\t  # calls already return the fully-redacted, `Jason.encode!/1`-ed JSON string\n151\t  # by the time it reaches here as a `{:string, binary}` msg.\n152\t  defp render_msg({:string, chardata}) do\n153\t    IO.chardata_to_string(chardata)\n154\t  rescue\n155\t    _ -> inspect(chardata)\n156\t  end\n157\t\n158\t  defp render_msg({:report, report}) when is_map(report) or is_list(report) do\n159\t    inspect(report)\n160\t  end\n161\t\n162\t  defp render_msg({:format, format, args}) do\n163\t    format\n164\t    |> :io_lib.format(args)\n165\t    |> IO.iodata_to_binary()\n166\t  rescue\n167\t    _ -> inspect({format, args})\n168\t  end\n169\t\n170\t  defp render_msg(other), do: inspect(other)\n171\t\n172\t  # ── war room tagging (enhanced-logging/war-room-mode, AC4/AC9) ─────────────\n173\t  #\n174\t  # Delegates the scope/filter match (against route_template/metadata's\n175\t  # user_id/device_id — never message content, see moduledoc SECURITY LAW)\n176\t  # to Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check/1, which is cheap/non-blocking when\n177\t  # no war room session is active (see that module's own moduledoc) and\n178\t  # never raises (any failure — including the WarRoom process being down —\n179\t  # is caught and treated as \"no match\", never a logging-path crash).\n180\t  defp maybe_tag_war_room(entry) do\n181\t    case safe_war_room_check(entry) do\n182\t      nil -> entry\n183\t      war_room_id -> Map.put(entry, :war_room_id, war_room_id)\n184\t    end\n185\t  end\n186\t\n187\t  defp safe_war_room_check(entry) do\n188\t    Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check(entry)\n189\t  rescue\n190\t    _ -> nil\n191\t  end\n192\t\n193\t  # ── side effects (broadcast / write) — isolated so one failing does not\n194\t  # prevent the other, and neither ever propagates back to the caller ──────────\n195\t\n196\t  defp safe_broadcast(entry) do\n197\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.PubSub) do\n198\t      Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(Nebu.PubSub, \"operational_logs\", entry)\n199\t    end\n200\t\n201\t    :ok\n202\t  rescue\n203\t    _ -> :ok\n204\t  end\n205\t\n206\t  defp safe_write(entry) do\n207\t    Nebu.Web.LogStore.Writer.write_async(entry)\n208\t  rescue\n209\t    _ -> :ok\n210\t  end\n211\tend\n212\t"}]},"uuid":"858b39bb-3bbe-45bd-96fb-c0f827004cea","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:54:29.182Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/web/log_store/handler.ex","content":"defmodule Nebu.Web.LogStore.Handler do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  The Erlang `:logger` handler callback for the operational log store\n  (enhanced-logging/operational-log-store, AC2/AC10).\n\n  Attached (idempotently) via `Nebu.Web.LogStore.attach_handler/0`, this\n  module's `log/2` is invoked SYNCHRONOUSLY, in the calling process, for\n  EVERY `Logger.*` call app-wide once attached — it therefore MUST be cheap\n  and MUST NEVER raise (a crash here would crash whatever unrelated code\n  happened to log something). It does no blocking DB write itself: it always\n  broadcasts (cheap, in-memory `Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast/3`) and, only when\n  the entry's level should be persisted per policy, hands the entry to\n  `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Writer.write_async/1` — a non-blocking `GenServer.cast/2`\n  to a dedicated batching writer process.\n\n  ## SECURITY LAW — never a second unredacted leak path\n\n  This is a GENERIC handler — it has no awareness of HTTP requests, Matrix\n  events, or `PayloadRedactor`. It renders whatever the log event's `msg`\n  already is and passes it through UNCHANGED. The \"No Sensitive Data in Logs\"\n  guarantee for HTTP payload content is therefore entirely the upstream\n  responsibility of `Nebu.Web.Plugs.RequestLogger` (which redacts BEFORE\n  calling `Logger.debug/1`) — this handler does no redaction of its own and\n  needs none, precisely BECAUSE it never re-derives fields from anything\n  other than the already-rendered log event it is handed.\n  \"\"\"\n\n  alias Nebu.Web.LogStore\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  The `:logger` handler callback: `HandlerModule:log(LogEvent, Config)`.\n  Always returns `:ok` — any unexpected shape in `log_event` is caught and\n  swallowed rather than raised, since this runs inline in an arbitrary\n  caller's process.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec log(map(), map()) :: :ok\n  def log(log_event, _config) do\n    entry =\n      log_event\n      |> parse_log_event()\n      |> maybe_tag_war_room()\n\n    safe_broadcast(entry)\n\n    # enhanced-logging/war-room-mode (AC4): an active, MATCHING war room\n    # session persists REGARDLESS of the ambient per-level retention policy\n    # — e.g. a DEBUG entry even when log_collect_debug is false. The\n    # war-room match/tag decision (maybe_tag_war_room/1 above) already ran\n    # BEFORE this gate, synchronously, precisely so an entry it tagged is\n    # never discarded here.\n    # enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls (AC4): route-aware so the\n    # Trace Map's off-by-default long-polling endpoints are genuinely not\n    # collected server-side (unless a temporary capture is active, or the\n    # entry is ERROR-level — errors-always-persist).\n    if LogStore.should_persist?(entry.level, entry.route_template) or not is_nil(entry[:war_room_id]) do\n      safe_write(entry)\n    end\n\n    :ok\n  rescue\n    _ -> :ok\n  end\n\n  # ── parsing ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  defp parse_log_event(log_event) do\n    meta = Map.get(log_event, :meta, %{})\n\n    %{\n      level: level_to_string(Map.get(log_event, :level)),\n      source: source_from_meta(meta),\n      route_template: metadata_string(Map.get(meta, :route_template)),\n      request_id: metadata_string(Map.get(meta, :request_id)),\n      message: render_msg(Map.get(log_event, :msg)),\n      metadata: safe_metadata(meta)\n    }\n  end\n\n  defp level_to_string(level) when is_atom(level), do: Atom.to_string(level)\n  defp level_to_string(level) when is_binary(level), do: level\n  defp level_to_string(level), do: inspect(level)\n\n  # A best-effort classification, only used to populate the `source` column —\n  # never relied upon for redaction/security decisions. Falls back to the\n  # calling module's own name so unrecognised sources are still identifiable.\n  #\n  # bug-fix cycle 6 (admin-logs-monitoring-view, code-review finding M2\n  # follow-up): this used to key off a bare `:module` metadata key, which\n  # Elixir's `Logger.debug/info/warning/error` macros NEVER actually inject —\n  # only `:mfa` (`{module, function, arity}`), captured automatically at the\n  # macro's call site regardless of whether the call is\n  # `Logger.info(\"string\", metadata)` or the \"report\" style\n  # `Logger.info(map)`/`Logger.debug(fn -> ... end)` used by `RequestLogger`\n  # and `Nebu.Repo.QueryTelemetry`. That meant `source_from_meta/1` ALWAYS\n  # fell through to `\"unknown\"` for every real call in the app, breaking\n  # `LogsLive.build_row/1`'s `source == \"http\"` lookup (AC6: status/method/\n  # duration row display) and the AC1 \"Sources\" trace-map bubble counts for\n  # ALL real traffic — see the regression tests below and in\n  # `handler_test.exs`/`request_logger_test.exs`. `module_from_meta/1` derives\n  # the calling module from `:mfa` first, falling back to a bare `:module` key\n  # (cheap, harmless) for any caller that ever sets it explicitly.\n  defp source_from_meta(meta) do\n    case module_from_meta(meta) do\n      Nebu.Web.Plugs.RequestLogger -> \"http\"\n      Nebu.Repo.QueryTelemetry -> \"ecto\"\n      module when is_atom(module) and not is_nil(module) -> Atom.to_string(module)\n      _ -> \"unknown\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp module_from_meta(meta) do\n    case Map.get(meta, :mfa) do\n      {module, _fun, _arity} -> module\n      _ -> Map.get(meta, :module)\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp metadata_string(nil), do: nil\n  defp metadata_string(value) when is_binary(value), do: value\n  defp metadata_string(value), do: to_string(value)\n\n  # Drops the fields already surfaced as their own columns/handled elsewhere\n  # (time/pid/gl/domain/module/function/file/line/mfa are Logger-internal\n  # bookkeeping; request_id/route_template are their own columns) and coerces\n  # remaining metadata values into JSON/inspect-safe terms.\n  @drop_meta_keys ~w(time pid gl domain module function file line mfa request_id route_template)a\n\n  defp safe_metadata(meta) when is_map(meta) do\n    meta\n    |> Map.drop(@drop_meta_keys)\n    |> Enum.into(%{}, fn {k, v} -> {to_string(k), safe_meta_value(v)} end)\n  end\n\n  defp safe_metadata(_meta), do: %{}\n\n  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_pid(v), do: inspect(v)\n  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_reference(v), do: inspect(v)\n  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_function(v), do: inspect(v)\n  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_tuple(v), do: inspect(v)\n  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_atom(v), do: v\n  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_binary(v), do: v\n  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_number(v), do: v\n  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_map(v), do: safe_metadata(v)\n  defp safe_meta_value(v) when is_list(v), do: Enum.map(v, &safe_meta_value/1)\n  defp safe_meta_value(v), do: inspect(v)\n\n  # Renders the `:logger` event's `msg` field, whatever shape it arrives in,\n  # to a plain string — WITHOUT touching/re-deriving its content (see\n  # moduledoc SECURITY LAW). `RequestLogger`'s `Logger.debug(fn -> ... end)`\n  # calls already return the fully-redacted, `Jason.encode!/1`-ed JSON string\n  # by the time it reaches here as a `{:string, binary}` msg.\n  defp render_msg({:string, chardata}) do\n    IO.chardata_to_string(chardata)\n  rescue\n    _ -> inspect(chardata)\n  end\n\n  defp render_msg({:report, report}) when is_map(report) or is_list(report) do\n    inspect(report)\n  end\n\n  defp render_msg({:format, format, args}) do\n    format\n    |> :io_lib.format(args)\n    |> IO.iodata_to_binary()\n  rescue\n    _ -> inspect({format, args})\n  end\n\n  defp render_msg(other), do: inspect(other)\n\n  # ── war room tagging (enhanced-logging/war-room-mode, AC4/AC9) ─────────────\n  #\n  # Delegates the scope/filter match (against route_template/metadata's\n  # user_id/device_id — never message content, see moduledoc SECURITY LAW)\n  # to Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check/1, which is cheap/non-blocking when\n  # no war room session is active (see that module's own moduledoc) and\n  # never raises (any failure — including the WarRoom process being down —\n  # is caught and treated as \"no match\", never a logging-path crash).\n  defp maybe_tag_war_room(entry) do\n    case safe_war_room_check(entry) do\n      nil -> entry\n      war_room_id -> Map.put(entry, :war_room_id, war_room_id)\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp safe_war_room_check(entry) do\n    Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check(entry)\n  rescue\n    _ -> nil\n  end\n\n  # ── side effects (broadcast / write) — isolated so one failing does not\n  # prevent the other, and neither ever propagates back to the caller ──────────\n\n  defp safe_broadcast(entry) do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.PubSub) do\n      Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(Nebu.PubSub, \"operational_logs\", entry)\n    end\n\n    :ok\n  rescue\n    _ -> :ok\n  end\n\n  defp safe_write(entry) do\n    Nebu.Web.LogStore.Writer.write_async(entry)\n  rescue\n    _ -> :ok\n  end\nend\n","numLines":212,"startLine":1,"totalLines":212}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"eee2c6a9-a1bc-49bb-ac77-db81623ad05c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
{"parentUuid":"858b39bb-3bbe-45bd-96fb-c0f827004cea","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a11938c512cfadfa8","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CcpJs8HK4Bkgs1kFYkUUX","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018s8D1eJfguqs6KJ8yaVGGN","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/compliance/audit_writer.ex"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8397,"cache_read_input_tokens":30658,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":8397,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CcpJs7GJ8dTiyYKMKjxqs","attributionAgent":"nebu-code-reviewer","type":"assistant","uuid":"db06003c-3960-41b3-9392-a1b48ec7f169","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:54:29.727Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
{"parentUuid":"db06003c-3960-41b3-9392-a1b48ec7f169","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"7f0c215b-3d46-41aa-829c-b3f1d75c1682","agentId":"a11938c512cfadfa8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018s8D1eJfguqs6KJ8yaVGGN","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Compliance.AuditWriter do\n2\t  require Logger\n3\t\n4\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n5\t  Generic audit log writer. Every call to log/6 or log/7 inserts exactly one\n6\t  row into `audit_log` via a separate Repo.transaction/1 — never inside the\n7\t  caller's transaction. A caller rollback cannot suppress the audit record.\n8\t\n9\t  Error policy: never raises. On DB failure returns {:error, :audit_write_failed}\n10\t  and logs via Logger.error/2.\n11\t\n12\t  Persistenz-Strategie: Option C — Stateless. No GenServer state, no queue.\n13\t  Each log/6 call is atomic in its own Repo.transaction/1.\n14\t\n15\t  Story 5.29c — AC8 (FB-52-02): unknown action strings are rejected with\n16\t  {:error, :audit_unknown_action} before any DB call. The @known_actions\n17\t  allowlist covers all Epic-5 vocabulary. Adding a new audit action requires\n18\t  updating this list first.\n19\t  \"\"\"\n20\t\n21\t  alias Nebu.Compliance.AuditLogEntry\n22\t\n23\t  # AC8 (Story 5.29c): allowlist of all valid audit action strings in Epic 5.\n24\t  # compliance_session_revoked was added by Story 5.29c (AC2).\n25\t  # user_deactivated, user_reactivated, update_user_role added for Epic 9 (Story 9.1 HIGH-1 fix).\n26\t  # member_added, member_removed added for admin-initiated room membership (room-management rm4).\n27\t  # room_visibility_changed added for admin room-visibility toggle (room-management rm1).\n28\t  # enable_temporary_capture, disable_temporary_capture added for the Trace Map's\n29\t  # off-by-default long-polling endpoints (enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls,\n30\t  # AC12 Option B — ANSWERED design question 2): enabling collection on a\n31\t  # previously-off endpoint is an admin-initiated logging-policy change.\n32\t  @known_actions ~w(\n33\t    admin_login\n34\t    admin_login_failed\n35\t    admin_logout\n36\t    bootstrap_completed\n37\t    bootstrap_failed\n38\t    room_created\n39\t    room_joined\n40\t    compliance_access_requested\n41\t    compliance_access_approved\n42\t    compliance_access_rejected\n43\t    compliance_session_issued\n44\t    compliance_session_expired\n45\t    compliance_session_revoked\n46\t    compliance_export_downloaded\n47\t    user_keys_deleted\n48\t    user_keys_deletion_attempted\n49\t    user_anonymized\n50\t    user_deactivated\n51\t    user_reactivated\n52\t    update_user_role\n53\t    room_archived\n54\t    room_unarchived\n55\t    room_auto_join_changed\n56\t    room_visibility_changed\n57\t    member_added\n58\t    member_removed\n59\t    device_renamed\n60\t    device_deleted\n61\t    server_config_updated\n62\t    room_upgraded\n63\t    gdpr_deletion\n64\t    users_bulk_imported\n65\t    enable_temporary_capture\n66\t    disable_temporary_capture\n67\t  )\n68\t\n69\t  # Configurable repo for test injection. Production uses Nebu.Repo.\n70\t  defp repo, do: Application.get_env(:compliance, :repo, Nebu.Repo)\n71\t\n72\t  def log(actor_user_id, action, target_type, target_id, metadata, outcome, error_detail \\\\ nil) do\n73\t    # AC8 (Story 5.29c): reject unknown action strings before changeset validation.\n74\t    # Unknown actions indicate a bug (typo, missing allowlist entry) rather than\n75\t    # a validation error — use a distinct error atom so callers can distinguish.\n76\t    if is_binary(action) and action != \"\" and action not in @known_actions do\n77\t      Logger.error(\"AuditWriter: rejected unknown audit action\",\n78\t        action: inspect(action),\n79\t        actor: actor_user_id\n80\t      )\n81\t      {:error, :audit_unknown_action}\n82\t    else\n83\t      do_log(actor_user_id, action, target_type, target_id, metadata, outcome, error_detail)\n84\t    end\n85\t  end\n86\t\n87\t  defp do_log(actor_user_id, action, target_type, target_id, metadata, outcome, error_detail) do\n88\t    # Kassandra MEDIUM-3 (2026-04-23): validate via changeset so required fields\n89\t    # (actor_user_id, action, outcome) cannot be silently persisted as nil or \"\".\n90\t    # Without this, insert! would have written \"actor_user_id=nil\" and corrupted\n91\t    # the audit trail.\n92\t    changeset =\n93\t      AuditLogEntry.changeset(%AuditLogEntry{}, %{\n94\t        actor_user_id: actor_user_id,\n95\t        action:        action,\n96\t        target_type:   target_type,\n97\t        target_id:     target_id,\n98\t        metadata:      metadata,\n99\t        outcome:       outcome,\n100\t        error_detail:  error_detail\n101\t      })\n102\t\n103\t    if changeset.valid? do\n104\t      entry = Ecto.Changeset.apply_changes(changeset)\n105\t\n106\t      # never-raise (moduledoc Error policy): a transaction against an unstarted /\n107\t      # missing Repo raises RuntimeError from Ecto.Repo.Registry.lookup. Catch it so\n108\t      # a down DB degrades to {:error, :audit_write_failed} instead of crashing the\n109\t      # caller (e.g. an Admin device action when the Repo is unavailable).\n110\t      try do\n111\t        case repo().transaction(fn -> repo().insert!(entry) end) do\n112\t          {:ok, _} ->\n113\t            :ok\n114\t\n115\t          {:error, reason} ->\n116\t            Logger.error(\"AuditWriter: failed to write audit log\",\n117\t              action: action,\n118\t              actor: actor_user_id,\n119\t              reason: inspect(reason)\n120\t            )\n121\t            {:error, :audit_write_failed}\n122\t        end\n123\t      rescue\n124\t        # Only degrade the documented case (RuntimeError from\n125\t        # Ecto.Repo.Registry.lookup on an unstarted/missing Repo) to a generic\n126\t        # audit-write failure. Narrowing to RuntimeError keeps genuine logic bugs\n127\t        # (FunctionClauseError/ArgumentError/etc.) surfacing instead of being\n128\t        # masked as :audit_write_failed.\n129\t        e in [RuntimeError] ->\n130\t          Logger.error(\"AuditWriter: failed to write audit log (raised)\",\n131\t            action: action,\n132\t            actor: actor_user_id,\n133\t            reason: inspect(e)\n134\t          )\n135\t          {:error, :audit_write_failed}\n136\t      end\n137\t    else\n138\t      Logger.error(\"AuditWriter: refused to write audit log — validation failed\",\n139\t        action: action,\n140\t        actor: actor_user_id,\n141\t        errors: inspect(changeset.errors)\n142\t      )\n143\t      {:error, :audit_write_invalid}\n144\t    end\n145\t  end\n146\tend\n147\t"}]},"uuid":"0aa3596f-c409-464d-8940-15434d45372b","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:54:29.756Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/compliance/audit_writer.ex","content":"defmodule Nebu.Compliance.AuditWriter do\n  require Logger\n\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  Generic audit log writer. Every call to log/6 or log/7 inserts exactly one\n  row into `audit_log` via a separate Repo.transaction/1 — never inside the\n  caller's transaction. A caller rollback cannot suppress the audit record.\n\n  Error policy: never raises. On DB failure returns {:error, :audit_write_failed}\n  and logs via Logger.error/2.\n\n  Persistenz-Strategie: Option C — Stateless. No GenServer state, no queue.\n  Each log/6 call is atomic in its own Repo.transaction/1.\n\n  Story 5.29c — AC8 (FB-52-02): unknown action strings are rejected with\n  {:error, :audit_unknown_action} before any DB call. The @known_actions\n  allowlist covers all Epic-5 vocabulary. Adding a new audit action requires\n  updating this list first.\n  \"\"\"\n\n  alias Nebu.Compliance.AuditLogEntry\n\n  # AC8 (Story 5.29c): allowlist of all valid audit action strings in Epic 5.\n  # compliance_session_revoked was added by Story 5.29c (AC2).\n  # user_deactivated, user_reactivated, update_user_role added for Epic 9 (Story 9.1 HIGH-1 fix).\n  # member_added, member_removed added for admin-initiated room membership (room-management rm4).\n  # room_visibility_changed added for admin room-visibility toggle (room-management rm1).\n  # enable_temporary_capture, disable_temporary_capture added for the Trace Map's\n  # off-by-default long-polling endpoints (enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls,\n  # AC12 Option B — ANSWERED design question 2): enabling collection on a\n  # previously-off endpoint is an admin-initiated logging-policy change.\n  @known_actions ~w(\n    admin_login\n    admin_login_failed\n    admin_logout\n    bootstrap_completed\n    bootstrap_failed\n    room_created\n    room_joined\n    compliance_access_requested\n    compliance_access_approved\n    compliance_access_rejected\n    compliance_session_issued\n    compliance_session_expired\n    compliance_session_revoked\n    compliance_export_downloaded\n    user_keys_deleted\n    user_keys_deletion_attempted\n    user_anonymized\n    user_deactivated\n    user_reactivated\n    update_user_role\n    room_archived\n    room_unarchived\n    room_auto_join_changed\n    room_visibility_changed\n    member_added\n    member_removed\n    device_renamed\n    device_deleted\n    server_config_updated\n    room_upgraded\n    gdpr_deletion\n    users_bulk_imported\n    enable_temporary_capture\n    disable_temporary_capture\n  )\n\n  # Configurable repo for test injection. Production uses Nebu.Repo.\n  defp repo, do: Application.get_env(:compliance, :repo, Nebu.Repo)\n\n  def log(actor_user_id, action, target_type, target_id, metadata, outcome, error_detail \\\\ nil) do\n    # AC8 (Story 5.29c): reject unknown action strings before changeset validation.\n    # Unknown actions indicate a bug (typo, missing allowlist entry) rather than\n    # a validation error — use a distinct error atom so callers can distinguish.\n    if is_binary(action) and action != \"\" and action not in @known_actions do\n      Logger.error(\"AuditWriter: rejected unknown audit action\",\n        action: inspect(action),\n        actor: actor_user_id\n      )\n      {:error, :audit_unknown_action}\n    else\n      do_log(actor_user_id, action, target_type, target_id, metadata, outcome, error_detail)\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp do_log(actor_user_id, action, target_type, target_id, metadata, outcome, error_detail) do\n    # Kassandra MEDIUM-3 (2026-04-23): validate via changeset so required fields\n    # (actor_user_id, action, outcome) cannot be silently persisted as nil or \"\".\n    # Without this, insert! would have written \"actor_user_id=nil\" and corrupted\n    # the audit trail.\n    changeset =\n      AuditLogEntry.changeset(%AuditLogEntry{}, %{\n        actor_user_id: actor_user_id,\n        action:        action,\n        target_type:   target_type,\n        target_id:     target_id,\n        metadata:      metadata,\n        outcome:       outcome,\n        error_detail:  error_detail\n      })\n\n    if changeset.valid? do\n      entry = Ecto.Changeset.apply_changes(changeset)\n\n      # never-raise (moduledoc Error policy): a transaction against an unstarted /\n      # missing Repo raises RuntimeError from Ecto.Repo.Registry.lookup. Catch it so\n      # a down DB degrades to {:error, :audit_write_failed} instead of crashing the\n      # caller (e.g. an Admin device action when the Repo is unavailable).\n      try do\n        case repo().transaction(fn -> repo().insert!(entry) end) do\n          {:ok, _} ->\n            :ok\n\n          {:error, reason} ->\n            Logger.error(\"AuditWriter: failed to write audit log\",\n              action: action,\n              actor: actor_user_id,\n              reason: inspect(reason)\n            )\n            {:error, :audit_write_failed}\n        end\n      rescue\n        # Only degrade the documented case (RuntimeError from\n        # Ecto.Repo.Registry.lookup on an unstarted/missing Repo) to a generic\n        # audit-write failure. Narrowing to RuntimeError keeps genuine logic bugs\n        # (FunctionClauseError/ArgumentError/etc.) surfacing instead of being\n        # masked as :audit_write_failed.\n        e in [RuntimeError] ->\n          Logger.error(\"AuditWriter: failed to write audit log (raised)\",\n            action: action,\n            actor: actor_user_id,\n            reason: inspect(e)\n          )\n          {:error, :audit_write_failed}\n      end\n    else\n      Logger.error(\"AuditWriter: refused to write audit log — validation failed\",\n        action: action,\n        actor: actor_user_id,\n        errors: inspect(changeset.errors)\n      )\n      {:error, :audit_write_invalid}\n    end\n  end\nend\n","numLines":147,"startLine":1,"totalLines":147}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"db06003c-3960-41b3-9392-a1b48ec7f169","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
