{"parentUuid":"dcbd24cc-c002-4fff-9f1e-098f927699a9","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"7f0c215b-3d46-41aa-829c-b3f1d75c1682","agentId":"a11938c512cfadfa8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015iSTPGHaNCrZ1RGjRDN4jY","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Application do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  The single OTP application for Nebu (ADR-019).\n4\t\n5\t  All former umbrella apps (nebu_db, signature, permissions, presence, compliance,\n6\t  session_manager, room_manager, event_dispatcher, nebu_http) are consolidated into\n7\t  this one supervisor tree. Domains are module namespaces under `lib/nebu/`.\n8\t\n9\t  Boot-time invariants (created here, owned by the application master process so they\n10\t  survive any supervised GenServer crash/restart):\n11\t\n12\t    * ETS tables `:NebuSessions`, `:NebuPresence`, `:NebuTxnDedup` — guarded, idempotent.\n13\t    * `:pg` scope — started once.\n14\t    * `:nebu_signing_key` persistent_term — server-level Ed25519 key (MVP).\n15\t  \"\"\"\n16\t  use Application\n17\t\n18\t  @impl true\n19\t  def start(_type, _args) do\n20\t    # ── Boot-time ETS tables (idempotent guard — survive same-VM restarts) ──\n21\t    for {name, opts} <- [\n22\t          {:NebuSessions, [:named_table, :set, :public]},\n23\t          {:NebuPresence, [:named_table, :set, :public]},\n24\t          {:NebuTxnDedup, [:named_table, :set, :public]}\n25\t        ] do\n26\t      if :ets.whereis(name) == :undefined, do: :ets.new(name, opts)\n27\t    end\n28\t\n29\t    # ── Request-time ETS tables created eagerly so the lazy fast path never races\n30\t    #    under concurrent first requests (security re-review R-2). ──\n31\t    Nebu.Web.TokenDenylist.init_table()\n32\t    Nebu.Web.Plugs.RateLimit.init_table()\n33\t    Nebu.Matrix.AccountDataStore.init_table()\n34\t\n35\t    # enhanced-logging/matrix-payload-debug-logging (CI-gate cycle-2, global fix):\n36\t    # `Nebu.Repo` runs with `log: false` (server/config/config.exs) — Ecto's own\n37\t    # query logger prints bound SQL parameter values (which include credentials\n38\t    # such as opaque tokens) verbatim at :debug level, so it is disabled globally.\n39\t    # This attaches the sanitized replacement (source + duration only, never bound\n40\t    # params) to Ecto's `[:nebu, :repo, :query]` telemetry event, which Ecto still\n41\t    # emits unconditionally. Attaching is harmless when no Repo is configured (no\n42\t    # DB in pure unit runs) — the event simply never fires.\n43\t    Nebu.Repo.QueryTelemetry.attach()\n44\t\n45\t    # ── :pg scope for room/presence process-group broadcast (ADR-002/005). ──\n46\t    case :pg.start_link() do\n47\t      {:ok, _pid} -> :ok\n48\t      {:error, {:already_started, _pid}} -> :ok\n49\t    end\n50\t\n51\t    # ── Server-level Ed25519 signing key for MVP event signing (room_manager). ──\n52\t    # WARNING (MVP): regenerated on every OS/VM restart — events signed before a\n53\t    # restart are not verifiable with the new key. Phase 2 persists per-user keys to\n54\t    # the DB. Guarded so a same-VM re-entry (hot reload / test re-invocation of\n55\t    # start/2) does NOT swap the key out from under running signing processes.\n56\t    if :persistent_term.get(:nebu_signing_key, nil) == nil do\n57\t      {pub_key, priv_key} = :crypto.generate_key(:eddsa, :ed25519)\n58\t      :persistent_term.put(:nebu_signing_key, {pub_key, priv_key})\n59\t    end\n60\t\n61\t    # enhanced-logging/operational-log-store (AC2): attaches the central\n62\t    # :logger handler that intercepts every Logger.* call app-wide. Safe to\n63\t    # call before the supervisor tree starts — the handler's own broadcast\n64\t    # (Nebu.PubSub, started as a child below) and persistence (Writer, also\n65\t    # below) paths each guard against their target process not being up yet.\n66\t    #\n67\t    # Gated behind :attach_log_store_handler (default true; test env sets it\n68\t    # false, mirroring start_jwks_cache/start_admin_endpoint/start_finch) so\n69\t    # `log_store_test.exs`'s own \"registers exactly one new handler\"\n70\t    # idempotency-count test (which must observe a handler_ids delta of\n71\t    # exactly +1 from ITS OWN call) is not pre-empted by a boot-time attach\n72\t    # that already happened before any test runs — the handler still gets\n73\t    # attached explicitly wherever a test/AC needs it (attach_handler/0 is\n74\t    # idempotent either way), and in prod/dev it is attached unconditionally\n75\t    # at boot per AC2.\n76\t    if Application.get_env(:nebu_http, :attach_log_store_handler, true) do\n77\t      :ok = Nebu.Web.LogStore.attach_handler()\n78\t    end\n79\t\n80\t    children =\n81\t      # Nebu.PubSub: the central :logger handler (attached above) broadcasts\n82\t      # EVERY Logger.* call here, regardless of whether DB persistence is\n83\t      # enabled (and regardless of whether Nebu.Repo is configured at all —\n84\t      # plain unit runs have no DB but still exercise attach_handler/0's\n85\t      # broadcast-only path, AC2/AC10). Started unconditionally (unlike\n86\t      # Nebu.Admin.PubSub, which is gated behind start_admin_endpoint) so it\n87\t      # is available in every environment, including a DB-less unit run.\n88\t      [Supervisor.child_spec({Phoenix.PubSub, name: Nebu.PubSub}, id: Nebu.PubSub)] ++\n89\t        repo_children() ++\n90\t        libcluster_children() ++\n91\t        [\n92\t          # Crash-prone stateful Matrix processes (Presence, Session, Horde rooms),\n93\t          # isolated under a high-intensity child supervisor so individual crashes\n94\t          # never cascade to the root supervisor.\n95\t          Nebu.StatefulSupervisor,\n96\t          # Cluster-aware, single-use, TTL-bounded store for the Matrix client SSO\n97\t          # flow (state + opaque loginToken). Owns an ETS table and replicates\n98\t          # writes/deletes across the cluster via :pg so the /sso/redirect,\n99\t          # callback, and POST /login hops can land on different nodes (ADR-020).\n100\t          Nebu.Matrix.SsoStore,\n101\t          # Node-local cache of server_config.oidc_user_id_claim (story\n102\t          # oidc-claim-boot-cache). Owns its :pg group membership for cross-node\n103\t          # invalidation; the cached value lives in persistent_term for an O(1)\n104\t          # hot-path read. Supervised so a crash repopulates lazily on next get/0.\n105\t          Nebu.Matrix.OidcClaimCache,\n106\t          # Nebu.Compliance (cross-cutting) — child supervisor reads :compliance, :workers\n107\t          Nebu.Compliance.Supervisor\n108\t        ] ++\n109\t        jwks_cache_children() ++\n110\t        media_children() ++\n111\t        admin_children() ++\n112\t        [\n113\t          # The single Bandit HTTP listener serves everything: Matrix API, media,\n114\t          # /admin, and /health + /info. (The gRPC network listener was removed —\n115\t          # ADR-021/grpc-legacy-removal; the Matrix HTTP layer calls\n116\t          # Nebu.Matrix.EventDispatcher.Server in-process. The former standalone\n117\t          # Nebu.Health.Server gen_tcp listener was removed too — it bound the same\n118\t          # port 4000 as Bandit and only ever worked by losing the race; /health and\n119\t          # /info are now Bandit routes.)\n120\t          {Bandit, plug: Nebu.Web.Router, port: http_port()}\n121\t        ]\n122\t\n123\t    opts = [strategy: :one_for_one, name: Nebu.Supervisor]\n124\t    Supervisor.start_link(children, opts)\n125\t  end\n126\t\n127\t  # Start the Repo only when its config is present (unit runs without NEBU_DB_URL\n128\t  # start zero DB children). Repo config lives under the unified :nebu app key.\n129\t  # S3: the auth-row sweeper (expired jwt_device_bindings + uia_sessions) is started\n130\t  # alongside the Repo — it has nothing to sweep without a DB, and gating it here keeps\n131\t  # the no-DB unit child count unchanged.\n132\t  # server-paced-session-refresh: RefreshWorker (proactive background renewal of\n133\t  # opaque matrix_sessions rows nearing their bound expires_at) is started the\n134\t  # same way — it has nothing to refresh without a DB either.\n135\t  # enhanced-logging/operational-log-store: Nebu.Web.LogStore.Writer (batches\n136\t  # operational_logs INSERTs) is started the same way — it has nothing to\n137\t  # flush to without a DB either. Supervised with the Supervisor default\n138\t  # (:permanent) restart strategy, same as every other child here, so a\n139\t  # crashed Writer is restarted under the same registered name (AC6).\n140\t  # Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache (refreshes the should_persist?/1 cache from\n141\t  # server_config) is started alongside it — nothing to cache without a DB\n142\t  # either.\n143\t  # enhanced-logging/war-room-mode (design-decisions.md #4):\n144\t  # Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom (caches the single active war room session +\n145\t  # owns the increment/timeout atomicity boundary) is started the same way,\n146\t  # right alongside Writer/PolicyCache — nothing to cache without a DB\n147\t  # either, and the same :permanent supervisor-restart contract applies (a\n148\t  # crashed WarRoom is restarted under the same registered name; its own\n149\t  # init/1 reloads the active session from the DB, immediately ending it if\n150\t  # it is already past its max_duration_s deadline — AC5 across a restart).\n151\t  # enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls: Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache\n152\t  # (caches which off-list endpoints have an active temporary capture, for\n153\t  # should_persist?/2's hot path) is started the same way — nothing to\n154\t  # cache without a DB either.\n155\t  defp repo_children do\n156\t    if Application.get_env(:nebu, Nebu.Repo),\n157\t      do: [\n158\t        Nebu.Repo,\n159\t        Nebu.Matrix.Session.Sweeper,\n160\t        Nebu.Matrix.Session.RefreshWorker,\n161\t        Nebu.Web.LogStore.Writer,\n162\t        Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache,\n163\t        Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom,\n164\t        Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache\n165\t      ],\n166\t      else: []\n167\t  end\n168\t\n169\t  # Story 13-6: libcluster Cluster.Supervisor only when topologies are configured\n170\t  # (prod/dev with CLUSTER_STRATEGY set). Test / single-node mode skip it.\n171\t  defp libcluster_children do\n172\t    case Application.get_env(:libcluster, :topologies) do\n173\t      nil -> []\n174\t      [] -> []\n175\t      topologies -> [{Cluster.Supervisor, [topologies, [name: Nebu.ClusterSupervisor]]}]\n176\t    end\n177\t  end\n178\t\n179\t  # JWKS cache (OIDC). Not started in test — jwks_cache_test starts its own instance\n180\t  # under the same registered name (config :nebu_http, :start_jwks_cache false).\n181\t  defp jwks_cache_children do\n182\t    if Application.get_env(:nebu_http, :start_jwks_cache, true) do\n183\t      [\n184\t        # oidc-env-optional-bootstrap: OidcConfig caches issuer/audience from\n185\t        # server_config so OidcPlug can read them without a per-request DB query.\n186\t        # Started before JwksCache so the config is warm before any key fetch.\n187\t        Nebu.Web.OidcConfig,\n188\t        {Nebu.Web.JwksCache, []}\n189\t      ]\n190\t    else\n191\t      []\n192\t    end\n193\t  end\n194\t\n195\t  # Finch pool for media preview_url fetching. Disabled in test (fake fetch boundary).\n196\t  defp media_children do\n197\t    if Application.get_env(:nebu_http, :start_finch, true) do\n198\t      [{Finch, name: Nebu.Media.Finch}]\n199\t    else\n200\t      []\n201\t    end\n202\t  end\n203\t\n204\t  # Phoenix LiveView Admin UI. Local PubSub (LiveView socket) + AdminEndpoint (server:\n205\t  # false; the main Bandit listener forwards /admin into it). Disabled in test so the\n206\t  # pure-unit children-count assertions are unaffected.\n207\t  defp admin_children do\n208\t    if Application.get_env(:nebu_http, :start_admin_endpoint, true) do\n209\t      # enhanced-logging/operational-log-store: gave this child an explicit\n210\t      # :id (default would be Phoenix.PubSub.Supervisor for every\n211\t      # {Phoenix.PubSub, ...} child, colliding with the always-started\n212\t      # Nebu.PubSub child above — Supervisor.start_link/2 rejects duplicate\n213\t      # child ids).\n214\t      [\n215\t        Supervisor.child_spec({Phoenix.PubSub, name: Nebu.Admin.PubSub}, id: Nebu.Admin.PubSub),\n216\t        Nebu.Admin.Endpoint\n217\t      ]\n218\t    else\n219\t      []\n220\t    end\n221\t  end\n222\t\n223\t  defp http_port, do: Application.get_env(:nebu_http, :port, 4000)\n224\tend\n225\t"}]},"uuid":"53b53d5c-830d-437c-99e4-54db7cbd2610","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:54:30.843Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/application.ex","content":"defmodule Nebu.Application do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  The single OTP application for Nebu (ADR-019).\n\n  All former umbrella apps (nebu_db, signature, permissions, presence, compliance,\n  session_manager, room_manager, event_dispatcher, nebu_http) are consolidated into\n  this one supervisor tree. Domains are module namespaces under `lib/nebu/`.\n\n  Boot-time invariants (created here, owned by the application master process so they\n  survive any supervised GenServer crash/restart):\n\n    * ETS tables `:NebuSessions`, `:NebuPresence`, `:NebuTxnDedup` — guarded, idempotent.\n    * `:pg` scope — started once.\n    * `:nebu_signing_key` persistent_term — server-level Ed25519 key (MVP).\n  \"\"\"\n  use Application\n\n  @impl true\n  def start(_type, _args) do\n    # ── Boot-time ETS tables (idempotent guard — survive same-VM restarts) ──\n    for {name, opts} <- [\n          {:NebuSessions, [:named_table, :set, :public]},\n          {:NebuPresence, [:named_table, :set, :public]},\n          {:NebuTxnDedup, [:named_table, :set, :public]}\n        ] do\n      if :ets.whereis(name) == :undefined, do: :ets.new(name, opts)\n    end\n\n    # ── Request-time ETS tables created eagerly so the lazy fast path never races\n    #    under concurrent first requests (security re-review R-2). ──\n    Nebu.Web.TokenDenylist.init_table()\n    Nebu.Web.Plugs.RateLimit.init_table()\n    Nebu.Matrix.AccountDataStore.init_table()\n\n    # enhanced-logging/matrix-payload-debug-logging (CI-gate cycle-2, global fix):\n    # `Nebu.Repo` runs with `log: false` (server/config/config.exs) — Ecto's own\n    # query logger prints bound SQL parameter values (which include credentials\n    # such as opaque tokens) verbatim at :debug level, so it is disabled globally.\n    # This attaches the sanitized replacement (source + duration only, never bound\n    # params) to Ecto's `[:nebu, :repo, :query]` telemetry event, which Ecto still\n    # emits unconditionally. Attaching is harmless when no Repo is configured (no\n    # DB in pure unit runs) — the event simply never fires.\n    Nebu.Repo.QueryTelemetry.attach()\n\n    # ── :pg scope for room/presence process-group broadcast (ADR-002/005). ──\n    case :pg.start_link() do\n      {:ok, _pid} -> :ok\n      {:error, {:already_started, _pid}} -> :ok\n    end\n\n    # ── Server-level Ed25519 signing key for MVP event signing (room_manager). ──\n    # WARNING (MVP): regenerated on every OS/VM restart — events signed before a\n    # restart are not verifiable with the new key. Phase 2 persists per-user keys to\n    # the DB. Guarded so a same-VM re-entry (hot reload / test re-invocation of\n    # start/2) does NOT swap the key out from under running signing processes.\n    if :persistent_term.get(:nebu_signing_key, nil) == nil do\n      {pub_key, priv_key} = :crypto.generate_key(:eddsa, :ed25519)\n      :persistent_term.put(:nebu_signing_key, {pub_key, priv_key})\n    end\n\n    # enhanced-logging/operational-log-store (AC2): attaches the central\n    # :logger handler that intercepts every Logger.* call app-wide. Safe to\n    # call before the supervisor tree starts — the handler's own broadcast\n    # (Nebu.PubSub, started as a child below) and persistence (Writer, also\n    # below) paths each guard against their target process not being up yet.\n    #\n    # Gated behind :attach_log_store_handler (default true; test env sets it\n    # false, mirroring start_jwks_cache/start_admin_endpoint/start_finch) so\n    # `log_store_test.exs`'s own \"registers exactly one new handler\"\n    # idempotency-count test (which must observe a handler_ids delta of\n    # exactly +1 from ITS OWN call) is not pre-empted by a boot-time attach\n    # that already happened before any test runs — the handler still gets\n    # attached explicitly wherever a test/AC needs it (attach_handler/0 is\n    # idempotent either way), and in prod/dev it is attached unconditionally\n    # at boot per AC2.\n    if Application.get_env(:nebu_http, :attach_log_store_handler, true) do\n      :ok = Nebu.Web.LogStore.attach_handler()\n    end\n\n    children =\n      # Nebu.PubSub: the central :logger handler (attached above) broadcasts\n      # EVERY Logger.* call here, regardless of whether DB persistence is\n      # enabled (and regardless of whether Nebu.Repo is configured at all —\n      # plain unit runs have no DB but still exercise attach_handler/0's\n      # broadcast-only path, AC2/AC10). Started unconditionally (unlike\n      # Nebu.Admin.PubSub, which is gated behind start_admin_endpoint) so it\n      # is available in every environment, including a DB-less unit run.\n      [Supervisor.child_spec({Phoenix.PubSub, name: Nebu.PubSub}, id: Nebu.PubSub)] ++\n        repo_children() ++\n        libcluster_children() ++\n        [\n          # Crash-prone stateful Matrix processes (Presence, Session, Horde rooms),\n          # isolated under a high-intensity child supervisor so individual crashes\n          # never cascade to the root supervisor.\n          Nebu.StatefulSupervisor,\n          # Cluster-aware, single-use, TTL-bounded store for the Matrix client SSO\n          # flow (state + opaque loginToken). Owns an ETS table and replicates\n          # writes/deletes across the cluster via :pg so the /sso/redirect,\n          # callback, and POST /login hops can land on different nodes (ADR-020).\n          Nebu.Matrix.SsoStore,\n          # Node-local cache of server_config.oidc_user_id_claim (story\n          # oidc-claim-boot-cache). Owns its :pg group membership for cross-node\n          # invalidation; the cached value lives in persistent_term for an O(1)\n          # hot-path read. Supervised so a crash repopulates lazily on next get/0.\n          Nebu.Matrix.OidcClaimCache,\n          # Nebu.Compliance (cross-cutting) — child supervisor reads :compliance, :workers\n          Nebu.Compliance.Supervisor\n        ] ++\n        jwks_cache_children() ++\n        media_children() ++\n        admin_children() ++\n        [\n          # The single Bandit HTTP listener serves everything: Matrix API, media,\n          # /admin, and /health + /info. (The gRPC network listener was removed —\n          # ADR-021/grpc-legacy-removal; the Matrix HTTP layer calls\n          # Nebu.Matrix.EventDispatcher.Server in-process. The former standalone\n          # Nebu.Health.Server gen_tcp listener was removed too — it bound the same\n          # port 4000 as Bandit and only ever worked by losing the race; /health and\n          # /info are now Bandit routes.)\n          {Bandit, plug: Nebu.Web.Router, port: http_port()}\n        ]\n\n    opts = [strategy: :one_for_one, name: Nebu.Supervisor]\n    Supervisor.start_link(children, opts)\n  end\n\n  # Start the Repo only when its config is present (unit runs without NEBU_DB_URL\n  # start zero DB children). Repo config lives under the unified :nebu app key.\n  # S3: the auth-row sweeper (expired jwt_device_bindings + uia_sessions) is started\n  # alongside the Repo — it has nothing to sweep without a DB, and gating it here keeps\n  # the no-DB unit child count unchanged.\n  # server-paced-session-refresh: RefreshWorker (proactive background renewal of\n  # opaque matrix_sessions rows nearing their bound expires_at) is started the\n  # same way — it has nothing to refresh without a DB either.\n  # enhanced-logging/operational-log-store: Nebu.Web.LogStore.Writer (batches\n  # operational_logs INSERTs) is started the same way — it has nothing to\n  # flush to without a DB either. Supervised with the Supervisor default\n  # (:permanent) restart strategy, same as every other child here, so a\n  # crashed Writer is restarted under the same registered name (AC6).\n  # Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache (refreshes the should_persist?/1 cache from\n  # server_config) is started alongside it — nothing to cache without a DB\n  # either.\n  # enhanced-logging/war-room-mode (design-decisions.md #4):\n  # Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom (caches the single active war room session +\n  # owns the increment/timeout atomicity boundary) is started the same way,\n  # right alongside Writer/PolicyCache — nothing to cache without a DB\n  # either, and the same :permanent supervisor-restart contract applies (a\n  # crashed WarRoom is restarted under the same registered name; its own\n  # init/1 reloads the active session from the DB, immediately ending it if\n  # it is already past its max_duration_s deadline — AC5 across a restart).\n  # enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls: Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache\n  # (caches which off-list endpoints have an active temporary capture, for\n  # should_persist?/2's hot path) is started the same way — nothing to\n  # cache without a DB either.\n  defp repo_children do\n    if Application.get_env(:nebu, Nebu.Repo),\n      do: [\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        Nebu.Matrix.Session.Sweeper,\n        Nebu.Matrix.Session.RefreshWorker,\n        Nebu.Web.LogStore.Writer,\n        Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache,\n        Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom,\n        Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache\n      ],\n      else: []\n  end\n\n  # Story 13-6: libcluster Cluster.Supervisor only when topologies are configured\n  # (prod/dev with CLUSTER_STRATEGY set). Test / single-node mode skip it.\n  defp libcluster_children do\n    case Application.get_env(:libcluster, :topologies) do\n      nil -> []\n      [] -> []\n      topologies -> [{Cluster.Supervisor, [topologies, [name: Nebu.ClusterSupervisor]]}]\n    end\n  end\n\n  # JWKS cache (OIDC). Not started in test — jwks_cache_test starts its own instance\n  # under the same registered name (config :nebu_http, :start_jwks_cache false).\n  defp jwks_cache_children do\n    if Application.get_env(:nebu_http, :start_jwks_cache, true) do\n      [\n        # oidc-env-optional-bootstrap: OidcConfig caches issuer/audience from\n        # server_config so OidcPlug can read them without a per-request DB query.\n        # Started before JwksCache so the config is warm before any key fetch.\n        Nebu.Web.OidcConfig,\n        {Nebu.Web.JwksCache, []}\n      ]\n    else\n      []\n    end\n  end\n\n  # Finch pool for media preview_url fetching. Disabled in test (fake fetch boundary).\n  defp media_children do\n    if Application.get_env(:nebu_http, :start_finch, true) do\n      [{Finch, name: Nebu.Media.Finch}]\n    else\n      []\n    end\n  end\n\n  # Phoenix LiveView Admin UI. Local PubSub (LiveView socket) + AdminEndpoint (server:\n  # false; the main Bandit listener forwards /admin into it). Disabled in test so the\n  # pure-unit children-count assertions are unaffected.\n  defp admin_children do\n    if Application.get_env(:nebu_http, :start_admin_endpoint, true) do\n      # enhanced-logging/operational-log-store: gave this child an explicit\n      # :id (default would be Phoenix.PubSub.Supervisor for every\n      # {Phoenix.PubSub, ...} child, colliding with the always-started\n      # Nebu.PubSub child above — Supervisor.start_link/2 rejects duplicate\n      # child ids).\n      [\n        Supervisor.child_spec({Phoenix.PubSub, name: Nebu.Admin.PubSub}, id: Nebu.Admin.PubSub),\n        Nebu.Admin.Endpoint\n      ]\n    else\n      []\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp http_port, do: Application.get_env(:nebu_http, :port, 4000)\nend\n","numLines":225,"startLine":1,"totalLines":225}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"dcbd24cc-c002-4fff-9f1e-098f927699a9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

Do NOT answer from this page alone if the answer may be further in the file.]</system-reminder>\n\n1\t// admin-ui-migration: LiveView client bootstrap for the Nebu Admin UI.\n2\t//\n3\t// No npm/esbuild build step. We load the vendored UMD builds of phoenix and\n4\t// phoenix_live_view (copied from deps/ at build time) which expose the `Phoenix`\n5\t// and `LiveView` globals, then wire a LiveSocket on /admin/live. The CSRF token is\n6\t// read from the <meta name=\"csrf-token\"> emitted by the root layout.\n7\t//\n8\t// These three <script> tags are emitted by NebuHttp.Admin.Layouts.root/1:\n9\t//   /admin/static/assets/phoenix.min.js          → window.Phoenix\n10\t//   /admin/static/assets/phoenix_live_view.min.js → window.LiveView\n11\t//   /admin/static/assets/app.js                   → this file (defer)\n12\t(function () {\n13\t  \"use strict\";\n14\t\n15\t  // device-revoke-confirm: CSP-compliant confirmation for destructive admin actions.\n16\t  //\n17\t  // The admin CSP is `script-src 'self'` with NO 'unsafe-inline', so inline\n18\t  // `onsubmit=\"return confirm(...)\"` handlers are blocked by the browser and never\n19\t  // fire. The per-user device page (a dead controller render — no LiveView socket)\n20\t  // therefore cannot rely on either an inline handler OR Phoenix `data-confirm`.\n21\t  //\n22\t  // This single delegated `submit` listener (loaded from this external file, which IS\n23\t  // allowed by `script-src 'self'`) intercepts any `[data-confirm]` form submit and\n24\t  // requires the operator to confirm before the destructive POST proceeds. It fires for\n25\t  // BOTH click-to-submit and keyboard (Enter) submit, so it is keyboard-operable, and\n26\t  // it works with or without LiveView present — hence it is wired BEFORE the LiveView\n27\t  // bootstrap guard below.\n28\t  function wireConfirmForms() {\n29\t    document.addEventListener(\n30\t      \"submit\",\n31\t      function (event) {\n32\t        var form = event.target;\n33\t        if (!form || typeof form.matches !== \"function\") return;\n34\t        if (!form.matches(\"form[data-confirm]\")) return;\n35\t        var message = form.getAttribute(\"data-confirm\");\n36\t        if (!message) return;\n37\t        if (!window.confirm(message)) {\n38\t          event.preventDefault();\n39\t        }\n40\t      },\n41\t      // Capture phase so the confirmation runs before any other submit handler.\n42\t      true,\n43\t    );\n44\t  }\n45\t\n46\t  // admin-sidebar-nesting: expand/collapse for the nested sidebar nav sections\n47\t  // (\"Logs\", \"Configuration\" — Nebu.Admin.Layouts.nav_item/1 parent mode).\n48\t  //\n49\t  // The admin CSP is `script-src 'self'` (no 'unsafe-inline'), and the sidebar\n50\t  // renders on BOTH dead controller pages (no LiveView socket — a `phx-click`\n51\t  // would never fire) and LiveView pages, so the toggle lives here as a single\n52\t  // DELEGATED document-level click listener (survives LiveView DOM patches;\n53\t  // nothing to re-wire after morphdom). A native <button> fires `click` for\n54\t  // mouse, Space, and Enter alike, so this one listener is fully\n55\t  // keyboard-operable (story AC9).\n56\t  //\n57\t  // Toggling flips three things the server rendered (expanded by default —\n58\t  // story Dev Notes Q3; collapse state intentionally does NOT persist across\n59\t  // navigations, Q1):\n60\t  //   - aria-expanded on the <button data-nav-toggle> (screen readers)\n61\t  //   - the boolean `hidden` attribute on the aria-controls'd child <ul>\n62\t  //     (children stay in the DOM — AC10; the <ul> carries no display utility\n63\t  //     class, so the UA's `[hidden] { display: none }` actually applies —\n64\t  //     see the trace-map-hierarchy cycle-1 flex-beats-hidden bug)\n65\t  //   - the chevron's `rotate-90` class (visual open/closed state — AC13)\n66\t  function wireNavSectionToggles() {\n67\t    document.addEventListener(\"click\", function (event) {\n68\t      var target = event.target;\n69\t      if (!target || typeof target.closest !== \"function\") return;\n70\t      var button = target.closest(\"button[data-nav-toggle]\");\n71\t      if (!button) return;\n72\t\n73\t      var nowExpanded = button.getAttribute(\"aria-expanded\") !== \"true\";\n74\t      button.setAttribute(\"aria-expanded\", nowExpanded ? \"true\" : \"false\");\n75\t\n76\t      var listId = button.getAttribute(\"aria-controls\");\n77\t      var list = listId ? document.getElementById(listId) : null;\n78\t      if (list) {\n79\t        if (nowExpanded) {\n80\t          list.removeAttribute(\"hidden\");\n81\t        } else {\n82\t          list.setAttribute(\"hidden\", \"hidden\");\n83\t        }\n84\t      }\n85\t\n86\t      var icon = button.querySelector('[data-testid=\"nav-toggle-icon\"]');\n87\t      if (icon) icon.classList.toggle(\"rotate-90\", nowExpanded);\n88\t    });\n89\t  }\n90\t\n91\t  // admin-add-user-autocomplete: CSP-safe fetch typeahead for the room/space add-member\n92\t  // field. The admin CSP is `script-src 'self'` (no 'unsafe-inline') but same-origin\n93\t  // fetch IS allowed, and these pages are dead controller renders (no LiveView socket).\n94\t  // This widget therefore lives entirely in this external file:\n95\t  //   - debounced GET /admin/users/search?q=… (>=2 chars; below that it clears)\n96\t  //   - renders <ul role=\"listbox\"> <li role=\"option\" data-role=\"user-suggestion\"\n97\t  //     data-user-id=\"…\"> options; user_id is set via textContent (XSS-safe — never\n98\t  //     innerHTML), so a crafted display value cannot inject markup\n99\t  //   - click / Enter / ArrowUp / ArrowDown / Escape are keyboard-operable\n100\t  //   - selecting an option fills the input and closes the listbox (free-text submit\n101\t  //     remains unchanged when no option is picked)\n102\t  var AUTOCOMPLETE_MIN_CHARS = 2;\n103\t  var AUTOCOMPLETE_DEBOUNCE_MS = 150;\n104\t\n105\t  function wireUserAutocomplete() {\n106\t    var inputs = document.querySelectorAll('[data-field=\"add-member-user-id\"][role=\"combobox\"]');\n107\t    Array.prototype.forEach.call(inputs, function (input) {\n108\t      if (input.getAttribute(\"data-autocomplete-wired\") === \"true\") return;\n109\t      input.setAttribute(\"data-autocomplete-wired\", \"true\");\n110\t      setupAutocomplete(input);\n111\t    });\n112\t  }\n113\t\n114\t  function setupAutocomplete(input) {\n115\t    var container =\n116\t      input.parentElement &&\n117\t      input.parentElement.querySelector('[data-role=\"user-suggestions\"]');\n118\t    if (!container) return;\n119\t\n120\t    var listboxId = container.getAttribute(\"data-listbox-id\") || \"user-suggestions-listbox\";\n121\t    var debounceTimer = null;\n122\t    var requestSeq = 0;\n123\t    var activeIndex = -1;\n124\t    var currentList = null;\n125\t\n126\t    function clearList() {\n127\t      container.textContent = \"\";\n128\t      currentList = null;\n129\t      activeIndex = -1;\n130\t      input.setAttribute(\"aria-expanded\", \"false\");\n131\t      input.removeAttribute(\"aria-activedescendant\");\n132\t    }\n133\t\n134\t    function options() {\n135\t      return currentList\n136\t        ? Array.prototype.slice.call(currentList.querySelectorAll('[role=\"option\"]'))\n137\t        : [];\n138\t    }\n139\t\n140\t    function highlight(idx) {\n141\t      var opts = options();\n142\t      opts.forEach(function (o, i) {\n143\t        if (i === idx) {\n144\t          o.setAttribute(\"aria-selected\", \"true\");\n145\t          o.classList.add(\"bg-base-300\");\n146\t          o.scrollIntoView({ block: \"nearest\" });\n147\t        } else {\n148\t          o.setAttribute(\"aria-selected\", \"false\");\n149\t          o.classList.remove(\"bg-base-300\");\n150\t        }\n151\t      });\n152\t      activeIndex = idx;\n153\t      if (idx >= 0 && opts[idx]) {\n154\t        input.setAttribute(\"aria-activedescendant\", opts[idx].id);\n155\t      } else {\n156\t        input.removeAttribute(\"aria-activedescendant\");\n157\t      }\n158\t    }\n159\t\n160\t    function select(userId) {\n161\t      input.value = userId;\n162\t      clearList();\n163\t      input.focus();\n164\t    }\n165\t\n166\t    function render(users, query) {\n167\t      container.textContent = \"\";\n168\t      activeIndex = -1;\n169\t\n170\t      var ul = document.createElement(\"ul\");\n171\t      ul.id = listboxId;\n172\t      ul.setAttribute(\"role\", \"listbox\");\n173\t      ul.className =\n174\t        \"menu bg-base-100 border border-base-300 rounded-box shadow w-full max-h-60 overflow-auto\";\n175\t\n176\t      if (users.length === 0) {\n177\t        // Empty-state marker — the query was long enough but matched nobody. Rendered\n178\t        // so the (server-confirmed) no-match state is observable, not a race.\n179\t        var empty = document.createElement(\"li\");\n180\t        empty.setAttribute(\"data-role\", \"no-user-suggestions\");\n181\t        empty.className = \"px-3 py-2 text-base-content/60 text-sm\";\n182\t        empty.textContent = \"No matching users\";\n183\t        ul.appendChild(empty);\n184\t      } else {\n185\t        users.forEach(function (u, i) {\n186\t          var li = document.createElement(\"li\");\n187\t          li.id = listboxId + \"-opt-\" + i;\n188\t          li.setAttribute(\"role\", \"option\");\n189\t          li.setAttribute(\"data-role\", \"user-suggestion\");\n190\t          li.setAttribute(\"data-user-id\", u.user_id);\n191\t          li.setAttribute(\"aria-selected\", \"false\");\n192\t          li.className = \"px-3 py-2 cursor-pointer hover:bg-base-200 font-mono text-sm\";\n193\t          // textContent only — never innerHTML. A crafted user_id renders as inert text.\n194\t          li.textContent = u.user_id;\n195\t          li.addEventListener(\"mousedown\", function (e) {\n196\t            // mousedown (not click) so the input's blur does not close the listbox first.\n197\t            e.preventDefault();\n198\t            select(u.user_id);\n199\t          });\n200\t          ul.appendChild(li);\n201\t        });\n202\t      }\n203\t\n204\t      container.appendChild(ul);\n205\t      currentList = ul;\n206\t      input.setAttribute(\"aria-expanded\", \"true\");\n207\t    }\n208\t\n209\t    function fetchSuggestions(query) {\n210\t      var seq = ++requestSeq;\n211\t      // No custom Accept header: the admin :browser pipeline negotiates `html` from the\n212\t      // default `*/*`; the controller's json/2 sets application/json on the response.\n213\t      fetch(\n214\t        \"/admin/users/search?q=\" + encodeURIComponent(query),\n215\t        { credentials: \"same-origin\" },\n216\t      )\n217\t        .then(function (resp) {\n218\t          return resp.ok ? resp.json() : { users: [] };\n219\t        })\n220\t        .then(function (data) {\n221\t          if (seq !== (seq !== requestSeq) return;\n226\t          clearList();\n227\t        });\n228\t    }\n229\t\n230\t    input.addEventListener(\"input\", function () {\n231\t      var query = input.value.trim();\n232\t      if (debounceTimer) clearTimeout(debounceTimer);\n233\t      if (query.length < AUTOCOMPLETE_MIN_CHARS) {\n234\t        clearList();\n235\t        return;\n236\t      }\n237\t      debounceTimer = setTimeout(function () {\n238\t        fetchSuggestions(query);\n239\t      }, AUTOCOMPLETE_DEBOUNCE_MS);\n240\t    });\n241\t\n242\t    input.addEventListener(\"keydown\", function (e) {\n243\t      var opts = options();\n244\t      if (e.key === \"ArrowDown\") {\n245\t        if (opts.length === 0) return;\n246\t        e.preventDefault();\n247\t        highlight((activeIndex + 1) % opts.length);\n248\t      } else if (e.key === \"ArrowUp\") {\n249\t        if (opts.length === 0) return;\n250\t        e.preventDefault();\n251\t        highlight((activeIndex - 1 + opts.length) % opts.length);\n252\t      } else if (e.key === \"Enter\") {\n253\t        if (activeIndex >= 0 && opts[activeIndex]) {\n254\t          e.preventDefault();\n255\t          select(opts[activeIndex].getAttribute(\"data-user-id\"));\n256\t        }\n257\t        // else: let Enter submit the form (free-text path unchanged)\n258\t      } else if (e.key === \"Escape\") {\n259\t        if (currentList) {\n260\t          e.preventDefault();\n261\t          clearList();\n262\t        }\n263\t      }\n264\t    });\n265\t\n266\t    // Close the listbox when focus leaves the widget (delayed so option mousedown wins).\n267\t    input.addEventListener(\"blur\", function () {\n268\t      setTimeout(clearList, 100);\n269\t    });\n270\t  }\n271\t\n272\t  // enhanced-logging/admin-logs-monitoring-view (AC1/AC3/AC4): the Trace Map's\n273\t  // filter-toggle bubbles are CLIENT-SIDE ONLY (no server round-trip per click —\n274\t  // AC3). The admin CSP (`script-src 'self'`, no `unsafe-inline`) rules out an\n275\t  // inline `<script>` tag in the LogsLive template, so — exactly like\n276\t  // `wireConfirmForms`/`wireUserAutocomplete` above — this lives here instead.\n277\t  //\n278\t  // Each bubble's `phx-click` is bound to `Phoenix.LiveView.JS.dispatch(...)`\n279\t  // (server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live.ex), which fires a plain, bubbling\n280\t  // CustomEvent on the clicked element and does NOT push anything to the\n281\t  // LiveView server. This delegated listener on `document` toggles the\n282\t  // bubble's own `aria-pressed` attribute, then recomputes which\n283\t  // `[data-testid=\"request-row\"]` rows and `[data-testid=\"filter-chip\"]` chips\n284\t  // should be visible — AND logic across dimensions (endpoint/level/source),\n285\t  // OR logic for multiple active values within the SAME dimension.\n286\t  function activeTraceMapFilters() {\n287\t    var filters = {};\n288\t    var bubbles = document.querySelectorAll(\n289\t      '[data-testid=\"trace-map-bubble\"][aria-pressed=\"true\"]',\n290\t    );\n291\t    Array.prototype.forEach.call(bubbles, function (bubble) {\n292\t      var dimension = bubble.dataset.bubbleDimension;\n293\t      var value = bubble.dataset.bubbleValue;\n294\t      if (!dimension) return;\n295\t      filters[dimension] = filters[dimension] || [];\n296\t      filters[dimension].push(value);\n297\t    });\n298\t    return filters;\n299\t  }\n300\t\n301\t  function traceMapRowMatches(row, filters) {\n302\t    for (var dimension in filters) {\n303\t      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(filters, dimension)) continue;\n304\t      var activeValues = filters[dimension];\n305\t      if (!activeValues || activeValues.length === 0) continue;\n306\t\n307\t      var rowValues;\n308\t      if (dimension === \"endpoint\") {\n309\t        rowValues = [row.dataset.endpoint || \"\"];\n310\t      } else if (dimension === \"level\") {\n311\t        rowValues = [row.dataset.highestLevel || \"\"];\n312\t      } else if (dimension === \"source\") {\n313\t        rowValues = (row.dataset.sources || \"\").split(\" \");\n314\t      } else {\n315\t        rowValues = [];\n316\t      }\n317\t\n318\t      var matchesThisDimension = activeValues.some(function (v) {\n319\t        return rowValues.indexOf(v) !== -1;\n320\t      });\n321\t      if (!matchesThisDimension) return false;\n322\t    }\n323\t    return true;\n324\t  }\n325\t\n326\t  function applyTraceMapFilters() {\n327\t    var filters = activeTraceMapFilters();\n328\t\n329\t    var rows = document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid=\"request-row\"]');\n330\t    Array.prototype.forEach.call(rows, function (row) {\n331\t      row.style.display = traceMapRowMatches(row, filters) ? \"\" : \"none\";\n332\t    });\n333\t\n334\t    var chips = document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid=\"filter-chip\"]');\n335\t    Array.prototype.forEach.call(chips, function (chip) {\n336\t      var dimension = chip.dataset.chipDimension;\n337\t      var value = chip.dataset.chipValue;\n338\t      var isActive = !!(filters[dimension] && filters[dimension].indexOf(value) !== -1);\n339\t      chip.style.display = isActive ? \"\" : \"none\";\n340\t    });\n341\t  }\n342\t\n343\t  // V2 fix (UX/accessibility gate, BLOCKING): the bubble's active/filled\n344\t  // visual state (AC1: \"Clicking a bubble activates it (filled/highlighted),\n345\t  // clicking again deactivates (dimmed/outline)\") was never actually applied\n346\t  // client-side — only `aria-pressed` toggled, not the `btn-active`/\n347\t  // `btn-outline` classes `trace_map_bubble.ex`'s own `class` computation\n348\t  // defines server-side for `active: true`/`active: false`. This mirrors\n349\t  // that EXACT class logic (`server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_bubble.ex`):\n350\t  //   active && not off  -> \"btn-active\" (and NOT \"btn-outline\")\n351\t  //   not active || off  -> \"btn-outline\" (and NOT \"btn-active\")\n352\t  // \"off\" bubbles never reach here with `pressed: true` (the click handler\n353\t  // below returns early for them), so they always stay in the \"btn-outline\"\n354\t  // branch, matching the server-rendered default.\n355\t  function setBubbleActiveClasses(bubble, pressed) {\n356\t    if (pressed) {\n357\t      bubble.classList.add(\"btn-active\");\n358\t      bubble.classList.remove(\"btn-outline\");\n359\t    } else {\n360\t      bubble.classList.remove(\"btn-active\");\n361\t      bubble.classList.add(\"btn-outline\");\n362\t    }\n363\t  }\n364\t\n365\t  // Bug fix (trace-map-filter-reset-bugfix, AC3): reflects the CURRENT\n366\t  // aria-pressed state of every Trace Map bubble into the page's own URL\n367\t  // query string (`?endpoint=...&level=...&source=...`), via\n368\t  // `history.pushState` — entirely client-side, no LiveView round trip\n369\t  // (keeps AC2's \"no perceptible click lag\" property; see `bubble_js/2`'s\n370\t  // own comment in `logs_live.ex` for why this could not be done via\n371\t  // `Phoenix.LiveView.JS.patch/2` + `handle_params/3` instead).\n372\t  //\n373\t  // dev-story cycle 1, code-review MAJOR finding M1: ALL active values per\n374\t  // dimension are encoded now, comma-joined (e.g. `?level=error,warning`),\n375\t  // not just the first one — `logs_live.ex`'s `@active_filters` now\n376\t  // mirrors this file's own OR-within-a-dimension semantics\n377\t  // (`activeTraceMapFilters`/`traceMapRowMatches`, already multi-select\n378\t  // since the original admin-logs-monitoring-view story) exactly, list for\n379\t  // list, instead of collapsing to a single scalar. Comma-joining (not\n380\t  // repeated bare params like `?level=error&level=warning`) was chosen\n381\t  // because `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1` — the server's own query-string\n382\t  // parser — documents \"if a value is given more than once, the LAST\n383\t  // value takes precedence\"; a repeated bare param would silently DROP\n384\t  // every value but the last one on the server side, defeating the whole\n385\t  // point of this fix.\n386\t  function syncTraceMapUrlFromBubbles() {\n387\t    // V8 fix (real-browser verification): the entry the page ORIGINALLY\n388\t    // loaded on carries a history `state` object LiveView itself seeded\n389\t    // BEFORE our very first click (shape: `{type: \"patch\", id:\n390\t    // <this-view's-own-phx-id>, position: 0}` — see\n391\t    // `phoenix_live_view.cjs.js`'s `historyPatch`/initial state setup).\n392\t    // Navigating \"back\" to THAT exact entry later makes LiveView's own\n393\t    // \"popstate\" listener see `navType === \"patch\" && id === this.main.id`\n394\t    // and attempt `this.main.pushLinkPatch(...)` — a REAL server round trip\n395\t    // requiring `handle_params/3`, the exact mechanism this fix cannot use\n396\t    // (see `bubble_js/2`'s comment in `logs_live.ex`) — which silently does\n397\t    // nothing observable (no error, no re-render) rather than falling back\n398\t    // to a full re-mount. Neutralizing that ONE, pre-existing entry's state\n399\t    // (replacing it with a plain marker, once, before our first push) makes\n400\t    // a later \"back\" to it fall through to LiveView's OTHER, DOES-work\n401\t    // branch (`replaceMain`, a full disconnect+remount that correctly reads\n402\t    // the target URL's query params fresh — exactly `mount/3`'s own\n403\t    // params-driven `@active_filters` seeding this story's fix already\n404\t    // relies on for the \"shareable URL\" case).\n405\t    if (!window.__nebuHistoryNeutralized) {\n406\t      window.__nebuHistoryNeutralized = true;\n407\t      window.history.replaceState(\n408\t        { nebuTraceMapFilters: true },\n409\t        \"\",\n410\t        window.location.pathname + window.location.search + window.location.hash,\n411\t      );\n412\t    }\n413\t\n414\t    var filters = activeTraceMapFilters();\n415\t    var parts = [];\n416\t    [\"endpoint\", \"level\", \"source\"].forEach(function (dimension) {\n417\t      var values = filters[dimension];\n418\t      if (values && values.length > 0) {\n419\t        var encoded = values.map(encodeURIComponent).join(\",\");\n420\t        parts.push(dimension + \"=\" + encoded);\n421\t      }\n422\t    });\n423\t\n424\t    var query = parts.length > 0 ? \"?\" + parts.join(\"&\") : \"\";\n425\t    var newUrl = window.location.pathname + query + window.location.hash;\n426\t    if (newUrl !== window.location.pathname + window.location.search + window.location.hash) {\n427\t      window.history.pushState({ nebuTraceMapFilters: true }, \"\", newUrl);\n428\t      syncLiveSocketHref(newUrl);\n429\t    }\n430\t  }\n431\t\n432\t  // V7 fix (real-browser verification against the actual running dev stack —\n433\t  // caught a SECOND, distinct variant of the original bug, not covered by\n434\t  // any unit test): this admin UI's LiveSocket is configured with a FORCED\n435\t  // `ThrottledLongPoll` transport (see this file's own `boot()`, `new\n436\t  // LiveSocket(\"/admin/live\", Socket, { transport: ThrottledLongPoll, ... })`)\n437\t  // — NOT a WebSocket-with-longpoll-fallback. Long-polling periodically\n438\t  // re-joins its underlying Phoenix Channel (confirmed empirically: a fresh\n439\t  // `mount:` diff appears in the browser console a few seconds after every\n440\t  // connect, with no user action). Phoenix.LiveView's OWN channel-join\n441\t  // payload builder reads `view.href` (cached on the View instance, NOT\n442\t  // re-read from `window.location.href` on every join — see\n443\t  // `deps/phoenix_live_view/priv/static/phoenix_live_view.cjs.js`'s `View`\n444\t  // class: `this.channel = this.liveSocket.channel(topic, () => ({ url:\n445\t  // this.href && this.expandURL(this.href), ... }))`). That cached `href` is\n446\t  // set ONCE at initial page load (`view.setHref(this.getHref())`) and is\n447\t  // ONLY ever updated again through LiveView's OWN internal push-patch flow\n448\t  // (`commitPendingLink`) — which requires a real `handle_params/3` server\n449\t  // round trip, the exact mechanism this story's Dev Notes evaluated FIRST\n450\t  // and rejected (see `bubble_js/2`'s comment in `logs_live.ex`). A bare\n451\t    // `history.pushState` (this file's `syncTraceMapUrlFromBubbles`, above)\n452\t  // updates the BROWSER's address bar just fine, but never touches this\n453\t  // cached `view.href` — so the NEXT long-poll rejoin sent the STALE,\n454\t  // pre-click URL as its join `url:` param, `mount/3` re-derived an EMPTY\n455\t  // `@active_filters` from it, and the filter silently reset — a NEW\n456\t  // variant of the EXACT bug this story exists to fix, invisible to\n457\t  // `Phoenix.LiveViewTest` (no real transport, no rejoin) and only caught by\n458\t  // testing against the real, running dev stack as instructed.\n459\t  //\n460\t  // Fix: directly keep the LiveSocket's and the main View's own internal\n461\t  // `href` caches in sync with our own URL updates. `window.liveSocket` is\n462\t  // already exposed globally by this file's `boot()`. These are marked\n463\t  // `@internal` in Phoenix LiveView's own source (no public API exists for\n464\t  // \"tell the client the URL changed without a server round trip\") — a\n465\t  // deliberate, narrowly-scoped, defensively-guarded use of an internal\n466\t  // field, chosen after confirming (via the real dependency's source, not\n467\t  // guesswork) that no supported alternative achieves a purely client-side\n468\t  // URL sync.\n469\t  function syncLiveSocketHref(newUrl) {\n470\t    var socket = window.liveSocket;\n471\t    if (!socket) return;\n472\t\n473\t    var absoluteHref = window.location.origin + newUrl;\n474\t    if (typeof socket.href === \"string\") {\n475\t      socket.href = absoluteHref;\n476\t    }\n477\t    if (socket.main && typeof socket.main.setHref === \"function\") {\n478\t      socket.main.setHref(absoluteHref);\n479\t    }\n480\t\n481\t    // V8 fix (real-browser verification): `LiveSocket` ALSO caches\n482\t    // `currentLocation` (separately from `href`, above) — its OWN \"popstate\"\n483\t    // listener (bound once, at boot, alongside ours) short-circuits as a\n484\t    // total no-op whenever `registerNewLocation(window.location)` reports\n485\t    // \"unchanged\" by comparing `window.location` against THIS cached value.\n486\t    // Since our raw `pushState` never touches it, `currentLocation` stayed\n487\t    // frozen at whatever the page's TRUE first load was — so navigating\n488\t    // \"back\" to that exact original URL (a very common case: click once,\n489\t    // then go back) was wrongly seen as \"the URL didn't change\", and\n490\t    // LiveView's own fallback re-mount (`replaceMain`, its documented\n491\t    // answer for a non-\"patch\" popstate) never ran at all, leaving the\n492\t    // stale, still-pressed bubble on screen. Calling the same\n493\t    // `registerNewLocation` method LiveView's own code calls after every\n494\t    // one of ITS OWN navigations keeps this cache honest too.\n495\t    if (typeof socket.registerNewLocation === \"function\") {\n496\t      socket.registerNewLocation(window.location);\n497\t    }\n498\t  }\n499\t\n500\t  // Companion to `syncTraceMapUrlFromBubbles` — re-derives EVERY bubble's\n501\t  // aria-pressed/class state FROM the page's current URL query string\n502\t  // (AC3's browser back/forward requirement). Used both by the\n503\t  // `popstate` listener below (back/forward navigation) and safe to call\n504\t  // at any time since it fully recomputes state rather than toggling.\n505\t  //\n506\t  // dev-story cycle 1, code-review MAJOR finding M1: a dimension's query\n507\t  // param is a comma-joined list of values (see `syncTraceMapUrlFromBubbles`\n508\t  // above) — a bubble is pressed if its OWN value appears ANYWHERE in that\n509\t  // list, not only if it equals the whole (previously single-valued)\n510\t  // param string.\n511\t  function applyTraceMapFiltersFromUrl() {\n512\t    var search = window.location.search || \"\";\n513\t    var params = new URLSearchParams(search);\n514\t\n515\t    var bubbles = document.querySelectorAll('[data-testid=\"trace-map-bubble\"]');\n516\t    Array.prototype.forEach.call(bubbles, function (bubble) {\n517\t      var dimension = bubble.dataset.bubbleDimension;\n518\t      var value = bubble.dataset.bubbleValue;\n519\t      if (!dimension) return;\n520\t\n521\t      var urlValues = (params.get(dimension) || \"\").split(\",\").filter(function (v) {\n522\t        return v !== \"\";\n523\t      });\n524\t      var pressed = bubble.dataset.bubbleOff !== \"true\" && urlValues.indexOf(value) !== -1;\n525\t      bubble.setAttribute(\"aria-pressed\", pressed ? \"true\" : \"false\");\n526\t      setBubbleActiveClasses(bubble, pressed);\n527\t    });\n528\t\n529\t    applyTraceMapFilters();\n530\t  }\n531\t\n532\t  // Bug fix (trace-map-filter-reset-bugfix, AC3 popstate-sync fix,\n533\t  // code-review finding): `applyTraceMapFiltersFromUrl`, above, only ever\n534\t  // updates the DOM (aria-pressed/CSS classes/row visibility) — it never\n535\t  // told the SERVER that a back/forward navigation changed which filters\n536\t  // are active. `logs_live.ex`'s `@active_filters` assign (the actual\n537\t  // source of truth every bubble re-renders `active:` from, per\n538\t  // `toggle_filter`/`remove_filter` above) stayed stale after a \"back\",\n539\t  // so the NEXT server-triggered re-render (e.g. a new log entry bumping\n540\t  // an unrelated bubble's count) silently re-asserted the STALE\n541\t  // pre-navigation filter state over the just-restored, correct DOM/URL\n542\t  // state — reintroducing this story's exact bug via the back/forward\n543\t  // path instead of a plain click.\n544\t  //\n545\t  // Called from the `popstate` listener below, right after\n546\t  // `applyTraceMapFiltersFromUrl` has re-derived the DOM: reads the SAME\n547\t  // now-current URL query params and pushes them to the server as a\n548\t  // `handle_event(\"sync_filters\", %{\"filters\" => %{...}}, socket)` round\n549\t  // trip that replaces `@active_filters` wholesale (see that clause's own\n550\t  // comment in `logs_live.ex` for why \"wholesale replace\", not a\n551\t  // per-dimension toggle).\n552\t  //\n553\t  // Uses `liveSocket.main.pushEvent(...)` directly rather than a\n554\t  // `phx-click`/`JS.push` command list (as every OTHER filter interaction\n555\t  // in this file does): there is no clicked element here — this is a\n556\t  // synthetic, popstate-driven update — and no public LiveView JS API\n557\t  // exists to push a plain event without either a bound element or a\n558\t  // `phx-hook`. `View.pushEvent(type, el, targetCtx, phxEvent, meta, opts,\n559\t  // onReply)` is exactly what `Phoenix.LiveView.JS.push/2` itself calls\n560\t  // under the hood for a real click (see\n561\t  // `deps/phoenix_live_view/assets/js/phoenix_live_view/js.js`'s\n562\t  // `exec_push`) — this is the same `@internal`-but-stable-shaped surface\n563\t  // `syncLiveSocketHref`, above, already uses and justifies for the\n564\t  // identical reason (no supported alternative achieves a purely\n565\t  // client-side-triggered server update).\n566\t  //\n567\t  // dev-story cycle 1, code-review MAJOR finding M2 (real crash bug): this\n568\t  // function is invoked from a WINDOW-level `popstate` listener bound ONCE\n569\t  // at boot (see `wireTraceMapFilters` below), unconditionally, regardless\n570\t  // of which LiveView happens to be currently mounted. `DashboardLive`/\n571\t  // `AuditLogLive` share the same `live_session :admin` as `LogsLive` but\n572\t  // define no `handle_event/3` clause at all (Phoenix LiveView injects no\n573\t  // default handler) — landing on `/admin/logs` (binding this listener at\n574\t  // boot), live-navigating to `/admin/dashboard`, then pressing browser\n575\t  // Back used to push `sync_filters` into whatever LiveView was mounted,\n576\t  // crashing it with `UndefinedFunctionError`. Fixed by requiring the\n577\t  // dedicated `#trace-map-sync-anchor` marker (see `logs_live.ex`'s\n578\t  // `render/1`) to be present in the DOM before pushing anything at all —\n579\t  // that marker only exists while `LogsLive` (the one LiveView that\n580\t  // actually owns the Trace Map / `handle_event(\"sync_filters\", ...)`) is\n581\t  // mounted, so a `popstate` firing while a sibling LiveView is mounted\n582\t  // instead is now a safe no-op.\n583\t  //\n584\t  // dev-story cycle 1, code-review MINOR finding (blast radius): the SAME\n585\t  // marker element also replaces `document.body` as the `pushEvent`\n586\t  // target — `document.body` set a PAGE-WIDE `data-phx-ref-lock` during\n587\t  // the round trip (blocking LiveView patches to the WHOLE page, not just\n588\t  // the Trace Map, e.g. the `new-entries-banner` aria-live region), while\n589\t  // this tiny, dedicated, hidden element confines the lock to itself.\n590\t  //\n591\t  // dev-story cycle 1, code-review MAJOR finding M1: sends the FULL list\n592\t  // of active values per dimension (mirrors `syncTraceMapUrlFromBubbles`'s\n593\t  // own comma-joined URL encoding, split back into an array here), not a\n594\t  // single scalar — `logs_live.ex`'s `handle_event(\"sync_filters\", ...)`\n595\t  // now stores real lists per dimension, matching this file's own\n596\t  // multi-select-within-a-dimension semantics.\n597\t  function pushFilterSyncToServer() {\n598\t    var socket = window.liveSocket;\n599\t    var anchor = document.getElementById(\"trace-map-sync-anchor\");\n600\t    if (!socket || !socket.main || typeof socket.main.pushEvent !== \"function\" || !anchor) {\n601\t      return;\n602\t    }\n603\t\n604\t    var params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search || \"\");\n605\t    var filters = {};\n606\t    [\"endpoint\", \"level\", \"source\"].forEach(function (dimension) {\n607\t      var raw = params.get(dimension) || \"\";\n608\t      filters[dimension] = raw\n609\t        ? raw.split(\",\").filter(function (v) {\n610\t            return v !== \"\";\n611\t          })\n612\t        : [];\n613\t    });\n614\t\n615\t    socket.main.pushEvent(\"click\", anchor, null, \"sync_filters\", null, {\n616\t      value: { filters: filters },\n617\t    });\n618\t  }\n619\t\n620\t  function wireTraceMapFilters() {\n621\t    if (window.__nebuTraceMapWired) return;\n622\t    window.__nebuTraceMapWired = true;\n623\t\n624\t    document.addEventListener(\"nebu:trace-bubble-click\", function (event) {\n625\t      var bubble = event.target;\n626\t      if (!bubble || !bubble.dataset) return;\n627\t\n628\t      // AC2: clicking an \"off\" (uncollected) bubble never activates a filter —\n629\t      // it represents a structural absence, not a toggleable dimension value.\n630\t      if (bubble.dataset.bubbleOff === \"true\") return;\n631\t\n632\t      var pressed = bubble.getAttribute(\"aria-pressed\") === \"true\";\n633\t      var nowPressed = !pressed;\n634\t      bubble.setAttribute(\"aria-pressed\", nowPressed ? \"true\" : \"false\");\n635\t      setBubbleActiveClasses(bubble, nowPressed);\n636\t      applyTraceMapFilters();\n637\t      syncTraceMapUrlFromBubbles();\n638\t    });\n639\t\n640\t    // The active-filter chip row's own \"×\" remove control (AC4) — a DISTINCT\n641\t    // interaction from re-clicking the bubble, but it must deactivate the\n642\t    // SAME bubble so both stay in lockstep.\n643\t    document.addEventListener(\"nebu:trace-chip-remove\", function (event) {\n644\t      var button = event.target;\n645\t      if (!button || !button.dataset) return;\n646\t\n647\t      var dimension = button.dataset.chipDimension;\n648\t      var value = button.dataset.chipValue;\n649\t      if (dimension && value) {\n650\t        var selector =\n651\t          '[data-testid=\"trace-map-bubble\"][data-bubble-dimension=\"' +\n652\t          dimension +\n653\t          '\"][data-bubble-value=\"' +\n654\t          value +\n655\t          '\"]';\n656\t        var bubble = document.querySelector(selector);\n657\t        if (bubble) {\n658\t          bubble.setAttribute(\"aria-pressed\", \"false\");\n659\t          setBubbleActiveClasses(bubble, false);\n660\t        }\n661\t      }\n662\t\n663\t      applyTraceMapFilters();\n664\t      syncTraceMapUrlFromBubbles();\n665\t    });\n666\t\n667\t    // AC8: copy the full chronological thread to the clipboard for support hand-off.\n668\t    document.addEventListener(\"nebu:copy-thread\", function (event) {\n669\t      var button = event.target;\n670\t      var text = button && button.dataset ? button.dataset.copyText : \"\";\n671\t      if (text && navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {\n672\t        navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).catch(function () {\n673\t          // Best-effort — clipboard permissions can be denied by the browser;\n674\t          // no user-visible error surface exists for this secondary action.\n675\t        });\n676\t      }\n677\t    });\n678\t\n679\t    // Bug fix (trace-map-filter-reset-bugfix, AC3): browser back/forward\n680\t    // navigation fires `popstate` (NOT a page reload, since\n681\t    // `syncTraceMapUrlFromBubbles` only ever uses `pushState`) — re-derive\n682\t    // every bubble's pressed state from the now-current URL and reapply\n683\t    // row/chip visibility to match.\n684\t    window.addEventListener(\"popstate\", function () {\n685\t      applyTraceMapFiltersFromUrl();\n686\t      pushFilterSyncToServer();\n687\t    });\n688\t\n689\t    // Run once at load so any server-rendered \"off\" state / initial counts are\n690\t    // reflected immediately, even before the first click.\n691\t    applyTraceMapFilters();\n692\t\n693\t    wireTraceMapRowObserver();\n694\t  }\n695\t\n696\t  // V6 fix (UX re-verification pass, cycle 3): `applyTraceMapFilters()` was only\n697\t  // ever re-run on a bubble click, a chip-remove click, and once at boot — never\n698\t  // when the `#request-rows` stream container itself gained new children. AC9/\n699\t  // AC11 stream new/updated rows in via `Phoenix.LiveView.stream_insert/4` (see\n700\t  // `logs_live.ex`'s `upsert_row/2`) entirely independently of any click, so a\n701\t  // row that streams in while a filter is active rendered fully visible —\n702\t  // silently bypassing the active filter (AC3 says filters apply to \"the\n703\t  // already-loaded/streaming rows in the browser\"; that's the \"streaming\" half\n704\t  // this was missing).\n705\t  //\n706\t  // M1 fix (bug-fix cycle 5, code-review MAJOR finding): the cycle-3 fix above\n707\t  // only wired this observer directly onto `document.getElementById(\"request-rows\")`\n708\t  // AND ONLY IF that element already existed at the moment `wireTraceMapFilters()`\n709\t  // ran (once, from `boot()`, on `DOMContentLoaded`). `router.ex` places\n710\t  // `DashboardLive`/`AuditLogLive`/`LogsLive` all inside the SAME `live_session\n711\t  // :admin` block, and `layouts.ex`'s sidebar uses `<.link navigate=\"...\">`\n712\t  // between them — Phoenix LiveView \"live navigation\" between LiveViews in the\n713\t  // same live_session does NOT reload the page, so `DOMContentLoaded` never\n714\t  // fires again and `#request-rows` (which only exists on `/admin/logs`) was\n715\t  // never present yet for any tab whose initial full page load landed on\n716\t  // Dashboard or AuditLog first (the common case). The observer was then\n717\t  // permanently never wired for that tab, even after navigating into\n718\t  // `/admin/logs` — silently reintroducing the cycle-3 bug for the normal\n719\t  // navigation path.\n720\t  //\n721\t  // Fixed by observing `document.body` (present at boot, exactly like\n722\t  // `wireDetailPanelFocus`'s (V4) own `document.body` observer just below —\n723\t  // that function already established this precedent for the identical \"this\n724\t  // container doesn't necessarily exist yet, and/or LiveView live-navigation\n725\t  // never re-fires DOMContentLoaded\" problem) instead of the by-id\n726\t  // `#request-rows` container directly. This makes the wiring:\n727\t  //   - container-existence-agnostic: no early-return if `#request-rows`\n728\t  //     isn't in the DOM yet at boot time.\n729\t  //   - live-navigation-agnostic: `#request-rows` appearing in the DOM\n730\t  //     *because the operator just live-navigated into `/admin/logs`* is\n731\t  //     itself a `childList` mutation on `document.body`'s subtree, so it is\n732\t  //     observed identically to any other row/tier-migration mutation — no\n733\t  //     separate LiveSocket/`phx:navigate` lifecycle hook is needed.\n734\t  //\n735\t  // `applyTraceMapFilters()` is idempotent (it recomputes visibility for every\n736\t  // row from scratch on every call, and no-ops when `[data-testid=\"request-row\"]`\n737\t  // / `[data-testid=\"trace-map-bubble\"]` simply don't exist yet, e.g. while on\n738\t  // Dashboard/AuditLog) so simply re-running it on ANY body-subtree mutation\n739\t  // with added nodes is safe and correct. This covers ALL of the following\n740\t  // identically, with no special-casing:\n741\t  //   - live-navigating into `/admin/logs` for the first time in a tab\n742\t  //     (`#request-rows` and its initial rows appear as a body-subtree\n743\t  //     mutation)\n744\t  //   - a genuinely new `request_id` -> `stream_insert/4` appends a new node\n745\t  //     (a `childList` mutation with an added node)\n746\t  //   - an existing row whose severity tier changed -> `stream_delete_by_dom_id/3`\n747\t  //     followed by `stream_insert/4` at the new index, which removes the old\n748\t  //     DOM node (wiping its previously-applied `display:none`) and inserts a\n749\t  //     FRESH one in its place (also a `childList` mutation with an added node)\n750\t  function wireTraceMapRowObserver() {\n751\t    if (window.__nebuTraceMapRowObserverWired) return;\n752\t    window.__nebuTraceMapRowObserverWired = true;\n753\t\n754\t    var observer = new MutationObserver(function (mutations) {\n755\t      var rowsChanged = mutations.some(function (mutation) {\n756\t        return mutation.addedNodes && mutation.addedNodes.length > 0;\n757\t      });\n758\t      if (rowsChanged) applyTraceMapFilters();\n759\t    });\n760\t\n761\t    observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });\n762\t  }\n763\t\n764\t  // V4 fix (UX/accessibility gate, BLOCKING — WCAG 2.4.3): `detail_panel.ex`'s\n765\t  // `role=\"dialog\"` had no focus management at all — opening it never moved\n766\t  // focus in, and closing it never returned focus to the row that opened it,\n767\t  // silently stranding a keyboard/screen-reader user's focus wherever it\n768\t  // happened to be (often on a now-detached/hidden element). This uses a\n769\t  // single `MutationObserver` on `document.body` (LiveView patches the DOM\n770\t  // via morphdom, not by replacing this file's own listeners, so one\n771\t  // observer set up once at boot survives every subsequent live update) to\n772\t  // detect the panel appearing/disappearing:\n773\t  //   - appears  -> focus moves to its first interactive control (the\n774\t  //     \"Copy thread\" button)\n775\t  //   - disappears -> focus returns to the request-row `<button>` that most\n776\t  //     recently opened a panel (tracked via a delegated click listener,\n777\t  //     since `data-testid=\"request-row\"` is the only server-rendered hook\n778\t  //     identifying \"what was clicked to open this\")\n779\t  //\n780\t  // NOTE (documented per the fix brief): this does NOT implement a full\n781\t  // Tab/Shift+Tab focus TRAP. See detail_panel.ex's moduledoc for the\n782\t  // rationale — only focus-in-on-open and focus-return-on-close are done.\n783\t  var detailPanelFocusState = { open: false, trigger: null };\n784\t\n785\t  function wireDetailPanelFocus() {\n786\t    if (window.__nebuDetailPanelFocusWired) return;\n787\t    window.__nebuDetailPanelFocusWired = true;\n788\t\n789\t    document.addEventListener(\"click\", function (event) {\n790\t      var trigger =\n791\t        event.target &&\n792\t        typeof event.target.closest === \"function\" &&\n793\t        event.target.closest('[data-testid=\"request-row\"]');\n794\t      if (trigger) detailPanelFocusState.trigger = trigger;\n795\t    });\n796\t\n797\t    var observer = new MutationObserver(function () {\n798\t      var panel = document.querySelector('[data-testid=\"detail-panel\"]');\n799\t\n800\t      if (panel && !detailPanelFocusState.open) {\n801\t        detailPanelFocusState.open = true;\n802\t        var focusTarget =\n803\t          panel.querySelector('[data-testid=\"copy-thread-button\"]') ||\n804\t          panel.querySelector(\"button\");\n805\t        if (focusTarget) focusTarget.focus();\n806\t      } else if (!panel && detailPanelFocusState.open) {\n807\t        detailPanelFocusState.open = false;\n808\t        var trigger = detailPanelFocusState.trigger;\n809\t        detailPanelFocusState.trigger = null;\n810\t        if (trigger && document.body.contains(trigger)) trigger.focus();\n811\t      }\n812\t    });\n813\t\n814\t    observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });\n815\t  }\n816\t\n817\t  // V5(b) fix (UX/accessibility gate, BLOCKING): AC10 (\"if the user has\n818\t  // scrolled down... new rows arrive silently... with an optional 'resume\n819\t  // live updates' button\") had a real `handle_event(\"pause_live_updates\",\n820\t  // ...)` clause server-side but nothing anywhere ever triggered it — the\n821\t  // pause path was dead code. The actual admin-page scroll container is\n822\t  // `#main-content` (`Nebu.Admin.Layouts.root/1`'s `<main\n823\t  // id=\"main-content\" class=\"... overflow-auto ...\">`), NOT\n824\t  // `#request-rows` (which has no `overflow`/`max-height` of its own and\n825\t  // therefore never itself scrolls). Once the operator scrolls that\n826\t  // container away from the top, this clicks the hidden, real\n827\t  // `phx-click=\"pause_live_updates\"` button LogsLive renders\n828\t  // (`[data-testid=\"pause-live-updates-trigger\"]`) — a genuine DOM click on\n829\t  // an element carrying `phx-click` is exactly what LiveView's own\n830\t  // click-delegation listens for, so this reuses the EXISTING server\n831\t  // `handle_event` clause rather than inventing a parallel client-only pause\n832\t  // mechanism. (AC8 already pauses server-side directly when a detail row is\n833\t  // opened — see `handle_event(\"open_detail\", ...)` in logs_live.ex — no JS\n834\t  // needed for that half of the trigger.) The \"Resume live updates\" button\n835\t  // was already wired to a real `phx-click=\"resume_live_updates\"`; clicking\n836\t  // it here just resets this file's own \"already triggered\" guard so a\n837\t  // later scroll-away can re-trigger the pause.\n838\t  function wireLiveUpdatesPause() {\n839\t    if (window.__nebuLiveUpdatesPauseWired) return;\n840\t    window.__nebuLiveUpdatesPauseWired = true;\n841\t\n842\t    var pauseTriggered = false;\n843\t\n844\t    document.addEventListener(\n845\t      \"scroll\",\n846\t      function (event) {\n847\t        var container = event.target;\n848\t        if (!container || container.id !== \"main-content\") return;\n849\t        if (pauseTriggered || container.scrollTop <= 0) return;\n850\t\n851\t        var pauseButton = document.querySelector(\n852\t          '[data-testid=\"pause-live-updates-trigger\"]',\n853\t        );\n854\t        if (!pauseButton) return;\n855\t\n856\t        pauseTriggered = true;\n857\t        pauseButton.click();\n858\t      },\n859\t      true,\n860\t    );\n861\t\n862\t    document.addEventListener(\"click\", function (event) {\n863\t      var resumeButton =\n864\t        event.target &&\n865\t        typeof event.target.closest === \"function\" &&\n866\t        event.target.closest('button[phx-click=\"resume_live_updates\"]');\n867\t      if (resumeButton) pauseTriggered = false;\n868\t    });\n869\t  }\n870\t\n871\t  // admin-liveview-session-refresh (AC3): the silent OIDC refresh in\n872\t  // Nebu.Admin.Auth.fetch_current_user/2 only runs on a genuine HTTP round-trip\n873\t  // through the :browser pipeline — it never re-fires for an operator who stays on\n874\t  // an open admin LiveView (Dashboard/AuditLog) with no new HTTP request, so a\n875\t  // Keycloak-class IdP's short id_token lifetime force-logs-out the operator even\n876\t  // with a valid stored OIDC refresh token. Rather than inventing a new\n877\t  // WebSocket-reachable refresh mechanism, this hook periodically fires an\n878\t  // invisible `GET /admin/keepalive` (well inside the existing 120s pre-expiry\n879\t  // window) while any admin LiveView with this hook mounted is on screen — that\n880\t  // request goes through the normal :browser pipeline, so fetch_current_user/2's\n881\t  // EXISTING silent-refresh leg runs unmodified. No response payload is read; the\n882\t  // side effect (refreshing the underlying HTTP session) is all that matters.\n883\t  var KEEPALIVE_INTERVAL_MS = 60 * 1000;\n884\t\n885\t  var SessionKeepalive = {\n886\t    mounted: function () {\n887\t      this.timer = setInterval(sendKeepalive, KEEPALIVE_INTERVAL_MS);\n888\t    },\n889\t    destroyed: function () {\n890\t      if (this.timer) clearInterval(this.timer);\n891\t    },\n892\t  };\n893\t\n894\t  function sendKeepalive() {\n895\t    fetch(\"/admin/keepalive\", {\n896\t      method: \"GET\",\n897\t      credentials: \"same-origin\",\n898\t      cache: \"no-store\",\n899\t    }).catch(function () {\n900\t      // Best-effort heartbeat: a transient network error just means this cycle's\n901\t      // refresh attempt is skipped — the next interval tick tries again, and the\n902\t      // operator's normal HTTP navigation remains an independent refresh trigger.\n903\t    });\n904\t  }\n905\t\n906\t  // enhanced-logging/admin-logs-retention-settings (AC7/AC8/AC11): the\n907\t  // /admin/logs/config page is a plain (dead) controller render — no\n908\t  // LiveView socket exists there — so the reused-for-visual-styling bubble\n909\t  // buttons' interactivity (click-to-toggle a level, reveal/hide its inline\n910\t  // retention fields, show/hide the DEBUG excessive-logging warning) is\n911\t  // implemented entirely here, following the exact same delegated-listener,\n912\t  // CSP-safe pattern as `wireConfirmForms`/`wireUserAutocomplete` above (the\n913\t  // admin CSP is `script-src 'self'`, no `unsafe-inline` — no inline\n914\t  // `onclick=` is possible on that page either).\n915\t  //\n916\t  // Data-attribute contract (see design-decisions.md #4c and\n917\t  // test-review-findings.md — pinned down for the Playwright e2e suite,\n918\t  // `e2e/features/admin/logs-config.feature`):\n919\t  //   - Bubble: [data-testid=\"trace-map-bubble\"][data-bubble-dimension=\"level\"][data-bubble-value=\"info\"|\"debug\"]\n920\t  //     (WARN/ERROR bubbles are non-interactive <div>s with no click handler)\n921\t  //   - Retention fields container: [data-testid=\"retention-fields\"][data-bubble-value=\"info\"|\"debug\"],\n922\t  //     containing input[name=\"info_days\"|\"debug_days\"] and\n923\t  //     input[name=\"info_max_entries\"|\"debug_max_entries\"]\n924\t  //   - DEBUG warning: [data-testid=\"debug-warning\"]\n925\t  //   - Hidden enabled-state input: [data-testid=\"info-enabled-input\"|\"debug-enabled-input\"]\n926\t  //\n927\t  // AC8: the FIRST time a level is toggled ON in this page's lifetime (i.e.\n928\t  // the retention-fields node does not already exist for it — it was\n929\t  // server-rendered absent because the level was off on load), the newly\n930\t  // created fields are pre-populated with the sensible defaults (7 days /\n931\t  // 1000 max entries). Toggling off then back on again within the SAME page\n932\t  // load reuses whatever the operator already typed (the node is hidden,\n933\t  // not destroyed, once created) — only a genuinely fresh page load can\n934\t  // reset back to \"no node yet\".\n935\t  var LOGS_CONFIG_DEFAULT_DAYS = \"7\";\n936\t  var LOGS_CONFIG_DEFAULT_MAX_ENTRIES = \"1000\";\n937\t\n938\t  var DEBUG_WARNING_TEXT =\n939\t    \"⚠️ DEBUG logging captures HTTP request/response payloads, including full \" +\n940\t    \"message content (redacted per policy, but even redacted payloads are large). This \" +\n941\t    \"endpoint is called continuously by every connected client. Enabling unbounded \" +\n942\t    \"collection could rapidly fill the database. Consider using War Room mode (see \" +\n943\t    \"settings below) for a bounded time/count capture instead of leaving DEBUG on \" +\n944\t    \"indefinitely.\";\n945\t\n946\t  function logsConfigBubble(level) {\n947\t    return document.querySelector(\n948\t      '[data-testid=\"trace-map-bubble\"][data-bubble-dimension=\"level\"][data-bubble-value=\"' +\n949\t        level +\n950\t        '\"]',\n951\t    );\n952\t  }\n953\t\n954\t  function logsConfigRetentionFields(level) {\n955\t    return document.querySelector(\n956\t      '[data-testid=\"retention-fields\"][data-bubble-value=\"' + level + '\"]',\n957\t    );\n958\t  }\n959\t\n960\t  function buildRetentionFields(level) {\n961\t    var container = document.createElement(\"div\");\n962\t    container.id = level + \"-retention-fields\";\n963\t    container.setAttribute(\"data-testid\", \"retention-fields\");\n964\t    container.setAttribute(\"data-bubble-value\", level);\n965\t    container.className = \"mb-4 flex flex-wrap gap-4 items-end\";\n966\t\n967\t    container.appendChild(\n968\t      buildRetentionLabel(\n969\t        \"Retention: days\",\n970\t        level + \"_days\",\n971\t        LOGS_CONFIG_DEFAULT_DAYS,\n972\t        \"0\",\n973\t        \"90\",\n974\t        level.toUpperCase() + \" retention days\",\n975\t      ),\n976\t    );\n977\t    container.appendChild(\n978\t      buildRetentionLabel(\n979\t        \"Retention: max entries\",\n980\t        level + \"_max_entries\",\n981\t        LOGS_CONFIG_DEFAULT_MAX_ENTRIES,\n982\t        \"0\",\n983\t        \"10000\",\n984\t        level.toUpperCase() + \" retention max entries\",\n985\t      ),\n986\t    );\n987\t\n988\t    return container;\n989\t  }\n990\t\n991\t  function buildRetentionLabel(labelText, name, value, min, max, ariaLabel) {\n992\t    var label = document.createElement(\"label\");\n993\t    label.className = \"form-control\";\n994\t\n995\t    var span = document.createElement(\"span\");\n996\t    span.className = \"label-text\";\n997\t    span.textContent = labelText;\n998\t    label.appendChild(span);\n999\t\n1000\t    var input = document.createElement(\"input\");\n1001\t    input.type = \"number\";\n1002\t    input.name = name;\n1003\t    input.value = value;\n1004\t    input.placeholder = \"Leave blank for no limit\";\n1005\t    input.setAttribute(\"aria-label\", ariaLabel);\n1006\t    input.min = min;\n1007\t    input.max = max;\n1008\t    input.className = \"input input-bordered input-sm\";\n1009\t    label.appendChild(input);\n1010\t\n1011\t    return label;\n1012\t  }\n1013\t\n1014\t  // UX-gate advisory fix: role=\"status\" (not \"alert\") — contextual\n1015\t  // guidance, not an urgent/interrupting error condition. Matches the\n1016\t  // server-rendered template and the codebase's own convention (role=\"alert\"\n1017\t  // reserved for actual error flashes).\n1018\t  function buildDebugWarning() {\n1019\t    var container = document.createElement(\"div\");\n1020\t    container.id = \"debug-warning\";\n1021\t    container.setAttribute(\"data-testid\", \"debug-warning\");\n1022\t    container.className = \"alert alert-warning mb-4 max-w-2xl\";\n1023\t    container.setAttribute(\"role\", \"status\");\n1024\t\n1025\t    var p = document.createElement(\"p\");\n1026\t    p.textContent = DEBUG_WARNING_TEXT;\n1027\t    container.appendChild(p);\n1028\t\n1029\t    return container;\n1030\t  }\n1031\t\n1032\t  // UX-gate blocking fix: also toggles `aria-expanded` (paired with the\n1033\t  // `aria-controls` attribute already present on the button in the\n1034\t  // server-rendered HTML) so assistive tech has an explicit, standard\n1035\t  // signal that this button expands/collapses a controlled region — not\n1036\t  // just a bare pressed-state change.\n1037\t  function setLogsConfigBubblePressed(bubble, pressed) {\n1038\t    bubble.setAttribute(\"aria-pressed\", pressed ? \"true\" : \"false\");\n1039\t    bubble.setAttribute(\"aria-expanded\", pressed ? \"true\" : \"false\");\n1040\t    if (pressed) {\n1041\t      bubble.classList.add(\"btn-active\");\n1042\t    } else {\n1043\t      bubble.classList.remove(\"btn-active\");\n1044\t    }\n1045\t  }\n1046\t\n1047\t  function toggleLogsConfigLevel(level) {\n1048\t    var bubble = logsConfigBubble(level);\n1049\t    if
