never off-tier.\n115\t  \"\"\"\n116\t  @spec off_endpoint?(term()) :: boolean()\n117\t  def off_endpoint?(route) when is_binary(route), do: route in @long_polling_endpoints\n118\t  def off_endpoint?(_route), do: false\n119\t\n120\t  @doc \"\"\"\n121\t  Pure computation of which OBSERVED endpoint route templates (from\n122\t  `endpoint_order`) a config change on `node_key` applies to (AC1/AC2):\n123\t\n124\t    * a satellite key (anything that is not its own domain, and not\n125\t      `\"nebu\"`) cascades only to itself (\"Applies to this endpoint only\") —\n126\t      returned regardless of whether it is actually present in\n127\t      `endpoint_order` yet.\n128\t    * a domain key (`TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1` maps it to\n129\t      itself) cascades to every endpoint in `endpoint_order` whose domain\n130\t      is that key — this correctly includes the domain key itself when it\n131\t      is ALSO a satellite (a single-segment route, e.g. `/health`, is\n132\t      simultaneously its own domain and a satellite — deduplicated via\n133\t      `Enum.uniq/1`, never double-counted).\n134\t    * `\"nebu\"` cascades to every observed endpoint on the map.\n135\t  \"\"\"\n136\t  @spec cascade_targets(String.t(), [String.t()]) :: [String.t()]\n137\t  def cascade_targets(\"nebu\", endpoint_order), do: Enum.uniq(endpoint_order)\n138\t\n139\t  def cascade_targets(node_key, endpoint_order) do\n140\t    if TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(node_key) == node_key do\n141\t      endpoint_order\n142\t      |> Enum.filter(&(TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(&1) == node_key))\n143\t      |> Enum.uniq()\n144\t    else\n145\t      [node_key]\n146\t    end\n147\t  end\n148\t\n149\t  # Bounded in-memory buffers (per-LiveView-process).\n150\t  @max_stream_lines 250\n151\t  @max_inspector_events 5\n152\t  @history_seed_cap 200\n153\t\n154\t  @tick_interval_ms 1_000\n155\t  @war_room_refresh_interval_ms 2_000\n156\t  @announce_debounce_ms 2_000\n157\t\n158\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n159\t  # Mount / handle_params\n160\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n161\t\n162\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n163\t  def mount(_params, session, socket) do\n164\t    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(Nebu.PubSub, @topic)\n165\t\n166\t    socket =\n167\t      socket\n168\t      # admin-sidebar-nesting coordination (AC1 / UX gate advisory A2): the\n169\t      # Leitstand highlights the \"Trace Map\" CHILD of the nested Logs section.\n170\t      |> assign(:active_nav, \"logs-trace-map\")\n171\t      |> assign(:page_title, \"Trace Map\")\n172\t      |> assign(:endpoint_order, [])\n173\t      |> assign(:extra_domains, [])\n174\t      |> assign(:node_states, %{})\n175\t      |> assign(:node_events, %{})\n176\t      |> assign(:stream_entries, [])\n177\t      |> assign(:selected, [])\n178\t      |> assign(:active_levels, MapSet.new(@ui_levels))\n179\t      |> assign(:inspected, nil)\n180\t      |> assign(:war_room, initial_war_room_status())\n181\t      |> assign(:announcement, \"\")\n182\t      |> assign(:last_announce_ms, 0)\n183\t      |> assign(:now_ms, System.system_time(:millisecond))\n184\t      # enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls: DB-backed captures\n185\t      # (ANSWERED design question 1 — \"state is a row, expiry is a\n186\t      # comparison against now()\"). @captures is keyed by endpoint route,\n187\t      # refreshed here and on every :trace_map_tick (mirrors the war-room\n188\t      # banner's own poll pattern).\n189\t      |> assign(:captures, load_active_captures())\n190\t      |> assign(:current_admin_id, admin_id_from_session(session))\n191\t      |> assign(:cascade_flash, \"\")\n192\t\n193\t    socket =\n194\t      Enum.reduce(initial_entries(), socket, fn entry, acc ->\n195\t        ingest_entry(acc, entry, announce: false)\n196\t      end)\n197\t\n198\t    if connected?(socket) do\n199\t      schedule_tick()\n200\t      # `WarRoomManager.status/0` is a live DB read — only poll when\n201\t      # `Nebu.Repo` is running (mirrors `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s guard).\n202\t      if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo), do: schedule_war_room_refresh()\n203\t    end\n204\t\n205\t    {:ok, socket}\n206\t  end\n207\t\n208\t  # Mirrors Nebu.Admin.LogsLive's own admin_id_from_session/1 (this module\n209\t  # is deliberately a separate LiveView, see moduledoc — the small helper\n210\t  # duplication follows the same precedent as initial_war_room_status/0).\n211\t  defp admin_id_from_session(%{\"current_user\" => %{\"sub\" => sub}}) when is_binary(sub), do: sub\n212\t\n213\t  defp admin_id_from_session(%{\"current_user\" => %{\"matrix_user_id\" => uid}})\n214\t       when is_binary(uid),\n215\t       do: uid\n216\t\n217\t  defp admin_id_from_session(_session), do: \"unknown-admin\"\n218\t\n219\t  # AC5: URL query params are the single source of truth for the endpoint\n220\t  # selection — seeded here on mount AND after every push_patch round trip.\n221\t  # A bookmarked URL may select endpoints/domains no traffic has been\n222\t  # observed for yet: those are added to the map (as satellite or\n223\t  # domain-only nodes) so the restored view renders its selection rings and\n224\t  # chips immediately (bookmarkable-URL contract, AC5/AC12 Scenario 10).\n225\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n226\t  def handle_params(params, _uri, socket) do\n227\t    selected = params |> Map.get(\"endpoint\") |> normalize_selected()\n228\t\n229\t    {endpoint_order, extra_domains} =\n230\t      Enum.reduce(selected, {socket.assigns.endpoint_order, socket.assigns.extra_domains}, fn key,\n231\t                                                                                              {order,\n232\t                                                                                               extras} ->\n233\t        cond do\n234\t          TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(key) == key ->\n235\t            # A key that IS its own domain (e.g. \"/admin\", \"/health\") is a\n236\t            # domain selection — render it as a domain node, never as a\n237\t            # synthetic satellite.\n238\t            if key in extras or Enum.any?(order, &(TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(&1) == key)) do\n239\t              {order, extras}\n240\t            else\n241\t              {order, extras ++ [key]}\n242\t            end\n243\t\n244\t          key in order ->\n245\t            {order, extras}\n246\t\n247\t          true ->\n248\t            {order ++ [key], extras}\n249\t        end\n250\t      end)\n251\t\n252\t    {:noreply,\n253\t     socket\n254\t     |> assign(:selected, selected)\n255\t     |> assign(:endpoint_order, endpoint_order)\n256\t     |> assign(:extra_domains, extra_domains)}\n257\t  end\n258\t\n259\t  defp normalize_selected(nil), do: []\n260\t  defp normalize_selected(value) when is_binary(value), do: [value]\n261\t\n262\t  # code-review m1 (dev cycle 3): Enum.uniq/1 — a crafted/bookmarked URL with\n263\t  # the same endpoint[] value repeated would otherwise seed duplicate chips,\n264\t  # and toggle_node's List.delete/2 only removes ONE occurrence per click.\n265\t  defp normalize_selected(values) when is_list(values) do\n266\t    values |> Enum.filter(&is_binary/1) |> Enum.reject(&(&1 == \"\")) |> Enum.uniq()\n267\t  end\n268\t\n269\t  defp normalize_selected(_other), do: []\n270\t\n271\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n272\t  # handle_event — node filter toggles (AC5) + level pills (AC9)\n273\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n274\t\n275\t  # An ordinary server round trip (unlike the Live Stream page's\n276\t  # client-side-instant bubbles — no zero-lag constraint applies here, per\n277\t  # the ATDD contract): toggle the key in the selection, then push_patch so\n278\t  # handle_params/3 re-seeds @selected from the URL. The clicked node also\n279\t  # becomes the inspected node (AC8; mirrors the dummy's `activate()`).\n280\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n281\t  # AC5/AC7 (one-click enable, story amendment 2026-07-07): clicking an\n282\t  # off-tier, NON-capturing satellite enables temporary capture instead of\n283\t  # toggling the stream filter — the second click, once capturing, is an\n284\t  # ordinary filter toggle (falls through to the clause below).\n285\t  def handle_event(\"toggle_node\", %{\"key\" => key}, socket)\n286\t      when is_binary(key) do\n287\t    if off_endpoint?(key) and not Map.has_key?(socket.assigns.captures, key) do\n288\t      {:noreply, enable_capture(socket, key)}\n289\t    else\n290\t      selected = socket.assigns.selected\n291\t\n292\t      new_selected =\n293\t        if key in selected, do: List.delete(selected, key), else: selected ++ [key]\n294\t\n295\t      {:noreply,\n296\t       socket\n297\t       |> assign(:inspected, key)\n298\t       |> push_patch(to: patch_path(new_selected))}\n299\t    end\n300\t  end\n301\t\n302\t  def handle_event(\"toggle_node\", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n303\t\n304\t  # ── AC1/AC2: inspector logging controls (Min level + Retention selects) ──\n305\t  #\n306\t  # phx-change on the logging form (AC10 — LiveView event, CSRF-safe by\n307\t  # construction, no inline JS). node_key/min_level/retention_days per the\n308\t  # Dev Notes hidden-field variant. Cascades to every endpoint\n309\t  # cascade_targets/2 computes for node_key (domain/nebu -> all descendants;\n310\t  # satellite -> itself only).\n311\t  def handle_event(\n312\t        \"configure_logging\",\n313\t        %{\"node_key\" => node_key, \"min_level\" => min_level, \"retention_days\" => retention_days},\n314\t        socket\n315\t      ) do\n316\t    groups = domain_groups(socket.assigns.endpoint_order, socket.assigns.extra_domains)\n317\t    observed = groups |> Enum.flat_map(fn {_domain, leaves} -> leaves end) |> Enum.uniq()\n318\t    targets = cascade_targets(node_key, observed)\n319\t\n320\t    changes =\n321\t      Enum.reduce(targets, %{}, fn target, acc ->\n322\t        acc\n323\t        |> Map.put(\"log_min_level_\" <> target, String.downcase(min_level))\n324\t        |> Map.put(\"log_retention_days_\" <> target, retention_days)\n325\t      end)\n326\t\n327\t    # `Process.whereis/1` returns a pid or nil, not a boolean — `and` (unlike\n328\t    # `&&`) requires an actual boolean on its left side and raises\n329\t    # BadBooleanError otherwise (crashed this exact handler live, cycle 0\n330\t    # debugging). `if` alone already treats nil as falsy, so a single guard\n331\t    # is both correct and sufficient.\n332\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n333\t      Nebu.Admin.DB.upsert_server_config(changes)\n334\t    end\n335\t\n336\t    socket =\n337\t      if length(targets) > 1 do\n338\t        assign(socket, :cascade_flash, \"Updated #{length(targets)} endpoints\")\n339\t      else\n340\t        socket\n341\t      end\n342\t\n343\t    {:noreply, socket}\n344\t  end\n345\t\n346\t  def handle_event(\"configure_logging\", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n347\t\n348\t  # AC5: the inspector's \"Enable for 5 min\" button (the post-early-disable\n349\t  # re-enable path, test-review m4) — same enable_capture/1 helper the\n350\t  # one-click node activation above uses.\n351\t  def handle_event(\"enable_capture\", params, socket) do\n352\t    key = Map.get(params, \"key\", socket.assigns.inspected)\n353\t\n354\t    if is_binary(key) do\n355\t      {:noreply, enable_capture(socket, key)}\n356\t    else\n357\t      {:noreply, socket}\n358\t    end\n359\t  end\n360\t\n361\t  # AC5/AC7: the inspector's \"Disable now\" button — ends the capture early\n362\t  # without touching the stream filter (Known Challenge 4).\n363\t  def handle_event(\"disable_capture\", params, socket) do\n364\t    key = Map.get(params, \"key\", socket.assigns.inspected)\n365\t\n366\t    if is_binary(key) do\n367\t      {:noreply, disable_capture(socket, key)}\n368\t    else\n369\t      {:noreply, socket}\n370\t    end\n371\t  end\n372\t\n373\t  # Clicking nebu (or the stream header's \"Clear all\") clears every filter.\n374\t  # AC1 (trace-map-node-controls): nebu is ALSO the map-wide cascade\n375\t  # target — clicking it inspects it (harmless when triggered by the\n376\t  # unrelated \"Clear all\" button too, since inspecting nebu is always a\n377\t  # safe, side-effect-free default view).\n378\t  def handle_event(\"clear_filters\", _params, socket) do\n379\t    {:noreply, socket |> assign(:inspected, \"nebu\") |> push_patch(to: patch_path([]))}\n380\t  end\n381\t\n382\t  # AC9's leitstand shortcut: all active + click one -> ONLY that one;\n383\t  # the sole active one clicked again -> all active; otherwise plain toggle.\n384\t  def handle_event(\"toggle_level\", %{\"level\" => level}, socket) when level in @ui_levels do\n385\t    active = socket.assigns.active_levels\n386\t    all = MapSet.new(@ui_levels)\n387\t\n388\t    new_active =\n389\t      cond do\n390\t        MapSet.equal?(active, all) -> MapSet.new([level])\n391\t        MapSet.equal?(active, MapSet.new([level])) -> all\n392\t        MapSet.member?(active, level) -> MapSet.delete(active, level)\n393\t        true -> MapSet.put(active, level)\n394\t      end\n395\t\n396\t    {:noreply, assign(socket, :active_levels, new_active)}\n397\t  end\n398\t\n399\t  def handle_event(\"toggle_level\", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n400\t\n401\t  # AC5 URL format (story amendment): bracket-array `endpoint[]=...` params,\n402\t  # percent-encoded (`endpoint%5B%5D=...`) — `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1`\n403\t  # natively decodes this into `%{\"endpoint\" => [...]}` with no data loss.\n404\t  defp patch_path([]), do: @base_path\n405\t\n406\t  defp patch_path(selected) do\n407\t    query = Enum.map_join(selected, \"&\", fn key -> \"endpoint%5B%5D=\" <> URI.encode_www_form(key) end)\n408\t    @base_path <> \"?\" <> query\n409\t  end\n410\t\n411\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n412\t  # Temporary capture (AC5/AC6/AC9/AC12) — DB-backed timer, DB-free guard\n413\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n414\t\n415\t  # DB-free unit-test contract (mirrors initial_war_room_status/0): with no\n416\t  # Nebu.Repo running, a capture is impossible — inspect the node (AC1's\n417\t  # \"clicking a node inspects it\" contract still applies) without touching\n418\t  # the DB or the filter/selection state (AC5/AC7: enabling is never a\n419\t  # filter toggle).\n420\t  defp enable_capture(socket, key) do\n421\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n422\t      # TemporaryCaptureManager.enable/2 always returns {:ok, capture} (an\n423\t      # upsert on the endpoint's own primary key never conflicts) — no\n424\t      # error branch to match.\n425\t      {:ok, capture} = TemporaryCaptureManager.enable(key, socket.assigns.current_admin_id)\n426\t\n427\t      socket\n428\t      |> assign(:inspected, key)\n429\t      |> update(:captures, &Map.put(&1, key, capture))\n430\t      |> assign(:announcement, \"Temporary capture enabled for #{key} · 5 minutes\")\n431\t    else\n432\t      assign(socket, :inspected, key)\n433\t    end\n434\t  end\n435\t\n436\t  defp disable_capture(socket, key) do\n437\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n438\t      case TemporaryCaptureManager.disable(key, socket.assigns.current_admin_id) do\n439\t        :ok ->\n440\t          socket\n441\t          |> update(:captures, &Map.delete(&1, key))\n442\t          |> assign(:announcement, \"Temporary capture disabled for #{key}\")\n443\t\n444\t        {:error, :not_found} ->\n445\t          socket\n446\t      end\n447\t    else\n448\t      socket\n449\t    end\n450\t  end\n451\t\n452\t  # AC5/Known Challenge 2: on mount AND on every tick, the captures table is\n453\t  # the single source of truth (DB row -> expires_at comparison, never a\n454\t  # process timer). DB-free guard mirrors initial_war_room_status/0.\n455\t  defp load_active_captures do\n456\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n457\t      TemporaryCaptureManager.active_captures() |> Map.new(&{&1.endpoint, &1})\n458\t    else\n459\t      %{}\n460\t    end\n461\t  end\n462\t\n463\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n464\t  # handle_info — live PubSub ingest (AC3) + tick (auto-heal) + war-room poll\n465\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n466\t\n467\t  # Advancing `@now_ms` every second is what makes the AC3 window actually\n468\t  # SLIDE for the reader: statuses/counts are pure functions of\n469\t  # `(state, now_ms)` (see TraceMapStatus), so a node with no new traffic\n470\t  # auto-heals to :ok the moment its last warning/error falls out of the\n471\t  # window — no acknowledge, no cleanup pass, just a fresher `now`.\n472\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n473\t  def handle_info(:trace_map_tick, socket) do\n474\t    schedule_tick()\n475\t\n476\t    # AC5/AC6/Known Challenge 2: the captures table is the timer — every\n477\t    # tick re-reads it (DB-free guarded) so an expiry is discovered by a\n478\t    # fresh `now()` comparison, never by a process-owned countdown. A key\n479\t    # that drops out of the fresh read (and was not already removed by an\n480\t    # explicit disable_capture/2, which updates @captures itself) has\n481\t    # auto-expired — announce it (AC5's \"no manual intervention\").\n482\t    previous_keys = socket.assigns.captures |> Map.keys() |> MapSet.new()\n483\t    fresh_captures = load_active_captures()\n484\t    expired_keys = MapSet.difference(previous_keys, fresh_captures |> Map.keys() |> MapSet.new())\n485\t\n486\t    socket =\n487\t      socket\n488\t      |> assign(:captures, fresh_captures)\n489\t      |> assign(:now_ms, System.system_time(:millisecond))\n490\t\n491\t    socket =\n492\t      case Enum.to_list(expired_keys) do\n493\t        [] -> socket\n494\t        [key | _rest] -> assign(socket, :announcement, \"Temporary capture expired for #{key}\")\n495\t      end\n496\t\n497\t    {:noreply, socket}\n498\t  end\n499\t\n500\t  # AC6: the banner reacts to sessions starting/stopping while the page is\n501\t  # open. Always rescheduled (unlike the Live Stream page's\n502\t  # only-while-active refresh) because the banner must also APPEAR when a\n503\t  # session starts later.\n504\t  #\n505\t  # UX gate V1: the banner itself is not a live region (it contains a\n506\t  # per-second clock) — a session START/STOP transition is announced as a\n507\t  # one-shot state-change sentence through the sr-only announcer instead.\n508\t  def handle_info(:refresh_war_room_status, socket) do\n509\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo), do: schedule_war_room_refresh()"}]},"uuid":"f80e6695-4b65-4772-bda2-1a7de66c6532","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:55:11.321Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex","content":"    \"/_matrix/media\" => \"Matrix Media\",\n    \"/admin\" => \"Admin\",\n    \"/health\" => \"Health\"\n  }\n\n  # enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls (AC4/AC13): the canonical,\n  # reusable off-by-default long-polling endpoint list. This is the SAME\n  # backend concept the superseded `noise-endpoint-bucketing` story would\n  # have used — `long_polling_endpoints/0` is the single source of truth,\n  # reused by `Nebu.Web.LogStore.should_persist?/2` (server-side\n  # enforcement) and available for future stories (war-room-dedicated-page's\n  # \"safe scope\" option, trace-map-hierarchy's domain-grouping).\n  @long_polling_endpoints [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", \"/admin/keepalive\", \"/health\"]\n\n  @retention_options [{\"1\", \"1 d\"}, {\"7\", \"7 d\"}, {\"30\", \"30 d\"}]\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  The config-driven off-by-default long-polling endpoint list (AC4/AC13).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec long_polling_endpoints() :: [String.t()]\n  def long_polling_endpoints, do: @long_polling_endpoints\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Whether `route` is one of the off-by-default long-polling endpoints\n  (AC4). `nil`/non-binary routes are never off-tier.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec off_endpoint?(term()) :: boolean()\n  def off_endpoint?(route) when is_binary(route), do: route in @long_polling_endpoints\n  def off_endpoint?(_route), do: false\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Pure computation of which OBSERVED endpoint route templates (from\n  `endpoint_order`) a config change on `node_key` applies to (AC1/AC2):\n\n    * a satellite key (anything that is not its own domain, and not\n      `\"nebu\"`) cascades only to itself (\"Applies to this endpoint only\") —\n      returned regardless of whether it is actually present in\n      `endpoint_order` yet.\n    * a domain key (`TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1` maps it to\n      itself) cascades to every endpoint in `endpoint_order` whose domain\n      is that key — this correctly includes the domain key itself when it\n      is ALSO a satellite (a single-segment route, e.g. `/health`, is\n      simultaneously its own domain and a satellite — deduplicated via\n      `Enum.uniq/1`, never double-counted).\n    * `\"nebu\"` cascades to every observed endpoint on the map.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec cascade_targets(String.t(), [String.t()]) :: [String.t()]\n  def cascade_targets(\"nebu\", endpoint_order), do: Enum.uniq(endpoint_order)\n\n  def cascade_targets(node_key, endpoint_order) do\n    if TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(node_key) == node_key do\n      endpoint_order\n      |> Enum.filter(&(TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(&1) == node_key))\n      |> Enum.uniq()\n    else\n      [node_key]\n    end\n  end\n\n  # Bounded in-memory buffers (per-LiveView-process).\n  @max_stream_lines 250\n  @max_inspector_events 5\n  @history_seed_cap 200\n\n  @tick_interval_ms 1_000\n  @war_room_refresh_interval_ms 2_000\n  @announce_debounce_ms 2_000\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Mount / handle_params\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def mount(_params, session, socket) do\n    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(Nebu.PubSub, @topic)\n\n    socket =\n      socket\n      # admin-sidebar-nesting coordination (AC1 / UX gate advisory A2): the\n      # Leitstand highlights the \"Trace Map\" CHILD of the nested Logs section.\n      |> assign(:active_nav, \"logs-trace-map\")\n      |> assign(:page_title, \"Trace Map\")\n      |> assign(:endpoint_order, [])\n      |> assign(:extra_domains, [])\n      |> assign(:node_states, %{})\n      |> assign(:node_events, %{})\n      |> assign(:stream_entries, [])\n      |> assign(:selected, [])\n      |> assign(:active_levels, MapSet.new(@ui_levels))\n      |> assign(:inspected, nil)\n      |> assign(:war_room, initial_war_room_status())\n      |> assign(:announcement, \"\")\n      |> assign(:last_announce_ms, 0)\n      |> assign(:now_ms, System.system_time(:millisecond))\n      # enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls: DB-backed captures\n      # (ANSWERED design question 1 — \"state is a row, expiry is a\n      # comparison against now()\"). @captures is keyed by endpoint route,\n      # refreshed here and on every :trace_map_tick (mirrors the war-room\n      # banner's own poll pattern).\n      |> assign(:captures, load_active_captures())\n      |> assign(:current_admin_id, admin_id_from_session(session))\n      |> assign(:cascade_flash, \"\")\n\n    socket =\n      Enum.reduce(initial_entries(), socket, fn entry, acc ->\n        ingest_entry(acc, entry, announce: false)\n      end)\n\n    if connected?(socket) do\n      schedule_tick()\n      # `WarRoomManager.status/0` is a live DB read — only poll when\n      # `Nebu.Repo` is running (mirrors `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s guard).\n      if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo), do: schedule_war_room_refresh()\n    end\n\n    {:ok, socket}\n  end\n\n  # Mirrors Nebu.Admin.LogsLive's own admin_id_from_session/1 (this module\n  # is deliberately a separate LiveView, see moduledoc — the small helper\n  # duplication follows the same precedent as initial_war_room_status/0).\n  defp admin_id_from_session(%{\"current_user\" => %{\"sub\" => sub}}) when is_binary(sub), do: sub\n\n  defp admin_id_from_session(%{\"current_user\" => %{\"matrix_user_id\" => uid}})\n       when is_binary(uid),\n       do: uid\n\n  defp admin_id_from_session(_session), do: \"unknown-admin\"\n\n  # AC5: URL query params are the single source of truth for the endpoint\n  # selection — seeded here on mount AND after every push_patch round trip.\n  # A bookmarked URL may select endpoints/domains no traffic has been\n  # observed for yet: those are added to the map (as satellite or\n  # domain-only nodes) so the restored view renders its selection rings and\n  # chips immediately (bookmarkable-URL contract, AC5/AC12 Scenario 10).\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def handle_params(params, _uri, socket) do\n    selected = params |> Map.get(\"endpoint\") |> normalize_selected()\n\n    {endpoint_order, extra_domains} =\n      Enum.reduce(selected, {socket.assigns.endpoint_order, socket.assigns.extra_domains}, fn key,\n                                                                                              {order,\n                                                                                               extras} ->\n        cond do\n          TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(key) == key ->\n            # A key that IS its own domain (e.g. \"/admin\", \"/health\") is a\n            # domain selection — render it as a domain node, never as a\n            # synthetic satellite.\n            if key in extras or Enum.any?(order, &(TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(&1) == key)) do\n              {order, extras}\n            else\n              {order, extras ++ [key]}\n            end\n\n          key in order ->\n            {order, extras}\n\n          true ->\n            {order ++ [key], extras}\n        end\n      end)\n\n    {:noreply,\n     socket\n     |> assign(:selected, selected)\n     |> assign(:endpoint_order, endpoint_order)\n     |> assign(:extra_domains, extra_domains)}\n  end\n\n  defp normalize_selected(nil), do: []\n  defp normalize_selected(value) when is_binary(value), do: [value]\n\n  # code-review m1 (dev cycle 3): Enum.uniq/1 — a crafted/bookmarked URL with\n  # the same endpoint[] value repeated would otherwise seed duplicate chips,\n  # and toggle_node's List.delete/2 only removes ONE occurrence per click.\n  defp normalize_selected(values) when is_list(values) do\n    values |> Enum.filter(&is_binary/1) |> Enum.reject(&(&1 == \"\")) |> Enum.uniq()\n  end\n\n  defp normalize_selected(_other), do: []\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # handle_event — node filter toggles (AC5) + level pills (AC9)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  # An ordinary server round trip (unlike the Live Stream page's\n  # client-side-instant bubbles — no zero-lag constraint applies here, per\n  # the ATDD contract): toggle the key in the selection, then push_patch so\n  # handle_params/3 re-seeds @selected from the URL. The clicked node also\n  # becomes the inspected node (AC8; mirrors the dummy's `activate()`).\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  # AC5/AC7 (one-click enable, story amendment 2026-07-07): clicking an\n  # off-tier, NON-capturing satellite enables temporary capture instead of\n  # toggling the stream filter — the second click, once capturing, is an\n  # ordinary filter toggle (falls through to the clause below).\n  def handle_event(\"toggle_node\", %{\"key\" => key}, socket)\n      when is_binary(key) do\n    if off_endpoint?(key) and not Map.has_key?(socket.assigns.captures, key) do\n      {:noreply, enable_capture(socket, key)}\n    else\n      selected = socket.assigns.selected\n\n      new_selected =\n        if key in selected, do: List.delete(selected, key), else: selected ++ [key]\n\n      {:noreply,\n       socket\n       |> assign(:inspected, key)\n       |> push_patch(to: patch_path(new_selected))}\n    end\n  end\n\n  def handle_event(\"toggle_node\", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n\n  # ── AC1/AC2: inspector logging controls (Min level + Retention selects) ──\n  #\n  # phx-change on the logging form (AC10 — LiveView event, CSRF-safe by\n  # construction, no inline JS). node_key/min_level/retention_days per the\n  # Dev Notes hidden-field variant. Cascades to every endpoint\n  # cascade_targets/2 computes for node_key (domain/nebu -> all descendants;\n  # satellite -> itself only).\n  def handle_event(\n        \"configure_logging\",\n        %{\"node_key\" => node_key, \"min_level\" => min_level, \"retention_days\" => retention_days},\n        socket\n      ) do\n    groups = domain_groups(socket.assigns.endpoint_order, socket.assigns.extra_domains)\n    observed = groups |> Enum.flat_map(fn {_domain, leaves} -> leaves end) |> Enum.uniq()\n    targets = cascade_targets(node_key, observed)\n\n    changes =\n      Enum.reduce(targets, %{}, fn target, acc ->\n        acc\n        |> Map.put(\"log_min_level_\" <> target, String.downcase(min_level))\n        |> Map.put(\"log_retention_days_\" <> target, retention_days)\n      end)\n\n    # `Process.whereis/1` returns a pid or nil, not a boolean — `and` (unlike\n    # `&&`) requires an actual boolean on its left side and raises\n    # BadBooleanError otherwise (crashed this exact handler live, cycle 0\n    # debugging). `if` alone already treats nil as falsy, so a single guard\n    # is both correct and sufficient.\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      Nebu.Admin.DB.upsert_server_config(changes)\n    end\n\n    socket =\n      if length(targets) > 1 do\n        assign(socket, :cascade_flash, \"Updated #{length(targets)} endpoints\")\n      else\n        socket\n      end\n\n    {:noreply, socket}\n  end\n\n  def handle_event(\"configure_logging\", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n\n  # AC5: the inspector's \"Enable for 5 min\" button (the post-early-disable\n  # re-enable path, test-review m4) — same enable_capture/1 helper the\n  # one-click node activation above uses.\n  def handle_event(\"enable_capture\", params, socket) do\n    key = Map.get(params, \"key\", socket.assigns.inspected)\n\n    if is_binary(key) do\n      {:noreply, enable_capture(socket, key)}\n    else\n      {:noreply, socket}\n    end\n  end\n\n  # AC5/AC7: the inspector's \"Disable now\" button — ends the capture early\n  # without touching the stream filter (Known Challenge 4).\n  def handle_event(\"disable_capture\", params, socket) do\n    key = Map.get(params, \"key\", socket.assigns.inspected)\n\n    if is_binary(key) do\n      {:noreply, disable_capture(socket, key)}\n    else\n      {:noreply, socket}\n    end\n  end\n\n  # Clicking nebu (or the stream header's \"Clear all\") clears every filter.\n  # AC1 (trace-map-node-controls): nebu is ALSO the map-wide cascade\n  # target — clicking it inspects it (harmless when triggered by the\n  # unrelated \"Clear all\" button too, since inspecting nebu is always a\n  # safe, side-effect-free default view).\n  def handle_event(\"clear_filters\", _params, socket) do\n    {:noreply, socket |> assign(:inspected, \"nebu\") |> push_patch(to: patch_path([]))}\n  end\n\n  # AC9's leitstand shortcut: all active + click one -> ONLY that one;\n  # the sole active one clicked again -> all active; otherwise plain toggle.\n  def handle_event(\"toggle_level\", %{\"level\" => level}, socket) when level in @ui_levels do\n    active = socket.assigns.active_levels\n    all = MapSet.new(@ui_levels)\n\n    new_active =\n      cond do\n        MapSet.equal?(active, all) -> MapSet.new([level])\n        MapSet.equal?(active, MapSet.new([level])) -> all\n        MapSet.member?(active, level) -> MapSet.delete(active, level)\n        true -> MapSet.put(active, level)\n      end\n\n    {:noreply, assign(socket, :active_levels, new_active)}\n  end\n\n  def handle_event(\"toggle_level\", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n\n  # AC5 URL format (story amendment): bracket-array `endpoint[]=...` params,\n  # percent-encoded (`endpoint%5B%5D=...`) — `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1`\n  # natively decodes this into `%{\"endpoint\" => [...]}` with no data loss.\n  defp patch_path([]), do: @base_path\n\n  defp patch_path(selected) do\n    query = Enum.map_join(selected, \"&\", fn key -> \"endpoint%5B%5D=\" <> URI.encode_www_form(key) end)\n    @base_path <> \"?\" <> query\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Temporary capture (AC5/AC6/AC9/AC12) — DB-backed timer, DB-free guard\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  # DB-free unit-test contract (mirrors initial_war_room_status/0): with no\n  # Nebu.Repo running, a capture is impossible — inspect the node (AC1's\n  # \"clicking a node inspects it\" contract still applies) without touching\n  # the DB or the filter/selection state (AC5/AC7: enabling is never a\n  # filter toggle).\n  defp enable_capture(socket, key) do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      # TemporaryCaptureManager.enable/2 always returns {:ok, capture} (an\n      # upsert on the endpoint's own primary key never conflicts) — no\n      # error branch to match.\n      {:ok, capture} = TemporaryCaptureManager.enable(key, socket.assigns.current_admin_id)\n\n      socket\n      |> assign(:inspected, key)\n      |> update(:captures, &Map.put(&1, key, capture))\n      |> assign(:announcement, \"Temporary capture enabled for #{key} · 5 minutes\")\n    else\n      assign(socket, :inspected, key)\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp disable_capture(socket, key) do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      case TemporaryCaptureManager.disable(key, socket.assigns.current_admin_id) do\n        :ok ->\n          socket\n          |> update(:captures, &Map.delete(&1, key))\n          |> assign(:announcement, \"Temporary capture disabled for #{key}\")\n\n        {:error, :not_found} ->\n          socket\n      end\n    else\n      socket\n    end\n  end\n\n  # AC5/Known Challenge 2: on mount AND on every tick, the captures table is\n  # the single source of truth (DB row -> expires_at comparison, never a\n  # process timer). DB-free guard mirrors initial_war_room_status/0.\n  defp load_active_captures do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      TemporaryCaptureManager.active_captures() |> Map.new(&{&1.endpoint, &1})\n    else\n      %{}\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # handle_info — live PubSub ingest (AC3) + tick (auto-heal) + war-room poll\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  # Advancing `@now_ms` every second is what makes the AC3 window actually\n  # SLIDE for the reader: statuses/counts are pure functions of\n  # `(state, now_ms)` (see TraceMapStatus), so a node with no new traffic\n  # auto-heals to :ok the moment its last warning/error falls out of the\n  # window — no acknowledge, no cleanup pass, just a fresher `now`.\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def handle_info(:trace_map_tick, socket) do\n    schedule_tick()\n\n    # AC5/AC6/Known Challenge 2: the captures table is the timer — every\n    # tick re-reads it (DB-free guarded) so an expiry is discovered by a\n    # fresh `now()` comparison, never by a process-owned countdown. A key\n    # that drops out of the fresh read (and was not already removed by an\n    # explicit disable_capture/2, which updates @captures itself) has\n    # auto-expired — announce it (AC5's \"no manual intervention\").\n    previous_keys = socket.assigns.captures |> Map.keys() |> MapSet.new()\n    fresh_captures = load_active_captures()\n    expired_keys = MapSet.difference(previous_keys, fresh_captures |> Map.keys() |> MapSet.new())\n\n    socket =\n      socket\n      |> assign(:captures, fresh_captures)\n      |> assign(:now_ms, System.system_time(:millisecond))\n\n    socket =\n      case Enum.to_list(expired_keys) do\n        [] -> socket\n        [key | _rest] -> assign(socket, :announcement, \"Temporary capture expired for #{key}\")\n      end\n\n    {:noreply, socket}\n  end\n\n  # AC6: the banner reacts to sessions starting/stopping while the page is\n  # open. Always rescheduled (unlike the Live Stream page's\n  # only-while-active refresh) because the banner must also APPEAR when a\n  # session starts later.\n  #\n  # UX gate V1: the banner itself is not a live region (it contains a\n  # per-second clock) — a session START/STOP transition is announced as a\n  # one-shot state-change sentence through the sr-only announcer instead.\n  def handle_info(:refresh_war_room_status, socket) do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo), do: schedule_war_room_refresh()","numLines":420,"startLine":90,"totalLines":1758}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"775854fb-5959-41b9-87a1-bf3c0d2bcc6f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

selection; nebu is never a toggle target (always\n1169\t  # \"false\" — clicking it CLEARS filters instead).\n1170\t  attr(:node, :map, required: true)\n1171\t\n1172\t  defp map_node(assigns) ERROR always wins (the\n1409\t  # errors-always-persist amendment) — an off-tier satellite with a\n1410\t  # windowed error renders \"error\" (with data-node-off still true, so the\n1411\t  # dashed off-tier stroke stays — see map_node/1), not the healthy-looking\n1412\t  # \"off\" state.\n1413\t  defp display_status(key, raw_status, captures) do\n1414\t    cond do\n1415\t      Map.has_key?(captures, key) -> \"temp\"\n1416\t      off_endpoint?(key) and raw_status == :error -> \"error\"\n1417\t      off_endpoint?(key) -> \"off\"\n1418\t      true -> Atom.to_string(raw_status)\n1419\t    end\n1420\t  end\n1421\t\n1422\t  defp build_node(key, kind, position, raw_status, display_status, label, route, selected, states, now, captures) do\n1423\t    counts = TraceMapStatus.counts(node_state(states, key), now)\n1424\t    is_selected = kind != \"nebu\" and key in selected\n1425\t    off_tier = kind == \"satellite\" and off_endpoint?(key)\n1426\t    capture = if kind == \"satellite\", do: Map.get(captures, key), else: nil\n1427\t\n1428\t    %{\n1429\t      key: key,\n1430\t      kind: kind,\n1431\t      # A stable DOM id per node: LiveView's DOM patching matches keyed\n1432\t      # elements in place, so a selection/status patch can never replace the\n1433\t      # <g> element itself — keyboard focus survives Enter/Space toggles\n1434\t      # (AC10; verified against the live stack in dev cycle 1).\n1435\t      #\n1436\t      # KIND-prefixed (dev cycle 2): \"/health\" is simultaneously a domain key\n1437\t      # AND a satellite key (a single-segment route is its own domain per\n1438\t      # TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1) — a key-only id produced two\n1439\t      # elements with the SAME id, which corrupts LiveView's id-keyed DOM\n1440\t      # patching for exactly those nodes (observed live: the toggle patch for\n1441\t      # the /health domain never applied).\n1442\t      dom_id: \"tm-node-\" <> kind <> \"-\" <> Base.url_encode64(key, padding: false),\n1443\t      x: coord(position.x),\n1444\t      y: coord(position.y),\n1445\t      r: position.r,\n1446\t      status: display_status,\n1447\t      off_tier: off_tier,\n1448\t      capture: capture,\n1449\t      countdown_text: if(capture, do: capture_countdown_text(capture, now), else: nil),\n1450\t      countdown_expires_at: if(capture, do: capture_expires_at_unix(capture), else: nil),\n1451\t      selected: is_selected,\n1452\t      pressed: is_selected,\n1453\t      label: label,\n1454\t      route: route,\n1455\t      counts: counts,\n1456\t      aria_label:\n1457\t        node_aria_label(kind, label, route, display_status, raw_status, counts, capture, now, off_tier),\n1458\t      tooltip_status: status_word(raw_status),\n1459\t      tooltip_counts: counts_text(counts),\n1460\t      tooltip_cta: tooltip_cta(kind),\n1461\t      click_event: if(kind == \"nebu\", do: \"clear_filters\", else: \"toggle_node\")\n1462\t    }\n1463\t  end\n1464\t\n1465\t  defp node_state(states, key), do: Map.get(states, key, TraceMapStatus.new())\n1466\t\n1467\t  defp build_edges(groups, layout) do\n1468\t    domain_edges =\n1469\t      Enum.map(layout.domains, fn {_key, domain} ->\n1470\t        edge(layout.nebu, domain)\n1471\t      end)\n1472\t\n1473\t    satellite_edges =\n1474\t      Enum.flat_map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n1475\t        domain_pos = Map.fetch!(layout.domains, domain)\n1476\t        Enum.map(leaves, fn route -> edge(domain_pos, Map.fetch!(layout.satellites, route)) end)\n1477\t      end)\n1478\t\n1479\t    domain_edges ++ satellite_edges\n1480\t  end\n1481\t\n1482\t  defp edge(a, b) do\n1483\t    {x1, y1, x2, y2} = TraceMapGeometry.trim_edge(a, b)\n1484\t    %{x1: coord(x1), y1: coord(y1), x2: coord(x2), y2: coord(y2)}\n1485\t  end\n1486\t\n1487\t  defp coord(value), do: :erlang.float_to_binary(value / 1, decimals: 2)\n1488\t\n1489\t  defp visible_lines(assigns) do\n1490\t    Enum.filter(assigns.stream_entries, fn line ->\n1491\t      MapSet.member?(assigns.active_levels, line.ui_level) and\n1492\t        endpoint_match?(line, assigns.selected)\n1493\t    end)\n1494\t  end\n1495\t\n1496\t  # AC5: OR within the endpoint dimension; a selected DOMAIN matches all its\n1497\t  # satellites' lines.\n1498\t  defp endpoint_match?(_line, []), do: true\n1499\t\n1500\t  defp endpoint_match?(line, selected) do\n1501\t    line.route in selected or (line.domain != nil and line.domain in selected)\n1502\t  end\n1503\t\n1504\t  defp inspected_node(%{inspected: nil}), do: nil\n1505\t\n1506\t  defp inspected_node(assigns) do\n1507\t    key = assigns.inspected\n1508\t    now = assigns.now_ms\n1509\t    state = node_state(assigns.node_states, key)\n1510\t\n1511\t    {kind, label, route} =\n1512\t      cond do\n1513\t        key == \"nebu\" -> {\"nebu\", \"nebu\", \"all traffic\"}\n1514\t        TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(key) == key -> {\"domain\", domain_label(key), key}\n1515\t        true -> {\"satellite\", satellite_label(key), key}\n1516\t      end\n1517\t\n1518\t    %{\n1519\t      key: key,\n1520\t      kind: kind,\n1521\t      label: label,\n1522\t      route: route,\n1523\t      status: state |> TraceMapStatus.status(now) |> Atom.to_string(),\n1524\t      counts: TraceMapStatus.counts(state, now),\n1525\t      events: Map.get(assigns.node_events, key, []),\n1526\t      # AC1/AC2/AC3: the \"Logging\" panel — built once per inspected node.\n1527\t      # min_level/retention_days are pure functions of (key, stored\n1528\t      # server_config), never of @now_ms/counts, so a stat-tick re-render\n1529\t      # produces byte-identical select markup (AC3).\n1530\t      scope_note: scope_note(kind),\n1531\t      min_level: current_min_level(key),\n1532\t      retention_days: current_retention_days(key),\n1533\t      # AC5/AC6/AC9: capture controls apply to ANY off-list key regardless\n1534\t      # of its domain/satellite display kind (/health is simultaneously\n1535\t      # both — see TraceMap.off_endpoint?/1's moduledoc).\n1536\t      off_tier: off_endpoint?(key),\n1537\t      capture: Map.get(assigns.captures, key)\n1538\t    }\n1539\t  end\n1540\t\n1541\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n1542\t  # Labels / text helpers\n1543\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n1544\t\n1545\t  defp domain_label(domain), do: Map.get(@domain_labels, domain, domain)\n1546\t\n1547\t  defp satellite_label(route) do\n1548\t    route\n1549\t    |> String.split(\"/\")\n1550\t    |> Enum.reject(&(&1 == \"\"))\n1551\t    |> List.last()\n1552\t    |> case do\n1553\t      nil -> route\n1554\t      segment -> segment\n1555\t    end\n1556\t  end\n1557\t\n1558\t  # AC1/AC2: the inspector's scope note (dummy copy).\n1559\t  defp scope_note(\"satellite\"), do: \"Applies to this endpoint only\"\n1560\t  defp scope_note(\"domain\"), do: \"Applies to all endpoints in this domain\"\n1561\t  defp scope_note(\"nebu\"), do: \"Applies to all endpoints on the map\"\n1562\t\n1563\t  # AC1/AC2: pre-population from server_config (Dev Notes key format:\n1564\t  # log_min_level_<route> / log_retention_days_<route>). DB-free guard\n1565\t  # mirrors the rest of this module's Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) contract —\n1566\t  # sensible defaults (INFO / 7 d) when unset or DB-free.\n1567\t  defp current_min_level(key) do\n1568\t    read_config_value(\"log_min_level_\" <> key, \"info\") |> to_string() |> String.upcase()\n1569\t  end\n1570\t\n1571\t  defp current_retention_days(key) do\n1572\t    read_config_value(\"log_retention_days_\" <> key, \"7\") |> to_string()\n1573\t  end\n1574\t\n1575\t  defp read_config_value(key, default) do\n1576\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n1577\t remaining\""}]},"uuid":"9d61005d-14bc-45f4-966d-48c88971eb16","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:56:04.381Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex","content":"\n        [domain_node | satellite_nodes]\n      end)\n\n    [nebu_node | domain_and_satellite_nodes]\n  end\n\n  # AC4/AC5/AC6/AC8: overlays the off-by-default/temp-capture states on top\n  # of a satellite's raw worst-status rank. ERROR always wins (the\n  # errors-always-persist amendment) — an off-tier satellite with a\n  # windowed error renders \"error\" (with data-node-off still true, so the\n  # dashed off-tier stroke stays — see map_node/1), not the healthy-looking\n  # \"off\" state.\n  defp display_status(key, raw_status, captures) do\n    cond do\n      Map.has_key?(captures, key) -> \"temp\"\n      off_endpoint?(key) and raw_status == :error -> \"error\"\n      off_endpoint?(key) -> \"off\"\n      true -> Atom.to_string(raw_status)\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp build_node(key, kind, position, raw_status, display_status, label, route, selected, states, now, captures) do\n    counts = TraceMapStatus.counts(node_state(states, key), now)\n    is_selected = kind != \"nebu\" and key in selected\n    off_tier = kind == \"satellite\" and off_endpoint?(key)\n    capture = if kind == \"satellite\", do: Map.get(captures, key), else: nil\n\n    %{\n      key: key,\n      kind: kind,\n      # A stable DOM id per node: LiveView's DOM patching matches keyed\n      # elements in place, so a selection/status patch can never replace the\n      # <g> element itself — keyboard focus survives Enter/Space toggles\n      # (AC10; verified against the live stack in dev cycle 1).\n      #\n      # KIND-prefixed (dev cycle 2): \"/health\" is simultaneously a domain key\n      # AND a satellite key (a single-segment route is its own domain per\n      # TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1) — a key-only id produced two\n      # elements with the SAME id, which corrupts LiveView's id-keyed DOM\n      # patching for exactly those nodes (observed live: the toggle patch for\n      # the /health domain never applied).\n      dom_id: \"tm-node-\" <> kind <> \"-\" <> Base.url_encode64(key, padding: false),\n      x: coord(position.x),\n      y: coord(position.y),\n      r: position.r,\n      status: display_status,\n      off_tier: off_tier,\n      capture: capture,\n      countdown_text: if(capture, do: capture_countdown_text(capture, now), else: nil),\n      countdown_expires_at: if(capture, do: capture_expires_at_unix(capture), else: nil),\n      selected: is_selected,\n      pressed: is_selected,\n      label: label,\n      route: route,\n      counts: counts,\n      aria_label:\n        node_aria_label(kind, label, route, display_status, raw_status, counts, capture, now, off_tier),\n      tooltip_status: status_word(raw_status),\n      tooltip_counts: counts_text(counts),\n      tooltip_cta: tooltip_cta(kind),\n      click_event: if(kind == \"nebu\", do: \"clear_filters\", else: \"toggle_node\")\n    }\n  end\n\n  defp node_state(states, key), do: Map.get(states, key, TraceMapStatus.new())\n\n  defp build_edges(groups, layout) do\n    domain_edges =\n      Enum.map(layout.domains, fn {_key, domain} ->\n        edge(layout.nebu, domain)\n      end)\n\n    satellite_edges =\n      Enum.flat_map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n        domain_pos = Map.fetch!(layout.domains, domain)\n        Enum.map(leaves, fn route -> edge(domain_pos, Map.fetch!(layout.satellites, route)) end)\n      end)\n\n    domain_edges ++ satellite_edges\n  end\n\n  defp edge(a, b) do\n    {x1, y1, x2, y2} = TraceMapGeometry.trim_edge(a, b)\n    %{x1: coord(x1), y1: coord(y1), x2: coord(x2), y2: coord(y2)}\n  end\n\n  defp coord(value), do: :erlang.float_to_binary(value / 1, decimals: 2)\n\n  defp visible_lines(assigns) do\n    Enum.filter(assigns.stream_entries, fn line ->\n      MapSet.member?(assigns.active_levels, line.ui_level) and\n        endpoint_match?(line, assigns.selected)\n    end)\n  end\n\n  # AC5: OR within the endpoint dimension; a selected DOMAIN matches all its\n  # satellites' lines.\n  defp endpoint_match?(_line, []), do: true\n\n  defp endpoint_match?(line, selected) do\n    line.route in selected or (line.domain != nil and line.domain in selected)\n  end\n\n  defp inspected_node(%{inspected: nil}), do: nil\n\n  defp inspected_node(assigns) do\n    key = assigns.inspected\n    now = assigns.now_ms\n    state = node_state(assigns.node_states, key)\n\n    {kind, label, route} =\n      cond do\n        key == \"nebu\" -> {\"nebu\", \"nebu\", \"all traffic\"}\n        TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(key) == key -> {\"domain\", domain_label(key), key}\n        true -> {\"satellite\", satellite_label(key), key}\n      end\n\n    %{\n      key: key,\n      kind: kind,\n      label: label,\n      route: route,\n      status: state |> TraceMapStatus.status(now) |> Atom.to_string(),\n      counts: TraceMapStatus.counts(state, now),\n      events: Map.get(assigns.node_events, key, []),\n      # AC1/AC2/AC3: the \"Logging\" panel — built once per inspected node.\n      # min_level/retention_days are pure functions of (key, stored\n      # server_config), never of @now_ms/counts, so a stat-tick re-render\n      # produces byte-identical select markup (AC3).\n      scope_note: scope_note(kind),\n      min_level: current_min_level(key),\n      retention_days: current_retention_days(key),\n      # AC5/AC6/AC9: capture controls apply to ANY off-list key regardless\n      # of its domain/satellite display kind (/health is simultaneously\n      # both — see TraceMap.off_endpoint?/1's moduledoc).\n      off_tier: off_endpoint?(key),\n      capture: Map.get(assigns.captures, key)\n    }\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Labels / text helpers\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  defp domain_label(domain), do: Map.get(@domain_labels, domain, domain)\n\n  defp satellite_label(route) do\n    route\n    |> String.split(\"/\")\n    |> Enum.reject(&(&1 == \"\"))\n    |> List.last()\n    |> case do\n      nil -> route\n      segment -> segment\n    end\n  end\n\n  # AC1/AC2: the inspector's scope note (dummy copy).\n  defp scope_note(\"satellite\"), do: \"Applies to this endpoint only\"\n  defp scope_note(\"domain\"), do: \"Applies to all endpoints in this domain\"\n  defp scope_note(\"nebu\"), do: \"Applies to all endpoints on the map\"\n\n  # AC1/AC2: pre-population from server_config (Dev Notes key format:\n  # log_min_level_<route> / log_retention_days_<route>). DB-free guard\n  # mirrors the rest of this module's Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) contract —\n  # sensible defaults (INFO / 7 d) when unset or DB-free.\n  defp current_min_level(key) do\n    read_config_value(\"log_min_level_\" <> key, \"info\") |> to_string() |> String.upcase()\n  end\n\n  defp current_retention_days(key) do\n    read_config_value(\"log_retention_days_\" <> key, \"7\") |> to_string()\n  end\n\n  defp read_config_value(key, default) do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      {:ok, config} = Nebu.Admin.DB.get_server_config()\n      Map.get(config, key, default)\n    else\n      default\n    end\n  end\n\n  # AC2 (story amendment 2026-07-07 / test-review M1): 1 d / 7 d / 30 d —\n  # the dummy's \"1 h\" option is dropped, day-granularity only.\n  defp retention_options, do: @retention_options\n\n  # A stable id-safe fragment for the inspected node's <label for=...>/\n  # <select id=...> pairing (AC9) — reuses the same Base64 scheme as each\n  # SVG node's own dom_id.\n  defp node_dom_key(key), do: Base.url_encode64(key, padding: false)\n\n  defp capture_expires_at_unix(%{expires_at: %DateTime{} = expires_at}) do\n    Integer.to_string(DateTime.to_unix(expires_at))\n  end\n\n  defp capture_countdown_text(capture, now_ms) do\n    now = DateTime.from_unix!(div(now_ms, 1000))\n    format_clock(TemporaryCaptureManager.remaining_seconds(capture, now))\n  end\n\n  # AC10: name + route + status + counts, e.g.\n  # \"users endpoint · /admin/users · errors · 1 error · 0 warnings · 1 request in the last 60 seconds\"\n  #\n  # AC4/AC8/AC9: an \"off\" display status names the state in text (\"logging\n  # off\") rather than the generic status word (status is never color-alone);\n  # \"temp\" names the capture state + remaining time.\n  # UX gate (2026-07-08, V3 fix): the off+error composite state (AC4\n  # errors-always-persist amendment) previously fell through to the\n  # generic `_` branch below, losing the \"logging off\" information\n  # entirely — the dashed-vs-solid stroke became the ONLY signal\n  # distinguishing an off-tier node with a windowed error from an\n  # ordinary always-on error node, violating status-never-color/shape-\n  # alone. `off_tier` is now threaded through so this composite state\n  # gets its own branch, naming BOTH facts in text.\n  defp node_aria_label(kind, label, route, display_status, raw_status, counts, capture, now_ms, off_tier) do\n    name =\n      case kind do\n        \"nebu\" -> \"nebu\"\n        \"domain\" -> label <> \" domain\"\n        _ -> label <> \" endpoint\"\n      end\n\n    base = Enum.join([name, route], \" · \")\n\n    cond do\n      display_status == \"off\" ->\n        base <> \" · logging off\"\n\n      display_status == \"temp\" ->\n        base <> \" · temporary capture · \" <> capture_remaining_phrase(capture, now_ms)\n\n      display_status == \"error\" and off_tier ->\n        base <>\n          \" · logging off · errors detected · \" <>\n          pluralize(counts.errors, \"error\") <> \" in the last 60 seconds\"\n\n      true ->\n        Enum.join(\n          [\n            base,\n            status_word(raw_status),\n            pluralize(counts.errors, \"error\"),\n            pluralize(counts.warnings, \"warning\"),\n            pluralize(counts.requests, \"request\") <> \" in the last 60 seconds\"\n          ],\n          \" · \"\n        )\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp capture_remaining_phrase(nil, _now_ms), do: \"5 minutes remaining\"\n\n  defp capture_remaining_phrase(capture, now_ms) do\n    now = DateTime.from_unix!(div(now_ms, 1000))\n    remaining = TemporaryCaptureManager.remaining_seconds(capture, now)\n    minutes = div(remaining, 60)\n    seconds = rem(remaining, 60)\n    pluralize(minutes, \"minute\") <> \" \" <> pluralize(seconds, \"second\") <> \" remaining\"","numLines":260,"startLine":1400,"totalLines":1758}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"599c8776-9494-4684-9530-ba04a998723d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"7a6212ab-94f2-4854-b49f-a8f4a1ef7cdb","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"7f0c215b-3d46-41aa-829c-b3f1d75c1682","agentId":"a11938c512cfadfa8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NRd76mt2yRfdkiMig18ubT","type":"tool_result","content":"335\t    test \"an error broadcast flips the off node to the error state on its dashed stroke\" do\n336\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n337\t\n338\t      broadcast_entry(\n339\t        route_template: @off_health,\n340\t        level: \"error\",\n341\t        message: \"distinctive-off-error-line\"\n342\t      )\n343\t\n344\t      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n345\t      node = node_locator(doc, @off_health, \"satellite\")\n346\t\n347\t      assert attr(node, \"data-node-status\") == \"error\",\n348\t             \"amendment: an off node with a windowed error must render the ERROR state\"\n349\t\n350\t      assert attr(node, \"data-node-off\") == \"true\",\n351\t             \"amendment: the node stays visually off-tier (dashed stroke) — \" <>\n352\t               \"data-node-off carries the off marker while status carries the error\"\n353\t\n354\t      badge = find(node, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-error\"]))\n355\t      assert badge, \"amendment: the ✕ badge must appear on the off node\"\n356\t      assert Floki.text(badge) =~ \"1\"\n357\t\n358\t      assert attr(node, \"aria-label\") =~ ~r/1 error/,\n359\t             \"the error must be announced in the node's aria-label, never color-alone\"\n360\t    end\n361\t\n362\t    test \"the off endpoint's error line reaches the stream and aggregates to domain and nebu\" do\n363\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n364\t\n365\t      broadcast_entry(\n366\t        route_template: @off_health,\n367\t        level: \"error\",\n368\t        message: \"distinctive-off-error-line\"\n369\t      )\n370\t\n371\t      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n372\t\n373\t      # Ties this test to the OFF-TIER contract (without this it would pass\n374\t      # vacuously against the shipped code, which has no off concept at all\n375\t      # and streams /health errors like any node): the satellite must\n376\t      # actually be off-tier-marked while its error streams/aggregates.\n377\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, @off_health, \"satellite\"), \"data-node-off\") == \"true\",\n378\t             \"amendment precondition: the node is off-tier (dashed marker) — \" <>\n379\t               \"the whole point is that the error escapes the OFF suppression\"\n380\t\n381\t      texts =\n382\t        doc\n383\t        |> find_all(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-stream-line\"]))\n384\t        |> Enum.map(&Floki.text/1)\n385\t\n386\t      assert Enum.any?(texts, &(&1 =~ \"distinctive-off-error-line\")),\n387\t             \"amendment: ERROR entries for off endpoints are collected and streamed\"\n388\t\n389\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, @off_health, \"domain\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"error\",\n390\t             \"amendment: the error propagates to the (otherwise off) domain\"\n391\t\n392\t      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"nebu\", \"nebu\"), \"aria-label\") =~ \"1 error\",\n393\t             \"amendment: the error aggregates into nebu's window\"\n394\t    end\n395\t  end\n396\t\n397\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n398\t  # AC1/AC2/AC9: inspector logging controls markup\n399\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════"}]},"uuid":"a5bc3ebc-1185-408e-949c-8824ce1f2c64","timestamp":"2026-07-08T08:58:03.774Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_node_controls_test.exs","content":"    test \"an error broadcast flips the off node to the error state on its dashed stroke\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n\n      broadcast_entry(\n        route_template: @off_health,\n        level: \"error\",\n        message: \"distinctive-off-error-line\"\n      )\n\n      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n      node = node_locator(doc, @off_health, \"satellite\")\n\n      assert attr(node, \"data-node-status\") == \"error\",\n             \"amendment: an off node with a windowed error must render the ERROR state\"\n\n      assert attr(node, \"data-node-off\") == \"true\",\n             \"amendment: the node stays visually off-tier (dashed stroke) — \" <>\n               \"data-node-off carries the off marker while status carries the error\"\n\n      badge = find(node, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-error\"]))\n      assert badge, \"amendment: the ✕ badge must appear on the off node\"\n      assert Floki.text(badge) =~ \"1\"\n\n      assert attr(node, \"aria-label\") =~ ~r/1 error/,\n             \"the error must be announced in the node's aria-label, never color-alone\"\n    end\n\n    test \"the off endpoint's error line reaches the stream and aggregates to domain and nebu\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n\n      broadcast_entry(\n        route_template: @off_health,\n        level: \"error\",\n        message: \"distinctive-off-error-line\"\n      )\n\n      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n\n      # Ties this test to the OFF-TIER contract (without this it would pass\n      # vacuously against the shipped code, which has no off concept at all\n      # and streams /health errors like any node): the satellite must\n      # actually be off-tier-marked while its error streams/aggregates.\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, @off_health, \"satellite\"), \"data-node-off\") == \"true\",\n             \"amendment precondition: the node is off-tier (dashed marker) — \" <>\n               \"the whole point is that the error escapes the OFF suppression\"\n\n      texts =\n        doc\n        |> find_all(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-stream-line\"]))\n        |> Enum.map(&Floki.text/1)\n\n      assert Enum.any?(texts, &(&1 =~ \"distinctive-off-error-line\")),\n             \"amendment: ERROR entries for off endpoints are collected and streamed\"\n\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, @off_health, \"domain\"), \"data-node-status\") == \"error\",\n             \"amendment: the error propagates to the (otherwise off) domain\"\n\n      assert attr(node_locator(doc, \"nebu\", \"nebu\"), \"aria-label\") =~ \"1 error\",\n             \"amendment: the error aggregates into nebu's window\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC1/AC2/AC9: inspector logging controls markup\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════","numLines":65,"startLine":335,"totalLines":1007}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7a6212ab-94f2-4854-b49f-a8f4a1ef7cdb","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"b659e671-0843-4466-8378-d67fef763ff7","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a11938c512cfadfa8","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CcpKBVdxF4ER8tdniiDSZ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Jn3eQdoVwo7w4ytCHZGsYh","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > \"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts/.claude/agent-memory/nebu-code-reviewer/feedback_documented_periodic_task_never_wired.md\" << 'EOF'\n---\nname: documented-periodic-task-never-wired\ndescription: A cleanup/sweeper function is defined and unit-tested but never wired into any production caller, while moduledocs assert a caller that doesn't actually call it\nmetadata:\n  type: feedback\n---\n\nA public `cleanup_expired/0`-style hygiene function was defined, unit-tested in\nisolation, and documented as \"Called periodically by <SomeGenServer>\" — but that\nGenServer's refresh path only calls the *read* function (`active_captures/0`),\nnever the cleanup. The only caller in the whole repo was its own test. Two moduledocs\n(the manager's AND the cache's) both asserted the periodic-cleanup wiring that does\nnot exist.\n\n**Why:** Story Dev Notes explicitly specified a periodic cleanup task; the dev built\nthe function + tests but forgot the wiring, and wrote moduledocs describing the\n*intended* design as if it were real. Impact was negligible here only by luck (PK =\nendpoint capped the table at ~3 rows), so nothing broke — masking the gap. Bonus trap:\nhad cleanup been wired into the cache GenServer's own refresh as the docs imply, it\nwould self-deadlock (cleanup -> notify_cache -> refresh_now -> GenServer.call(self)).\n\n**How to apply:** When a story specifies a periodic/background task, grep for an\nactual caller (Sweeper, application.ex child, GenServer handle_info) — not just the\nfunction definition + its unit test. A `grep -rn '\\.the_function' lib/` that returns\nonly the module itself + test files = unwired. Also cross-check that moduledoc \"called\nby X\" claims match reality: open X and confirm it calls it. {"parentUuid":"7e364cf0-7337-4e08-88af-576f5e70b66c","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a11938c512cfadfa8","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CcpKFFHcAjm6yhuvXCPUy","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Code review complete for `trace-map-node-controls` (cycle 0).\n\n## 0 MAJOR, 2 MINOR, 5 INFO\n\nThe backend/data/security-adjacent surface is genuinely solid. Expiry logic, cascade, `should_persist?/2` precedence, the RLS extension, cluster-safety, and audit-once semantics are all correct and comprehensively unit-tested. No MAJOR/CRITICAL issues — nothing requires a user decision.\n\n### Verified correct (the high-risk items called out for extra attention)\n- **Expiry boundary consistency**: `expired?/2` uses `now >= expires_at` (`compare != :lt`); DB `active?`/`active_captures` use strict `expires_at > now()`; `remaining_seconds/2` clamps at 0. At the exact boundary the DB says inactive and `expired?` says expired — consistent, no off-by-one. Boundary tests exist (contract_test 155-172).\n- **CaptureCache staleness/cluster**: `enable/disable/cleanup` call `refresh_now/0` (synchronous `GenServer.call`) on the local node; other nodes converge within the 2s poll. Expiry is a DB-time comparison (`expires_at > now()`), so all BEAM nodes agree within 2s. No node-local process owns correctness. Matches the war-room precedent. Cluster-safe.\n- **`should_persist?/2` precedence** (`server/lib/nebu/web/log_store.ex:160`): `error → always persist` first, then off-list `→ capture-active AND level-policy`, else level-policy. Matches the errors-always-persist amendment exactly (off silences DEBUG/INFO/WARN, never ERROR). Errors still reach node status because `Handler.log/2` always broadcasts regardless of persistence.\n- **RLS migration** (`000020...up.sql`): dynamic `log_min_level_%` / `log_retention_days_%` LIKE families are backslash-escaped (PG default LIKE escape, no ESCAPE clause needed), don't over-match the enumerated keys, and the first-INSERT path is covered by the pre-existing `config_insert_only ... WITH CHECK (true)` (verified in `000001`). No injection/bypass — key values are data in an RLS predicate, not SQL.\n- **Audit fires once**: `enable/2` always writes one row; `disable/2` writes only when `DELETE ... RETURNING` returned a row (atomic — a concurrent double-disable can't double-write). ADR-018/020 respected (inline SQL, Admin→Compliance.AuditWriter public API, no domain-model layer). AuditWriter allowlist extended correctly.\n- **Cycle-0 / UX-gate regressions**: the `BadBooleanError` guard (`if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo)`, not `and`), the KIND-prefixed `dom_id` for the `/health` domain+satellite collision, and the one-click/Known-Challenge-2/auto-revert flows all have dedicated tests.\n\n### MINOR (hand back to dev agent)\n\n1. **`TemporaryCaptureManager.cleanup_expired/0` is never wired into production.** Its own moduledoc (line 219-221) and `CaptureCache`'s moduledoc (line 51) both claim it is \"Called periodically by `Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache`\", but `CaptureCache.refresh` only calls `active_captures/0` — `cleanup_expired` is invoked only from its own unit test (verified repo-wide). The story Dev Notes explicitly specified a periodic cleanup task; it's absent. Correctness impact is negligible (expiry is a comparison, and `endpoint` being PRIMARY KEY caps the table at ~3 rows), but the two moduledocs are factually wrong. Note the latent trap: if someone \"fixes\" this by wiring `cleanup_expired` into `CaptureCache`'s own refresh as the docs imply, it self-deadlocks (`cleanup_expired → notify_capture_cache → refresh_now → GenServer.call(self)`). Fix: wire cleanup into a non-self caller (e.g. `Sweeper`) or correct both moduledocs to say cleanup is a manual/unscheduled hygiene helper.\n\n2. **UX-gate V3 fix (off+error composite aria-label) has no pinning regression test.** `node_aria_label/9` (trace_map.ex:1633) produces `\"... · logging off · errors detected · N error ...\"` for an off-tier node with a windowed error. The test at `trace_map_node_controls_test.exs:335` only asserts the aria-label matches `/1 error/` — which the pre-V3 generic branch (`\"... · errors · 1 error ...\"`) *also* satisfies. A regression dropping the `off_tier` composite branch would still pass green. Only test 239 pins \"logging off\", and only for the plain (non-error) off state. Add an assertion on the `logging off` / `errors detected` substrings for the off+error node. (Recurring \"fix lacks direct regression test\" pattern.)\n\n### INFO (no action required; some flagged for Kassandra, who runs next)\n\n1. `CaptureCache.refresh` calls `:persistent_term.put/2` unconditionally every 2s even when the active-capture set is unchanged (almost always the empty set) — each put triggers a global GC scan. This exactly matches the shipped `PolicyCache` precedent (verified), so it's consistent, not a regression; both would benefit from a \"put only when changed\" guard.\n2. `handle_event(\"configure_logging\", ...)` does no validation of `min_level`/`retention_days`/`node_key` before building dynamic config keys — an admin could persist arbitrary `log_min_level_<junk>` rows (harmless unused keys, admin-only, RLS-allowed by the LIKE family). For Kassandra completeness; low risk.\n3. Admin authz on the new `enable_capture`/`disable_capture`/`configure_logging` handlers relies entirely on the inherited `live_session :admin` on_mount gate (prior-shipped `trace-map-leitstand` infra) — no per-event re-check, which is the codebase convention. Flag for Kassandra to confirm the gate covers this LiveView's socket.\n4. Auto-expiry writes no audit entry (only explicit enable/disable do). Consistent with AC12 Option B (admin actions) and the moduledoc rationale; noted since AC12's text names \"enable/disable\" and expiry is neither.\n5. `e2e/fixtures/db.ts` + steps add capture-seeding SQL (test-only) — flag for Kassandra per review dimension 5 (dynamic SQL in fixtures).\n\nRelevant files:\n- `/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/temporary_capture_manager.ex` (MINOR 1 — moduledoc lines 45-49, 219-221)\n- `/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/web/log_store/capture_cache.ex` (MINOR 1 — moduledoc line 51;
